### 9 subject events

- **1914.06** `statistical_infection`
  Klara在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang病倒了，痢疾让身体发热、虚弱，接下来的日子只能卧床。

- **1914.07** `statistical_recovery`
  Klara从痢疾中慢慢恢复，能下床喝水，但身体还虚弱。

- **1914.12** `iw18-infancy.ceiling-shadows::ctx-vienna-1912::milieu-tenement/the-coat-is-just-a-coat` tags: infancy, night, wonder, gaslight, fear, family, vienna, urban
  Josef Bauer把Klara抱起来，走到衣钩下面，让孩子伸手去抓那件旧大衣。粗呢的袖口擦过掌心，Klara先愣住，随后皱着鼻子哭了一声。Josef Bauer笑着把大衣掀开，说“看，只有衣服”，灯影也跟着晃了一下。
  _effects: mood 0.03; flag +learned_the_coat_rack_

- **1915.10** `vienna-early-childhood.stairwell-fall/landlord-complaint` tags: childhood, family, home, street
  摔伤之后两天，房东女人在门厅拦住Josef Bauer，楼里的Katharina Bauer正提着菜篮站在门边。房东女人说楼梯间不许孩子嬉闹，铁扶手磕坏要赔。Josef Bauer把裹着布条的Klara往身后一拉，回了句“灯都没有，谁看得见台阶”。房东女人把话记在房租本边上，说下回修灯的钱从房租里扣。
  _effects: mood -0.07; flag +stairs_accident_noted_

- **1917.10** `statistical_infection`
  Klara在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang病倒了，肺炎让身体发热、虚弱，接下来的日子只能卧床。

- **1917.12** `statistical_illness_worsened`
  Klara的肺炎没有完全好转，咳嗽和发热又拖了一个月，家里人轮流守着。

- **1918.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.market-line/line-half-flour` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, market, food
  队伍从面包房门口拐进巷子，Klara把布袋夹在腋下，学着前面人的样子，用胳膊肘顶住后面人的篮子。窗口的伙计舀面粉时，Klara踮脚看着秤，直到半袋面倒进袋口。挤出人群时鞋跟被踩掉，Klara单脚跳了两步，在泥里摸回鞋，索性赤脚走回家。
  _effects: food; health -0.020000000000000018 (赤脚走石板路磨伤脚底); skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-2_

- **1918.10** `vienna-childhood-street.hoop/hoop-mother-call` tags: childhood, street, social, home
  窗户上的玻璃响了一下，有人探出头，让Klara立刻回家。铁环还在Mitzi Lechner脚边，Klara蹲下去抽，抽不动。Mitzi Lechner说除非明天把铁钩带来。Klara点头，Mitzi Lechner把脚一抬，铁环滚到Klara手边，上面沾了一层鞋底灰。
  _effects: relation friend with person-mitzi-lechner; relation friend with person-mitzi-lechner; mood -0.02_

- **1918.10** `statistical_death/pneumonia` tags: death
  Klara在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang发起高烧，咳嗽越来越深。邻居帮忙去请医生，医生到时只留下药粉和一张账单；几天后呼吸变得很浅。Anna Bauer在场。
  _effects: death (肺炎)_

### 179 subject events

- **1914.02** `vienna-infant-household.laundry-day/sibling-watches` tags: infancy, health, family, home
  Alois Lechner在厨房的木盆里搓亚麻布，热水汽模糊了窗。Mitzi Lechner坐在摇床边的矮凳上晃拨浪鼓，Mitzi伸手去抓。等Alois Lechner晾完最后一块尿布，Mitzi已经在Mitzi Lechner的看护下睡熟，摇床没被蹬歪。
  _effects: relation kin with person-phase15-Mitzi-world-vienna-1912; mood 0.03_

- **1918.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.laundry-help/laundry-bread-with-friend` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, laundry, work
  Franz Lechner家的孩子站在绳子另一头，Mitzi递夹子时故意慢半拍，两个人把床单拉得没有一道褶。收工后Franz Lechner端出半包隔夜面包，掰开时掉了一桌屑。Mitzi把分到的那块用围裙兜住，回家路上闻着麦味，硬是没咬。
  _effects: food; relation acquaintance with person-franz-lechner; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-23_

- **1919.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.market-line/line-half-flour` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, market, food
  面粉袋压在胸口，越抱越沉。Mitzi前面的大婶一直往后靠，Mitzi就把膝盖顶住对方的篮子，硬留出半步。买到半袋面粉后，Mitzi的鞋跟留在人堆里，找回来时鞋面全是泥。回家路上布袋换了两次肩，赤脚踩过石板时脚底发麻。
  _effects: food; health -0.020000000000000004 (赤脚走石板路磨伤脚底); skill_

- **1919.11** `p9-childhood-household.fever-night/hospital-deposit` tags: childhood, family, household, home, siblings, illness, neighbors
  医院走廊里全是湿鞋印。Alois Lechner把Mitzi放在长椅上，自己去窗口交钱，六克朗换来一张薄薄的收据。护士把Mitzi领进观察室，白灯照得人睁不开眼。Mitzi抓着Alois Lechner的袖口不放，直到药水推进胳膊才松手。
  _effects: cash -6 (医院急诊押金和电车票); health 0.08 (在医院接受处理); mood -0.04_

- **1920.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.laundry-help/laundry-three-heller` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, laundry, work
  “拽平，别留褶。”Friedrich Huber在绳子那头喊。Mitzi踮着脚把床单拉直，碱水把指缝泡得发白。收工时天已经黑了，Friedrich Huber数了三赫勒，又在围裙上擦干手才递过来。Mitzi把钱塞进袜筒，回家路上一直攥着，虎口的裂口沾了汗，火辣辣地疼。
  _effects: cash 0.03 (帮邻居晾床单挣的三赫勒); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-5; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-5; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-13_

  Mitzi在洗衣房的长凳上睡着，醒来时喉咙像塞了棉花。Alois Lechner把最后的干净布巾浸了凉水，敷在Mitzi额头上，整夜换了七八回。第四天Mitzi能坐起来喝粥，下嘴唇脱下一小片皮，Alois Lechner用指尖替Mitzi拈掉。

  _effects: flag +school_transfer_approved_

  Mitzi在阁楼练琴，楼下邻居上来敲门，说孩子要睡觉。Mitzi把弱音器夹上，声音闷得像蚊子。练到半夜，Mitzi索性放下琴，把谱子默写了一遍，第二天在课上凭记忆拉完，Franz Lechner问Mitzi是不是没睡好。第二天Mitzi把弱音器取下来，换到更远的阁楼。
  _effects: mood -0.04000000000000001; skill_

- **1928.01** `yf18-firstlook.parish-dance::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/friend-cuts-in` tags: first_love, dance, church, courtship, jealousy, reform, medical, schooling
  Josef在第二支曲子前把Rosa请走，说Mitzi答应过要替她看管外套。Mitzi抱着一件不属于自己的大衣站在圣像下面，看他们在蜡烛光里转圈；回家路上，Mitzi把舞卡撕成小片，扔进多瑙运河。

  Mitzi在教堂门口或学校走廊里又遇见Rosa。这一次对方先点了头，Mitzi准备好的整段话全忘了，只把手里的书换到另一只手上；回家后Mitzi才发现，自己把书拿倒了。
  _effects: state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.intensity 70 -> 75; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.nerve 15 -> 23; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.reciprocated true -> true; relation friend with person-phase15-Rosa-world-vienna-1912; mood 0.030000000000000027_

- **1928.01** `p9-childhood-apprenticeship.leave-bad-workshop/walked-off-unpaid` tags: adolescence, work, apprenticeship, labour, training, family, task, task:work
  Mitzi以为走就走了，工钱总会结。等到月底，家里人把空钱袋翻过来，Mitzi才回到作坊。Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne正在给新学徒示范上墨，头也没抬，说人没打招呼就走，只能给几个铜币。Mitzi站在门口，听见里面的机器又转起来，铜币在掌心硌得生疼。
  _effects: cash -1.5 (没拿到最后一个月的整月工钱); mood -0.08000000000000002; unemployment (不告而别离开作坊); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-51_

- **1928.07** `yf18-runaway.companion::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/stay-and-spend-the-fare` tags: runaway, friendship, companion, plan, betrayal, reform, medical, schooling
  Mitzi把票退了，和Elisabeth Bauer在普拉特门口的摊子上吃了两碗热汤。汤里有太多盐，Mitzi喝到一半就放下勺子；Elisabeth Bauer说等明年攒够钱再一起走，Mitzi点头，没有说自己已经把包袱收进了柜子最下层。
  _effects: state_remove yf18-runaway-plan state:world-vienna-1912:86; cash 2 (the refunded fare); flag +yf18_stayed_for_friend; mood -0.010000000000000009; flag -yf18_runaway_plan_active_

  Mitzi把那句轻视记了很久。它没有变成一个决定，但也没有消失。
  _effects: mood -0.03999999999999998; flag +wish:knowledge:wounded_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:002914_

- **1929.01** `yf18-friend.two-tickets::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/confront-at-the-cloakroom` tags: first_love, friendship, betrayal, dance, tickets, reform, medical, schooling
  Mitzi在衣帽间堵住Rosa，问那两张票怎么到了Elisabeth Bauer手里。Rosa说Mitzi从来没敢开口，自己只是替Mitzi把话说了；Mitzi把票根揉成一团，塞进Rosa的西装口袋，转身时撞翻了侍者的托盘。

  Mitzi只把Rosa的名字写在一张草稿纸背面，第二天那张纸就在教室后排传了一圈。有人把名字念出来，有人问Mitzi是不是要请人喝咖啡；Mitzi说那是表姐的名字，声音大得连自己都吓了一跳。
  _effects: state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.talk 22 -> 34; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.nerve 18 -> 12; reputation -0.03; mood -0.02999999999999997_

  _effects: skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-34; flag +typesetting_mentor; mood 0.06; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-34_

- **1929.06** `yf18-politics.latin-pamphlet::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/pass-it-on` tags: politics, school, pamphlet, censorship, awakening, reform, medical, schooling
  Franz Lechner把一本拉丁文课本推到Mitzi面前，说第三十四页的译文错了。Mitzi翻到那一页，夹在里面的是一张油印纸，写着面粉配给和工厂检查的数字。放学后，Mitzi把纸抄了一遍，塞进Rosa的历史作业本；那天晚上，Mitzi第一次觉得自己住的街区有一张看不见的地图。
  _effects: knowledge “配给数字与官方公告并不一致”; flag +yf18_read_pamphlet; relation acquaintance with person-franz-lechner; mood 0.030000000000000027_

  Maria Kral 2先说试三个月，不给工钱，只管一顿午饭。家里同意了，因为多一张嘴比多一双手更难。Mitzi每天最早到，最晚走，三个月后师傅才把契约拿出来。
  _effects: food; mood -0.03999999999999998; state_add apprenticeship-contract state:world-vienna-1912:130; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-36; education apprenticeship; role_

- **1929.11** `r3-teen.first-love::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/reciprocated` tags: love, courtship, school, tram, secrecy, reform, medical, schooling
  Elisabeth Bauer把回信写在电车月票背面：星期三，六点，第二站。Mitzi提前半小时到，站在报亭旁边，把那张月票看了十几遍。Elisabeth Bauer从电车上下来时，围巾被风吹到脸上，两个人都笑了。
  _effects: mood 0.12; flag +first_love_reciprocated; relation partner with person-elisabeth-bauer_

  市场掌柜把账本翻到空白页，铅笔写上“月底清”，Mitzi 把拇指按进印泥，红印盖得有点歪。Josef Weiss 扛着半袋面粉往回走，袋底漏出的粉在石板路上留下白印。
  _effects: cash -5 (以自家名义赊购面粉); food; mood -0.04999999999999999; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-8; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-8_

- **1930.02** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.laundry_line_dispute/subject_yields_line` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Mitzi看Otto Berg盆里那几件小衬衫，把自家床单卷起来抱回屋，说等下午再晾。Otto Berg愣了一下，闷头把衣服挂好，收工回来时在Mitzi门口放了一小包用旧报纸裹的洗衣碱。Mitzi打开报纸，碱面里还掺着几粒粗盐。
  _effects: relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-37; food; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-37_

- **1930.06** `vienna-parish-aid.funeral-society/funeral-society.quit` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  Mitzi说互助会先退了，这笔钱要留给煤和面包。Hermann Kral 2没争辩，在蓝皮本上把名字划了一道，问清楚是不是全家都退，然后把划掉的那行折进本子夹缝。她说以后想再进，要重新排三个月。门关上，Mitzi听见铅笔在楼梯上轻轻敲了两下栏杆。
  _effects: cash 0.4 (省下本周会费留作煤钱); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-17; flag +funeral_society_left; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-17_

- **1930.06** `vienna-housing-move.flat-viewing/flat-viewing-too-damp` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money
  Mitzi在单间里站了一刻钟，脚底发潮，墙纸底下透出一股霉味。看门人说刷一层石灰就好，Alois Lechner却在门口轻轻咳了一声，提醒这背面没有阳光。Mitzi把定金收回口袋，谢过看门人，宁可多付三克朗，也不愿把湿气带进夜里。
  _effects: mood -0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person-alois-lechner; flag +still_looking_for_room_

- **1931.01** `oj18-perfume.apprentice::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/sold-the-wrong-bottle` tags: perfume, apprentice, smell, shop, craft, work, odd_job, reform
  Mitzi把一瓶玫瑰水当成古龙水卖给Josef Bauer，收了两克朗。第二天，Josef Bauer带着发红的脖颈回来，Freie Gewerkschaft der graphischen Arbeiter Wiens当着人把瓶子摔进垃圾桶，赔了一瓶新的。Mitzi被罚擦三天柜台，鼻尖一直留着玫瑰和酒精混在一起的甜味。
  _effects: state_add oj18-odd-apprenticeship state:world-vienna-1912:186; cash -1 (paid for the returned bottle); reputation -0.04; relation acquaintance with person-josef-bauer; flag +perfume_apprentice; knowledge “玫瑰水不能当古龙水卖，{customer}的脖颈第二天就红了。”; role; flag +odd_job_apprentice_

- **1931.01** `state_tick:apprenticeship-contract/workshop-year/learned` tags: apprenticeship-contract, education, craft, apprentice, work, state, state_tick

  _effects: state_parameter apprenticeship-contract.progress 8 -> 28; skill; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1931.01** `r13-fam.repair-friendship::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/cup-refused` tags: task, social, friendship, repair, trust, reform, medical, schooling
  Rosa Berg 2把杯子推回来，说摔坏的东西粘得再好也有缝。Mitzi在门口站了一会儿，把杯子放进外套口袋，杯沿磕在扣子上，响了一声。回家的路上，Mitzi绕开了两人常走的那条街。
  _effects: mood -0.12; flag +friendship_unrepaired; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-60; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-60_

- **1931.09** `vienna-legacy-worklife.sick-fund/sick-fund.advance-from-fund` tags: legacy, work, family, reform, ordinary_life
  药房在街口，柜台玻璃下面压着价目纸。Mitzi把两克朗推过去，伙计找回来十二赫勒，药片用报纸包成一个小包。工友接过去时手还在抖，报纸边角很快被汗洇软。
  _effects: cash -2 (从互助金垫付药费); health 0.06000000000000005 (及时买到药); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-20; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-71; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-20_

- **1931.09** `vienna-parish-aid.rent-box/rent-box.refuse` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  Mitzi说这个月还能撑，房租差的那几克朗打算替人洗衣补上，请Gustav Winter把篮子提回去给更困难的人家。Gustav Winter在门口停了一会儿，把一小块肥皂留在窗台上，说不是救济，是教区多出来的。门关上后，屋里只剩煤箱底刮出的响声。
  _effects: mood -0.02999999999999997; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-12; flag +parish_refused_aid; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-12_

- **1931.12** `vienna-medical-treatment.toothache/drops-and-endure` tags: health, medical, family, money
  夜里疼醒时，Mitzi摸黑往棉球上又滴药水，手抖得洒在枕头上。Josef Weiss第二天一早来敲门，看见肿脸就说别硬撑了，可Mitzi只是摆摆手，说再熬一天也许就好了。
  _effects: cash -0.6 (牙痛药水); condition_

- **1932.01** `oj18-perfume.recipe::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/recipe-stolen` tags: perfume, shop, recipe, theft, customer, work, odd_job, reform
  对街的店员Berta Hofer抄走了Mitzi的配方：前调多放一滴橙花，后调少放半滴麝香，成本就能降一成。Mitzi在市场上看见同样的瓶子，标签换了名字；店主没有去告，只在门口挂了一块小牌：本店不卖便宜的味道。
  _effects: cash -2 (lost sales to the copied scent); reputation -0.03; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-30; flag +recipe_stolen; knowledge “对街的瓶子用了我的配方，前调多一滴橙花，后调少半滴麝香。”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-63; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-30_

  一年过去，Mitzi已经能闭着眼说出哪块地板会响、哪盏煤气灯该在几点关。师傅把备用钥匙交下来，没有解释为什么第三把钥匙磨得最亮；Mitzi也没有问，只在巡查簿最后一页画了一个小小的记号。
  _effects: state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.trust 15 -> 20; state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.strangeKnowledge 12 -> 17; state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.nightsWorked 0 -> 12; cash 1 (night-shift wages); skill_

  Mitzi暂时把那件事收进抽屉；日子还有别的东西要应付。

- **1932.01** `r13-civ.public-health-campaign::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/posters-pasted` tags: health, public_health, campaign, poster, institution, reform, medical, schooling
  Magistrat der k. k. Reichshaupt- und Residenzstadt Wien的办事员把一叠防疫告示交给Mitzi，说浆糊要刷匀，别让风揭了角。Mitzi沿着市场街贴到天黑，手指被纸边割出细口。第二天，告示下面多了一行铅笔字：“水要煮开”。
  _effects: reputation 0.06; flag +pasted_public_health_notices; role; skill; knowledge “the notice was answered with a handwritten instruction to bo”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-22; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-67_

- **1932.05** `p9-gap-adult-unemployed.factory-gate/gate-name-taken` tags: adulthood, unemployment, casual_labour, poverty, daily, factory, hiring
  玻璃后面的招工纸被门房用尺子压平，Mitzi的名字写在横线本上，墨迹歪着。旁边的Anna Moser 2凑过来看了一眼，说这厂子去年也招过两回，录用的多半是厂里老人的亲戚。
  _effects: flag +factory_callback; mood 0.030000000000000027; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-55_

- **1932.07** `vienna-legacy-worklife.apprentice-wage/apprentice-wage.hand-to-family` tags: legacy, work, family, reform, ordinary_life
  工资袋上的油墨还没干透，Mitzi的手指染了一点黑。母亲把硬币倒在厨房桌上，一枚枚排开，排到第六枚时停住。灶上的水壶响了，她拿起来又放下，没继续问。
  _effects: cash 3 (按新规定多出的学徒工资交给家里); food; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-14_

- **1932.11** `vienna-courtship-courtyard.breakup/courtyard-quarrel` tags: adulthood, courtship, partner, social, family, separation
  话没说完就变成了争。Elisabeth Bauer把外套扔在晾衣杆下，Mitzi说了一句不中听的，声音大得整院都听见。Anna Weiss 2在三楼把窗开了一条缝，看完了全程，第二天院子里人人都知道；这一架把两个人的关系吵断了，也把Mitzi的名字在院子里吵薄了一层。
  _effects: relation_end partner with person-elisabeth-bauer; reputation -0.07; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-39; mood -0.09999999999999998; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-39_

- **1933.01** `vienna-courtship-courtyard.letter-answer/mother-opened-letter` tags: adulthood, courtship, partner, social, family
  回信没有到Mitzi手里。Anton的母亲先拆了它，连信封上的邮戳一起摊在洗衣房的桌上，说有人写字来缠自家孩子。话传到院子里时已经添了油，Hedwig隔着栏杆把原话学了一遍，说得很难听；Anton再见到Mitzi时，眼睛先往母亲那边看。Mitzi站在水桶边，知道这封信已经被拆成了两家的脸面。
  _effects: reputation -0.06; mood -0.07999999999999996; relation acquaintance with person-phase15-Anton-world-vienna-1912_

  Mitzi天没亮就把Luise Winter裹好，抱到楼下Maria Kral 2家。Maria Kral 2接过孩子，先摸了摸尿布，又指给Mitzi看灶上温着的米汤。Mitzi把一星期的工钱数出一份放在柜上，Maria Kral 2数也没数就收进围裙；出门时，Mitzi听见孩子在屋里哭了一声，又停了。
  _effects: cash -2 (上工前把小的送下楼); condition; flag +arranged_daily_childcare; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-36; knowledge “the minder keeps a pot of rice water on the stove”_

- **1933.07** `p9-social-tenement-neighbors.gramophone-wall/gramophone-wall.knock-and-bargain` tags: housing, neighbors, tenement, daily, money, reputation
  Mitzi上楼敲了Josef Berg 2的门，说唱针刮在墙上，夜班的人睡不安稳。Josef Berg 2把留声机从墙边挪到窗下，垫了一块毛毡，答应十点以后不再放。下楼时进行曲还在响，但闷了些，Mitzi能听见自家水壶的响声了。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-63; health 0.03 (夜里终于能早些入睡)_

- **1933.09** `vienna-legacy-worklife.union-office/union-office.post-ledger` tags: legacy, work, family, reform, ordinary_life

  _effects: cash -3 (牙医拔牙费用); health 0.05999999999999999 (拔除病牙解除疼痛); mood -0.030000000000000027_

- **1939.05** `df18-mark.first-appearance::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/burn-bird` tags: body, mark, omen, illness, rumor, supernatural, eerie, vienna
  印刷机的滚筒咬住Mitzi的袖口时，谁都以为只擦破了一层皮。伤口结痂后，却留下一只张开翅膀的鸟形烫痕。Emil Berg 2给了Mitzi两枚银币，说别去报工伤，厂里正查安全。Mitzi把钱收下，回到排字房，觉得那只鸟在袖子里发烫。
  _effects: cash 2 (工头给的封口费); state_add df18-uncanny-mark state:world-vienna-1912:420; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-93; flag +df18_mark_appeared_after_injury; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-99; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-93; flag +df18_mark_noticed; knowledge “我身上有一处说不清的痕迹，别人对它各有各的说法。”_

- **1939.09** `vienna-courtship-courtyard.letter-answer/mother-opened-letter` tags: adulthood, courtship, partner, social, family
  回信没有到Mitzi手里。Rosa Berg的母亲先拆了它，连信封上的邮戳一起摊在洗衣房的桌上，说有人写字来缠自家孩子。话传到院子里时已经添了油，Gustav Winter隔着栏杆把原话学了一遍，说得很难听；Rosa Berg再见到Mitzi时，眼睛先往母亲那边看。Mitzi站在水桶边，知道这封信已经被拆成了两家的脸面。
  _effects: reputation -0.06; mood -0.010000000000000009; relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000953:child_

- **1940.01** `state_tick:oj18-odd-apprenticeship/strange-year/trusted-with-the-keys` tags: oj18-odd-apprenticeship, work, odd_job, apprenticeship, night, trust, secrecy, state
  一年过去，Mitzi已经能闭着眼说出哪块地板会响、哪盏煤气灯该在几点关。师傅把备用钥匙交下来，没有解释为什么第三把钥匙磨得最亮；Mitzi也没有问，只在巡查簿最后一页画了一个小小的记号。
  _effects: state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.trust 27 -> 32; state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.strangeKnowledge 15 -> 20; state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.nightsWorked 12 -> 24; cash 1 (night-shift wages); skill_

- **1940.01** `state_tick:df18-uncanny-mark/mark-year/kept-small` tags: df18-uncanny-mark, body, mark, omen, superstition, reputation, mystery, faith
  又过了一年，Mitzi学会了在洗澡时不去盯那块印子。它还在，边缘有时发红，有时像普通的旧疤；街坊有了新的闲话，Mitzi的袖子也换了更长的款式。
  _effects: state_parameter df18-uncanny-mark.attention 35 -> 29; state_parameter df18-uncanny-mark.pain 20 -> 16; mood 0.010000000000000009_

- **1940.01** `state_tick:wish-knowledge/knowledge-pressure/press-on` tags: wish-knowledge, wish, wish:knowledge, knowledge, education, curiosity, state, state_tick
  Mitzi又把那件没实现的事翻出来想了一遍；这一次没有告诉任何人。
  _effects: mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1940.01** `agency:attend_meeting` tags: agency, agency:attend_meeting
  Mitzi走到Arbeiter-Zeitung im Vorwärts-Haus门口，看见便衣和拥挤的人群，最终没有进去。
  _effects: mood -0.030000000000000027_

- **1940.03** `vienna-parish-aid.funeral-society/funeral-society.pay` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  Mitzi数出八赫勒交给Alois Berg，看她在蓝皮本上找到自家门牌，把这一周的格子划掉。Alois Berg说上个月隔壁巷子的人走了，公账出了棺材和一场小弥撒，剩下的钱记在册尾。楼梯口的灯很暗，Mitzi盯着自己的名字看了两遍才转身。
  _effects: cash -0.4 (一周丧葬互助会会费); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-1; flag +funeral_society_member; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-1_

- **1940.08** `vienna-housing-move.flat-viewing/flat-viewing-bed-only` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money, household
  顶楼那间屋里有三张床，靠墙一张空着，床板上留着上一个住客的煤灰印。Mitzi把铺盖卷放下，试了试窗上的插销，外面是通风井。Elisabeth Bauer帮着把一只小箱子搁在床头，说这地方不要指望晒到太阳。
  _effects: cash -3 (先付一张床位的月租); residence_

- **1940.11** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.shared_stove_fire/fire_scald_hand` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Mitzi在合租房走廊的公用灶台上煮土豆，转身取盐时袖子扫翻了铁锅，滚水浇在左手背上。Rosa Huber从隔壁冲出来，扯下自己的围裙裹住烫伤处，又跑下楼叫来住在二楼的接生婆。接生婆用蛋清和旧亚麻布包扎，嘱咐三天不能沾水。
  _effects: relation friend with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:001110:child_

- **1941.01** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.laundry_line_dispute/compromise_split_line` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Mitzi把洗好的床单搭上院子里的公用晾衣绳，占了整条。Rosa Huber抱着一盆湿衣服下来，说自家孩子明天要穿干净衬衫上学。两人在绳子两头扯了一阵，最后Mitzi把床单对折，腾出一半，Rosa Huber用木夹子把衬衫一件件排上去。谁也没道歉，但第二天见面都点了头。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:001110:child; mood 0.020000000000000018_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:008935_

  Mitzi帮Mitzi Huber把铁帘拉下来，只留一道缝递面包。队伍在外面敲了两下门，随后安静了。Hermann Lechner从缝里递进一张写着名字的纸，说家里有病人；Mitzi把纸压回对方手里，说从明天起早点来。
  _effects: state_parameter hs18-blackout-rumor.panic 42 -> 47; state_parameter hs18-blackout-rumor.credibility 90 -> 85; food; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-25; flag +closed_bakery_during_blackout; mood -0.010000000000000009; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-25_

  Mitzi在人群里挽了一下袖子，第二天就有人来问那块印子是不是“会动的”。Mitzi说不会，问的人却已经把自己的解释说完了。
  _effects: state_parameter df18-uncanny-mark.attention 29 -> 37; mood -0.010000000000000009; reputation -0.01_

- **1941.01** `state_tick:df18-uncanny-mark/mark-year/story-grows` tags: df18-uncanny-mark, body, mark, omen, superstition, reputation, mystery, faith
  入冬后，Mitzi在集市上听见一个陌生女人向别人描述“那个有手印的人”。故事已经比原来多了一个雨夜和一声尖叫。Mitzi低头挑菜，发现自己的手正按着袖口。
  _effects: state_parameter df18-uncanny-mark.attention 37 -> 47; state_parameter df18-uncanny-mark.pain 16 -> 22; state_parameter df18-uncanny-mark.claimed unknown -> curse; reputation -0.02; mood -0.020000000000000018_

  Helene Bauer把借据推到Mitzi面前，说只要签个名，钱就借给Mitzi Weiss。Mitzi看见Mitzi Weiss的手一直按在桌沿上，指节发白；Mitzi把笔蘸了墨，在第二个名字后面又按了手印。Mitzi Weiss没有说话，只把借据折好，收进内袋。
  _effects: condition; flag +vouched_for_friend_loan; role; relation friend with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000292:child; knowledge “the loan falls due at the end of the quarter”_

- **1941.05** `hs18-blackout.denial::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/tear-off-a-corner` tags: blackout, rumor, denial, official, neighbor, ambiguity, vienna, urban
  Mitzi趁Josef Lechner转身，把告示从电线杆上撕下一角。纸很薄，背面还印着昨天的电影广告。Helene Bauer说别撕，留着还能包东西；Mitzi把那一角揉成团，塞进口袋，决定先去地窖看看。
  _effects: state_parameter hs18-blackout-rumor.officialDenial true -> true; state_parameter hs18-blackout-rumor.credibility 85 -> 90; state_parameter hs18-blackout-rumor.panic 47 -> 55; knowledge “辟谣告示背面是电影广告。”; flag +tore_denial_notice_

  十二赫勒换来两杯淡啤酒，找零是四枚一赫勒的铜币。Mitzi把铜币收好，听师兄讲那年冬天的事，讲到一半师兄停了停，说“你别到处讲”。酒馆里有人拉手风琴，声音盖过了后半句。
  _effects: cash -1 (请师兄喝一杯啤酒); relation colleague with person-phase15-Emil-world-vienna-1912; skill_

  Mitzi没有填表，只把半块面包和一张电车票塞进孩子的口袋。Josef Lechner敲了敲桌子，叫下一个。Friedrich Winter追到门口，问姓名；Mitzi摆摆手，说名字写在票背面。其实票背面什么也没有。
  _effects: food; cash -1 (gave away a tram ticket); relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000135:child; flag +helped_refugee_without_papers; mood 0.020000000000000018_

  Mitzi把Hermann Weiss的名字写在编制表的空行上，又在旁边注了“试用三个月”。Luise Hofer提醒说预算只够半个位置，Mitzi还是把名字留下了。第二天，Hermann Weiss提前半小时到，把走廊里的旧档案按年份重新排了一遍。

  分红按购货额算，Mitzi一年买了四十七克朗的杂货，分到三克朗二十赫勒。Josef Stein 2把硬币数在柜台上，说今年比去年少，因为对面新开了平价铺。Mitzi把钱又放回柜台，换了两袋面粉和一罐糖。
  _effects: cash 3 (合作社的分红日); flag +received_cooperative_dividend; role; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-57; knowledge “the dividend is calculated from annual purchases”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-57_

- **1942.10** `vienna-courtship-courtyard.family-table/leave-before-dessert` tags: adulthood, courtship, partner, social, family, partnership
  问到后来，Alois Lechner提起别家有个更稳当的差事。Ferdinand Berg把餐巾往桌上一放，说吃好了，先走。两人在楼梯间站住，Ferdinand Berg说不是Mitzi的错，可下次再来还得一样坐这张桌子；Mitzi心里松了一点，也知道这顿饭没吃完，事情并没有过去。
  _effects: relation partner with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:001793:child; relation kin with person-alois-lechner; mood 0.030000000000000027_

- **1942.12** `vienna-parish-aid.sick-call/sick-call.with-priest` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  Mitzi觉得一个人去不合适，绕到教堂请了修女一同上楼。修女看过病人，留下药包和一小瓶圣水，说会从教区账上出钱请医生来看一趟。Elisabeth Weiss在楼下等着，说这样妥当，病人家里没男人，多个见证人心里安稳。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-83; mood 0.040000000000000036; flag +parish_sick_visitor_

- **1943.01** `vienna-family-childbirth/daughter-born` tags: family, childbirth, death, lifecycle, birth, state, state:raising-child, raising-child
  Mitzi在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang生下一个女婴，取名Katharina Berg。接生的人收六克朗，Ferdinand Berg在走廊烧水，邻居借来一只摇篮；Mitzi起身时眼前发黑，三天后腰还像断了一样，孩子哭声响亮，把屋里的冷气都顶了回去。
  _effects: birth female; cash -6 (midwife fee（其中6克朗记入债务）); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-100; state_add raising-child state:world-vienna-1912:578_

  _effects: reputation 0.020000000000000004; skill; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-72; flag +blocked_permit; knowledge “扩建申请的排水图与消防通道不符合现行规定。”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-72_

- **1944.01** `historical_phase`
  历史进程《环城大道写字楼管理处在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang经过数年的招募、会议和互助，把分散的行动整理成公开纲领；》从 emerging 进入 mobilizing。
  _effects: knowledge “我亲历或知晓「环城大道写字楼管理处在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang经”; knowledge “我亲历或知晓「环城大道写字楼管理处在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang经”_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:011849_

- **1944.03** `vienna-women-housework.wash-day/wash-day.quarrel` tags: gender, housework, home, labour, family
  水龙头滴滴答答地漏，两人都伸手去扶桶沿。Mitzi看见自家未洗的床单还堆着，胸口发闷，把桶让了半掌宽。晾绳上她的衬衣角被风掀起，正挡着邻家的窗户。
  _effects: mood -0.050000000000000044; relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:001365:child; reputation -0.02_

- **1944.03** `ml18-business.bottle-cap-patent::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/sell-the-patent-outright` tags: business, patent, money, windfall, invention, comic, vienna, urban
  破产拍卖会上，Mitzi用四克朗买下一张没人举牌的瓶盖专利，只因为图纸画得好看。三个月后，圣马克斯殡葬合作社的矿泉水厂找上门：那圈软木垫正好能解决他们的漏气问题。Luise Stein核对完专利号，圣马克斯殡葬合作社当场开出八十克朗的支票。
  _effects: cash 80 (sale of the bottle-cap patent); state_add ml18-sudden-fortune state:world-vienna-1912:611; reputation 0.06; flag +sold_patent; mood 0.09999999999999998; flag +sudden_fortune; reputation 0.02; mood 0.030000000000000027_

- **1944.05** `vienna-market-debt.pawn-watch/pawn-whole-watch` tags: market, debt, money, home, shop
  你把银壳怀表推到绒布托盘上，当铺掌柜用放大镜扫了一眼机芯，报出八克朗。你盯着那张窄票上模糊的赎当日期，想起父亲在第十区工棚里上弦的样子。邻居在门外咳嗽，你捏着票和钱走出来，先拐去房东那里付了半个月的欠租。
  _effects: cash 8 (典当银壳怀表所得); mood -0.040000000000000036; flag +watch_pawned_

- **1945.01** `vienna-life-1945-liberation/postwar-illness` tags: war, liberation, 1945
  Mitzi在战后第二个月病倒，医院没有足够的药，医生只给了退烧粉。邻居每天送来一碗汤，放在门口就走。
  _effects: cash -4 (medicine（其中4克朗记入债务）); condition_

- **1945.01** `af17-office.clerical-luck::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/the-name-was-misspelled` tags: office, politics, bureaucracy, luck, scandal, legal, absurd, vienna
  审计传票上，Mitzi的名字被Ferdinand Huber 2多写了一个字母，门房因此把信退回了收发室。等更正后的传票送到时，真正的采购单已经从另一只抽屉里露了出来；质询会延期两周，Mitzi在走廊里第一次听见有人替自己说话。Ferdinand Huber 2没有邀功，只把那张写错的传票夹进自己的抽屉。
  _effects: state_parameter office-under-siege.exposure 30 -> 15; state_parameter office-under-siege.support 60 -> 68; state_parameter office-under-siege.months 0 -> 1; reputation 0.03; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-124; flag +saved_by_a_typo; mood 0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-95_

- **1945.01** `state_expire:df18-uncanny-mark` tags: df18-uncanny-mark, body, mark, omen, superstition, reputation, mystery, faith
  Mitzi的「不祥的印记」到期；相关安排结束。
  _effects: state_remove df18-uncanny-mark state:world-vienna-1912:420; flag +df18_mark_faded_

- **1945.01** `state_tick:office-under-siege/pressure-year/held-on` tags: office-under-siege, office, scandal, pressure, politics, legal, risk, state
  质询会开了两个小时，Mitzi把每一笔签字都念了一遍。没有人被说服，但也没有人再追问；散会时，支持Mitzi的人比进门时多了两个。
  _effects: state_parameter office-under-siege.support 68 -> 73; state_parameter office-under-siege.exposure 15 -> 11; state_parameter office-under-siege.months 1 -> 2; mood 0.010000000000000009_

- **1945.01** `state_tick:ml18-sudden-fortune/fortune-year/money-keeps-working` tags: ml18-sudden-fortune, money, windfall, sudden_fortune, household, luck, inheritance, lottery
  一年过去，钱没有变多，却也没有一下花完。Mitzi把一部分存进邮局储蓄所，一部分借给楼下的鞋匠；鞋匠每月送来一双补好的鞋，利息就夹在鞋垫下面。街口的杂货商开始把好面粉留到Mitzi来的时候。
  _effects: cash 2 (interest and small repayments on the windfall); state_parameter ml18-sudden-fortune.claims 1 -> 2; mood 0.010000000000000009_

- **1945.01** `agency:support_process` tags: agency, agency:support_process
  Mitzi为环城大道写字楼管理处在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsam出了力：整理名单、传递消息或替同伴作证，名字被记进参与记录。
  _effects: historical development:initiative-building:person-phase15-Mitzi-world-vienna-1912-evt:world-vienna-1912:007211-1944 as supporter; historical_influence; reputation 0.015_

- **1945.08** `state_reaction:ml18-sudden-fortune/a-relative-remembers` tags: ml18-sudden-fortune, money, windfall, sudden_fortune, household, luck, inheritance, lottery
  一个多年没走动的亲戚提着一盒点心上门，先问Mitzi的身体，再问那笔钱是怎么来的。临走时他说起小时候替Mitzi家垫过一冬天的煤钱，数字记不清了，但意思很清楚。
  _effects: state_parameter ml18-sudden-fortune.claims 0 -> 1; flag +windfall_claim_by_relative; mood -0.020000000000000018_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:012011_

- **1945.09** `vienna-church-community.saint-vincent-box/box-request-refused` tags: faith, social, family, home
  登记员翻遍册子，说这户上个月已经领过。楼里的人围着看，谁也没先走开。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-77; reputation -0.04000000000000001; mood -0.050000000000000044_

- **1945.12** `vienna-parish-aid.funeral-society/funeral-society.pay` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  Mitzi数出八赫勒交给Alois Berg，看她在蓝皮本上找到自家门牌，把这一周的格子划掉。Alois Berg说上个月隔壁巷子的人走了，公账出了棺材和一场小弥撒，剩下的钱记在册尾。楼梯口的灯很暗，Mitzi盯着自己的名字看了两遍才转身。
  _effects: cash -0.4 (一周丧葬互助会会费（其中0.4克朗记入债务）); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-1_

- **1946.01** `ml18-tax.audit-of-the-windfall::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/pay-and-regularize` tags: tax, legal, money, windfall, bureaucracy, comic, vienna, urban
  Emil把黑皮本翻到Mitzi那一页，说邮局储蓄的利息没有报，数目不大，但漏了就是漏了。Maria Weiss在旁边把计算尺推来推去，最后算出一个八克朗的数目。Mitzi当天去缴了款，把收据贴在账本最后一页；回家的路上，账本第一次没有在口袋里折着。
  _effects: cash -8 (back taxes and a small fine on the windfall's interest（其中8克朗记入债务）); state_parameter ml18-sudden-fortune.principal 70 -> 62; reputation 0.020000000000000004; relation acquaintance with person-phase15-Emil-world-vienna-1912; knowledge “邮局储蓄的利息也要报税，漏报的八克朗已经补齐。”; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1946.01** `state_tick:raising-child/school-year/struggles` tags: raising-child, family, child, care, school, household, state, state_tick
  Mitzi被叫到学校，老师说Katharina Berg这学期总在课上看窗外。回家的路上，Mitzi没有骂，只把买好的铅笔又数了一遍；晚饭时，两个人都吃得很少。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 18 -> 13; state_parameter raising-child.caregiverBurden 50 -> 55; cash -2 (extra lessons and replacement pencils（其中2克朗记入债务）); mood -0.030000000000000027_

- **1946.04** `vienna-housing-move.moving-day/moving-day-handcart` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money
  手推车每走十几步就要停一下，楼道里的门半开着，有人探头看热闹。Mitzi把照片铁盒交给Otto Lechner 2抱着，自己低头把最后一箱锅具拖上楼。天擦黑时，新屋里才勉强归置出下脚的地方。
  _effects: cash -1 (借手推车买烟熨谢邻居的小开销（其中1克朗记入债务）); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-51_

- **1946.05** `vienna-market-debt.coal-credit/coal-part-cash-part-furniture` tags: market, debt, money, home, shop
  你数出四十赫勒搁在秤盘上，说剩下的一克朗用那张旧折叠桌抵。老板撇了撇嘴，让伙计跟你上楼搬桌子。桌子腿有点晃，但木料还结实。邻居在楼道里搭了把手，账本上这一页只写了一行，没写利息。
  _effects: cash -0.4 (买煤付的部分现钱（其中0.4克朗记入债务）); food; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-60_

- **1946.10** `vienna-market-household.market-credit/market-credit.delay` tags: home, market, food, social
  周五Mitzi摊开手掌，只有两个克朗二十个十字币。玛丽亚挥挥手让下周再给。下周Mitzi把钱放到秤盘上，玛丽亚擦掉名字，却在木牌角上画了个叉。
  _effects: cash -3 (延迟偿还市场赊账（其中3克朗记入债务）); food; reputation -0.05; relation acquaintance with person-phase15-Josef-world-vienna-1912_

- **1947.01** `office16-budget.sign-or-block::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/the-budget-was-already-spent` tags: office, budget, permit, administration, power, economy, vienna, urban
  账本上那笔“市政美化”已经花掉了：前年在广场立了一座铜像，去年给铜像换了底座。Mitzi仍把许可证签了，因为申请人已经把材料款付给了铜像的承包商。冬天，铜像底下多了一圈没有报批的围栏。
  _effects: organization_funds; reputation -0.04; flag +budget_already_spent; knowledge “市政美化预算已经被铜像和底座用掉，许可证的款项来自同一个承包商。”; mood -0.030000000000000027_

- **1947.03** `vienna-parish-aid.sick-call/sick-call.door-only` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  Mitzi把药包和半块肥皂交给开门的孩子，嘱咐用水冲开、一天两次，就下了楼。楼梯间里孩子一直站在门口看着，手里攥着纸包，没关门。Mitzi走到二层又折回去，帮他把门带上，说自己家里也等着火。
  _effects: health 0.01 (没有久留，避开了病人的屋子); relation acquaintance with person-alois-lechner; mood -0.029999999999999916_

- **1947.04** `vienna-medical-treatment.toothache/drops-and-endure` tags: health, medical, family, money, injury
  夜里疼醒时，Mitzi摸黑往棉球上又滴药水，手抖得洒在枕头上。Rosa Huber第二天一早来敲门，看见肿脸就说别硬撑了，可Mitzi只是摆摆手，说再熬一天也许就好了。
  _effects: cash -0.6 (牙痛药水（其中0.6克朗记入债务）); health -0.01 (牙病拖延未治)_

- **1947.10** `vienna-housing-move.rent-notice/rent-notice-demand-repair` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money
  Mitzi没有立刻答话，先去敲Rosa的门。两人一起数落墙上的水渍和门框里灌进来的风，楼梯间的窗户修好了，自家墙皮还在剥落。Rosa提议一起把话带给看门人：先修好漏水的墙角，涨租的事再谈。Mitzi把纸重新折好，请他把话带到门外。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-phase15-Rosa-world-vienna-1912; mood 0.030000000000000027_

- **1948.01** `vienna-life-injury-recovery/clinic-treatment` tags: health, work, family
  Mitzi在受伤后的第三天去了诊所。医生清洗伤口、换药，收下诊费，嘱咐不要急着回机器旁。Mitzi把药方折好，回家路上买了纱布。
  _effects: health 0.05 (wound treatment); cash -8 (clinic and bandages（其中8克朗记入债务）)_

- **1948.07** `r3-crime.stop-and-search::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/turned-out-and-released` tags: crime, police, papers, street, humiliation, vienna, urban, postwar
  Josef Lechner让Mitzi靠墙站好，先拍外套，再翻口袋。车票、钥匙、半块肥皂被摆在人行道上，Josef Bauer提着牛奶壶从旁边绕过去。Josef Lechner把身份证对着路灯看了很久，最后说“走吧”，地上的东西让Mitzi自己捡。
  _effects: mood -0.010000000000000009; flag +stopped_and_searched; knowledge “the patrol checks pockets after curfew”_

  Mitzi发现自己搭在栏杆上的床单被人挪到了绳尾，角上沾了煤灰，一时火起，在楼梯间拦住Luise Kral问是谁动的。Luise Kral说是Josef嫌滴水，两人越说越响，最后各自回屋摔上门，炉子空着，谁也没再去烧水。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-luise-kral_

- **1927.01** `p9-childhood-youth-aspiration.trade-school-application/missing-certificate` tags: adolescence, education, aspiration, school, books, club, exam, task
  Franz Lechner用指甲刮了刮毕业证上的印章，说这印子模糊得像被水泡过。Josef把课程表折好放进口袋，走出教室时听见身后有人问下一个。
  _effects: cash -5 (报名手续费与押金); mood -0.04000000000000001; flag +needs_school_certificate; relation acquaintance with person-luise-kral_

- **1927.01** `state_reaction:yf18-first-awakening/exam-week-distraction` tags: yf18-first-awakening, love, youth, first_love, desire, school, awakening, state
  考试周里，Josef把课本摊在桌上，眼睛却一直看着窗外那条电车线。笔记的页边多了几个没有写完的名字，Josef用橡皮擦掉，纸面起了毛。
  _effects: state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.intensity 69 -> 66; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.talk 3 -> 5_

  Ferdinand Winter先说试三个月，不给工钱，只管一顿午饭。家里同意了，因为多一张嘴比多一双手更难。Josef每天最早到，最晚走，三个月后师傅才把契约拿出来。
  _effects: food; mood -0.04000000000000001; state_add apprenticeship-contract state:world-vienna-1912:85; education apprenticeship; role_

- **1927.09** `yf18-runaway.bundle::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/buy-the-ticket-tonight` tags: runaway, escape, family, plan, night_train, reform, medical, schooling
  Josef趁Josef Bauer去上夜班，把储钱罐砸在窗台上。硬币滚进床缝，Josef趴在地上摸到最后一枚，跑去南站买了一张最便宜的单程票；售票员问要不要往返，Josef说不用，声音比自己想象中平静。
  _effects: state_add yf18-runaway-plan state:world-vienna-1912:84; cash -3 (the savings jar was broken for a ticket); flag +yf18_ticket_bought; mood 0.04000000000000001; flag +yf18_runaway_plan_active_

- **1928.01** `yf18-friend.two-tickets::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/confront-at-the-cloakroom` tags: first_love, friendship, betrayal, dance, tickets, reform, medical, schooling
  Josef在衣帽间堵住Elisabeth Bauer，问那两张票怎么到了Rosa手里。Elisabeth Bauer说Josef从来没敢开口，自己只是替Josef把话说了；Josef把票根揉成一团，塞进Elisabeth Bauer的西装口袋，转身时撞翻了侍者的托盘。
  _effects: state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.intensity 66 -> 71; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.talk 5 -> 15; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.nerve 0 -> 0; relation friend with person-elisabeth-bauer; relation friend with person-phase15-Rosa-world-vienna-1912; reputation -0.01999999999999999; mood -0.05000000000000002_

- **1928.01** `state_reaction:wish-craft/craft-humiliation` tags: wish-craft, wish, wish:craft, craft, work, mastery, state, state_reaction
  Josef把那句轻视记了很久。它没有变成一个决定，但也没有消失。
  _effects: mood -0.03999999999999998; flag +wish:craft:wounded_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:002905_

- **1928.01** `state_tick:yf18-first-awakening/half-year-of-glances/answered-glance` tags: yf18-first-awakening, love, youth, first_love, desire, school, awakening, state
  Josef在教堂门口或学校走廊里又遇见Mitzi。这一次对方先点了头，Josef准备好的整段话全忘了，只把手里的书换到另一只手上；回家后Josef才发现，自己把书拿倒了。
  _effects: state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.intensity 71 -> 76; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.nerve 0 -> 8; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.reciprocated false -> true; mood 0.030000000000000027_

  Josef藏在糖罐后面的硬币少了一小叠。父亲说家里这个月煤钱不够，先拿去用了；Josef看着空出来的那圈灰，什么也没说。那天晚上，Josef第一次认真算了一笔账：从维也纳到格拉茨，到底要攒几个冬天。
  _effects: state_parameter yf18-runaway-plan.suspicion 23 -> 38; state_parameter yf18-runaway-plan.resolve 68 -> 62; mood -0.030000000000000027_

  Josef又学会了一道工序。师傅没有夸，只把下一块料推到面前。
  _effects: state_parameter apprenticeship-contract.progress 11 -> 31; skill; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1928.01** `state_tick:wish-craft/craft-pressure/press-on` tags: wish-craft, wish, wish:craft, craft, work, mastery, state, state_tick
  Josef又把那件没实现的事翻出来想了一遍；这一次没有告诉任何人。
  _effects: mood 0.020000000000000018; flag +wish:craft:pursuing_

  Anna Moser 2来了两次，第三次教室门锁着，黑板上写着“课程暂停”。Josef和Friedrich Winter在走廊里等了一刻钟，最后把绷带卷带回教室，互相缠着玩。
  _effects: mood -0.04999999999999999; flag +first_aid_course_cancelled; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-55_

- **1928.05** `state_reaction:yf18-runaway-plan/a-family-question` tags: yf18-runaway-plan, escape, youth, plan, home, travel, risk, state
  饭桌上又为房租吵起来。Josef把面包掰成很小的块，听见父亲说“这个家养不起闲人”；Josef没有回嘴，只在心里把格拉茨的地址又背了一遍。
  _effects: state_parameter yf18-runaway-plan.suspicion 15 -> 23; state_parameter yf18-runaway-plan.resolve 70 -> 68_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:002900_

- **1928.08** `r13-turn.school-leaving::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement::reform-leaving-age/straight-to-factory` tags: education, school, work, family, transition, reform, medical, schooling
  Franz Lechner把Josef的名字从升学名单上划掉，说厂里正缺人手。家里没有人争，只把午饭里的半块面包塞给Josef。Josef进了纺织厂，工牌上写着“临时”，每天站十二个钟头，月底工钱比学徒多两克朗。
  _effects: cash 2 (毕业钟响过以后); health -0.06000000000000005 (毕业钟响过以后); condition; flag +left_school_for_factory; role_

- **1928.08** `yf18-apprentice.press-guard::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/call-the-inspector` tags: apprenticeship, work, danger, machine, injury, refusal, reform, medical
  Josef趁第二区夜班出租合作社去喝咖啡，跑到街角的工厂检查所，把卸下来的护板指给Luise Kral看。Luise Kral第二天带着卷尺来了，在压印机旁边量了很久；第二区夜班出租合作社陪着笑，等门一关，就把Josef的工具从案子上扫进了木箱。
  _effects: relation employer with person-maria-kral; reputation 0.03; state_parameter apprenticeship-contract.progress 8 -> 11; flag +yf18_reported_press; mood 0.02999999999999997_

- **1929.01** `yf18-runaway.companion::ctx-klosterneuburg-rural-1912::milieu-tenement/stay-and-spend-the-fare` tags: runaway, friendship, companion, plan, betrayal, rural, agriculture, small_town
  Josef把票退了，和Emil在普拉特门口的摊子上吃了两碗热汤。汤里有太多盐，Josef喝到一半就放下勺子；Emil说等明年攒够钱再一起走，Josef点头，没有说自己已经把包袱收进了柜子最下层。
  _effects: state_remove yf18-runaway-plan state:world-vienna-1912:84; cash 2 (the refunded fare); flag +yf18_stayed_for_friend; mood -0.010000000000000009; flag -yf18_runaway_plan_active_

- **1929.01** `task-vienna-poor-apprentice.foreman-introduction/foreman-records-name` tags: adolescence, apprentice, work, education, poverty, printshop, task, task:work
  工头说暂时没有空缺，但答应把他的名字记在招工簿上。亲戚在旁边补了两句好话，工头只点点头；他走出来时，招工簿还摊在门房的木台上。
  _effects: flag +name_in_hiring_book; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-9; mood -0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-9_

- **1929.03** `yf18-body.danube-cabin::ctx-klosterneuburg-rural-1912::milieu-tenement/wade-in-first` tags: body, desire, river, swimming, shame, first_love, rural, agriculture
  多瑙河的浴棚按男女分开，木板缝里能看见对面的人影。Josef穿着借来的毛线泳衣，站在第三间门口不敢下水；Rosa从水里冒出来，头发贴在额头上，说水不冷。Josef走进河里时，满脑子都是自己肋骨上那道疤。
  _effects: state_add yf18-first-awakening state:world-vienna-1912:129; health 0.020000000000000018 (cold river water and a long swim); relation friend with person-phase15-Rosa-world-vienna-1912; mood 0.02999999999999997_

- **1929.07** `vienna-adolescence.brothers-torn-jacket/sell-the-coat` tags: adolescence, school, work, family, social
  旧货贩子把外套翻过来看了看里布，伸出两个手指。Josef接过两枚克朗，塞进鞋垫底下，走回家时脚心一直硌着。晚上Josef Bauer站在门后，手搭在空衣钩上，问外套呢。Josef说不知道，声音比平时高了一点。
  _effects: cash 2 (卖掉哥哥旧外套); relation kin with person-josef-bauer; mood -0.03999999999999998_

- **1930.01** `vienna-family-courtship/ask-to-share-a-home` tags: family, courtship, household, social, lifecycle
  Josef在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang靠窗的桌边，把一只空咖啡杯转了半圈，说房租两人合付、桌子两人共用。Luise Moser的手停在糖罐盖上，问了一句“要是过不下去呢”，没有当场答应，只说要想一想；杯里的咖啡凉了，话还悬在桌面上。
  _effects: interaction; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-41_

  Josef在厨房里对着镜子练了七遍开场白，第二天却在离Rosa三步远的地方拐进面包店。Josef买了一袋并不需要的盐，回家路上把开场白又想了一遍；这一次，连标点都更熟练了。

- **1930.01** `kernel-health.task-house-call::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement::house-call-medical-students/house-call-medical-teaching` tags: health, task, house_call, care, poverty, reform, medical, schooling
  医院派来一名实习医生，身后跟着两个学生。他们把Josef的胸口听了个遍，最后请来上级；药费减半，但Josef被当成了讲课的病例。学生们走后，实习医生把听诊器在手里捂了很久，才敢再贴上来。
  _effects: cash -4 (reduced fee for a teaching-house call); health 0.030000000000000027 (examined by a teaching team); condition; role; knowledge “教学出诊会有实习医生和学生轮流听诊，最后请上级定方案，药费可以减半。”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-1; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-30_

- **1930.08** `vienna-courtship-courtyard.family-table/wage-question-silence` tags: adulthood, courtship, partner, social, family, partnership
  Josef Bauer问起工钱和房租，Josef报了个数，又补了一句冬天活少。桌上一时只剩刀叉声。Luise Moser低着头把面包掰成小块，Josef Bauer往汤里又加了一勺盐，谁都没再开口；这顿饭把两个人的将来问成了一个没答完的数目。
  _effects: mood -0.07; relation kin with person-josef-bauer; relation partner with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-41_

- **1930.08** `statistical_infection`
  Josef在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang病倒了，肺炎让身体发热、虚弱，接下来的日子只能卧床。

- **1930.09** `hs18-cinema.newsreel::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/recognize-a-neighbor` tags: cinema, newsreel, neighbor, recognition, media, history, reform, medical
  银幕上是城西的街口，镜头扫过一排晾衣绳。Josef认出绳下站着Friedrich Huber，也认出旁边那个被两名便衣夹住的人。散场灯亮时，Friedrich Huber还在笑，Josef却把围巾往上拉了拉。
  _effects: knowledge “新闻片里那个被便衣夹住的人，我认识。”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-5; flag +recognized_neighbor_on_newsreel; mood -0.02999999999999997; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-17_

- **1930.09** `statistical_illness_worsened`
  Josef的肺炎没有完全好转，咳嗽和发热又拖了一个月，家里人轮流守着。

- **1930.10** `vienna-medical-treatment.night-fever/corner-doctor` tags: health, medical, family, money
  药粉含在嘴里发苦，Josef就着糖浆咽下去，喉头一阵凉。医生拎包出门时，楼梯间的煤气灯刚好灭了，Franz Lechner摸黑送他到拐角，回来时手里攥着找回的几枚赫勒。
  _effects: cash -4 (医生出诊费和药费); health 0.05000000000000002 (药粉和糖浆缓解症状); relation friend with person-franz-lechner_

- **1930.12** `statistical_recovery`
  Josef从肺炎中慢慢恢复，能下床喝水，但身体还虚弱。

  床位要等一年，Josef先回家。白天咳得少了就做点糊盒活，晚上把痰吐进一只带盖的杯子；Mitzi Lechner送来一瓶鱼肝油，说山里的空气不要钱，可惜搬不去。杯子每天煮一次，杯沿还是有一圈洗不掉的印子。

- **1931.05** `vienna-market-household.soup-kitchen/soup-kitchen.keep` tags: home, market, food, social
  扁豆汤的香味从门缝飘出去，Maria Winter 2敲了门。Josef掀开锅盖又盖上，说只够自家吃。Maria Winter 2什么也没说就走了。夜里Josef听见隔壁有人用勺子刮碗底。
  _effects: food; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-50; mood -0.050000000000000044_

  Josef把地址纸条递给门房，门房在招工簿上写下他的名字。试工两天，他跟着Rosa Winter给车床油箱上油，手指沾满黑油。小巷电影院在周末把他留下，说下周起按周发薪，先领八克朗，中午在厂里吃一顿。
  _effects: employment 机器厂上油工 @ cinema:person-luise-kral-evt:world-vienna-1912:002198; cash 8 (第一周工钱); skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-26_

  _effects: cash 2 (分期补发的头一笔欠薪); skill; relation employer with person-maria-kral; mood -0.040000000000000036_

- **1932.08** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.shared_stove_fire/fire_no_damage` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Josef刚把锅端下灶，火苗就蹿了起来，Alois Weiss一脚踩灭地上的油布，顺手抄起沙箱里的沙子盖住灶口。两人都没受伤，但Alois Weiss的鞋底烧穿一个洞，那是唯一一双出门的鞋。

  _effects: mood 0.020000000000000018; flag +knows_downstairs_quarrel_

  _effects: flag +taught_parent_to_write_name; role; skill; relation kin with person-josef-bauer; skill_

  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-17; mood 0.03999999999999998; flag +parish_sick_visitor_

  学徒在旁边小声说，拒绝按手印的人名字会被单独抄在一张纸上。Josef没接话，把八克朗换成一克朗的硬币，数给家里时硬币在桌上滚了两圈才停。屋里的煤油灯芯剪得太短，光只够照亮手边的桌面。

  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:008352_

- **1940.01** `state_reaction:oj18-odd-apprenticeship/the-thing-in-the-basement` tags: oj18-odd-apprenticeship, work, odd_job, apprenticeship, night, trust, secrecy, state
  夜班结束前，Josef听见地下室传来拖拽声。下去时，门开着，地上只有一道湿拖把的痕迹；第二天，所有人都说那间屋子锁了十年。
  _effects: state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.strangeKnowledge 20 -> 28; state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.trust 22 -> 19; mood -0.02999999999999997; knowledge “地下室那扇门后面有东西被搬动过，但巡查簿上没有记录。”_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:008883_

- **1940.01** `office16-rival.removal-motion::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/the-vote-ties` tags: office, rivalry, politics, removal, power, intrigue, vienna, urban
  第二轮投票是十七比十七。按章程，主席可以投第二票；Josef把自己的名字念了两遍，会议室里没有人笑。散会后，Friedrich Hofer在门口说下一次会带更多的人来，Josef说椅子够用。
  _effects: reputation -0.01999999999999999; mood -0.020000000000000018; relation rival with person-friedrich-hofer; flag +tie_broken_by_chair; knowledge “章程允许主席在平票时投第二票，但没有人记得上一次使用是什么时候。”_

- **1940.01** `state_tick:oj18-odd-apprenticeship/strange-year/trusted-with-the-keys` tags: oj18-odd-apprenticeship, work, odd_job, apprenticeship, night, trust, secrecy, state
  一年过去，Josef已经能闭着眼说出哪块地板会响、哪盏煤气灯该在几点关。师傅把备用钥匙交下来，没有解释为什么第三把钥匙磨得最亮；Josef也没有问，只在巡查簿最后一页画了一个小小的记号。
  _effects: state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.trust 19 -> 24; state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.strangeKnowledge 28 -> 33; state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.nightsWorked 0 -> 12; cash 1 (night-shift wages); skill_

  _effects: mood -0.020000000000000018_

  “先替我看两个月仓库。”Berta Hofer说得很平，好像这是最省事的安排。Josef跟着到仓库，麻袋咬破的地方漏出一路面粉，角落里那张床短得伸不直腿。
  _effects: relation kin with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-30; food; condition_

- **1940.04** `vienna-historical-social-movement.district-list/organize-relief-table` tags: history, historical_process, social_movement, organization, politics
  Mitzi Kral 2从围裙里掏出几张揉皱的收据，Josef把它们按日期排开。屋外有人上楼，脚步在门口停了一下又走了。Josef把新表折成四折塞进衣袋，说先管这条街，别的明天再说。
  _effects: skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-71; mood 0.030000000000000027; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-71_

  Luise Berg 3的信从战地寄来，Rosa Berg 2先拆开读了一遍，再把能念的段落念给Josef听。念到“等我”时，Rosa Berg 2停了一下，把信纸折回去，说剩下的等打完仗再读。
  _effects: role; relation partner with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-86; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-60; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-60_

- **1940.09** `major-crisis.rent-increase/tenant-meeting` tags: crisis, housing, rent, eviction, family, poverty
  巷口洗衣房的老板娘把几张椅子摆开，叫来半条街的租户。Josef把涨租单摊在桌上，逐条念出上面的数字；Otto Berg站在门边，既没走，也没替房东说话。大家决定先集体迟交一个月，签名纸传到Josef手里时，纸上已经有一块油渍。
  _effects: cash -2 (paper, ink and tram fare for the tenants meeting); role; flag +rent_strike; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-37; reputation 0.03; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-37_

- **1941.01** `vienna-family-fatherhood/child-born-at-home` tags: family, childbirth, death, lifecycle, birth
  Luise Berg 3在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang生下一个孩子，取名Anna Bauer。Josef在厨房和走廊之间来回，接生的人让烧水、拿布、付六克朗；孩子天亮后睡着了，Josef从此在户籍表上多了“父亲”一栏，手背上还留着烫水壶的红印。
  _effects: birth female; cash -6 (midwife fee)_

- **1941.01** `curse15-betrayal.signature::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/the-seal-was-used` tags: betrayal, office, power, politics, curse, institution, vienna, urban
  Rosa用Josef留在抽屉里的印章签了一份采购单，把库房里最后一批煤卖给了城外的砖厂。Josef发现时，账已经做平，签字栏里是Josef自己的名字。Rosa说只是替Josef省了麻烦，还把那支用旧的印泥放回了原处。
  _effects: cash -3 (为平账先垫出去的钱); reputation -0.1; condition; flag +seal_used_by_trusted_hand; mood -0.06999999999999995_

  楼上住户投诉夜里有人拖椅子，管事的把巡查簿翻到Josef的那一页，说签字太潦草，看不出巡过几趟。Josef想辩解，却想起那晚确实在楼梯间多站了一刻钟；扣掉的工钱刚好够买一双不漏水的鞋。

  Emil Weiss 2的孩子在人群里哭，Josef把Emil Weiss 2往后推了一步，自己站到第七个位置。Luise Stein数人头时，Josef说“名单上是我”；Luise Stein看了Josef一眼，让士兵把Emil Weiss 2放走。当晚，Josef被关进钟楼下的储藏室。

  Josef把Katharina Berg的名字写在“联络员”后面，月薪一克朗，职责是每周来两次、把外面的消息带进来。Gustav Winter说这个岗位没有编制，Josef说先按临时用工算；第一个月，Katharina Berg带来的消息比文件还多。

  姐姐家在多瑙河对岸，院子里晾着床单。孩子在那里住了一个月，回来时脸圆了些，手里攥着一块河边的白石子。可他站在门口不肯叫Josef，姐姐蹲下来哄，Josef把行李提进屋，听见身后孩子叫了一声姨。
  _effects: mood -0.09999999999999998; relation kin with person-elisabeth-bauer; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-77_

- **1942.06** `vienna-market-debt.rent-arrears/rent-negotiate-installments` tags: market, debt, money, home, shop
  你没去借钱，直接下楼找房东，说月底前先付两克朗，剩下两克朗分两个星期补齐。房东叼着雪茄，拿铅笔在你的纸条背面写了个日期，说再拖一天就换锁。邻居在楼道口听见了，冲你摇了摇头，但没说什么。
  _effects: cash -2 (按分期付掉的第一笔欠租（其中2克朗记入债务）); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-50; flag +rent_installment_plan_

- **1942.07** `vienna-housing-move.sublet-room/sublet-declined` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money
  Josef正要把空床许给Rosa，Gustav Kral却在楼梯上拦住了，说这条街上去年有人招了生人合住，失过钥匙，惹出些麻烦。Josef回家把铺盖重新叠好，回绝了Rosa，只让他白天来喝口热水。空床仍空着，房租还是从自己的工钱里补。
  _effects: mood -0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-74; relation acquaintance with person-phase15-Rosa-world-vienna-1912_

  新来的军官站在市政厅门口，旧办事员还在窗后盖章。Josef排队领一张新的通行证，窗口里的人低声说：“名字照旧写，住址别改。”队伍里没有人说话，只有纸张翻动的声音。
  _effects: state_add occupied-city-regime state:world-vienna-1912:536; mood -0.030000000000000027; knowledge “旧办事员仍在窗后盖章，只要名字和住址不改，通行证还能办。”_

- **1943.01** `vienna-family-same-sex-male/private-arrangement` tags: family, intimacy, same_sex, persecution, lifecycle, partnership
  Josef和Hermann Winter 2在城边租了一间小屋，对外只说是同事合住。房租四克朗，两人不同时出门，信件用假名，夜里把窗帘拉严；Hermann Winter 2在门后放了一根木棍，Josef把两人的工票分开放，连咳嗽都压着声。
  _effects: relation partner with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-114; mood 0.040000000000000036; cash -4 (separate room rent（其中3克朗记入债务）); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-114_

  _effects: position 档案助理 @zoo:person-phase15-Josef-world-vienna-1912-evt:world-vienna-1912:007785; relation kin with person-elisabeth-bauer; flag +hired_relative; knowledge “考勤表上记了七次迟到，工资一次没有扣。”; mood 0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-124_

  院子里晾着三条床单，风一过就拍到人脸上。Josef把备用钥匙和一张照相馆拍的合影交还，Hermann Winter 2把那件借来的外套叠好递回。两人说好各找各的住处，谁都没有提高声音；分手分得干净，可两个人心里都空了一块。
  _effects: relation_end partner with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-114; mood -0.08999999999999997; flag +courtship_ended_cleanly_

  _effects: cash -1 (没有地址的信); mood -0.08000000000000018; condition; flag +letter_returned_undeliverable; relation kin with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-26_

  Josef没有让Hedwig进门，只把救济站的地址写在烟盒背面，又画了怎么绕过岗亭。Hedwig把烟盒攥在手里，鞠了一躬。第二天，走廊的墙上多了一张用铅笔写的谢谢。
  _effects: reputation 0.03; flag +directed_refugee_to_relief; knowledge “the relief office opens at seven and does not ask for a resi”_

  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-elisabeth-bauer; knowledge “空屋的门牌号也能通过登记。”; flag +gave_false_refugee_address; mood -0.040000000000000036_

  Josef用配给卡换到半袋面粉和几根冻土豆。锅里煮的是稀汤，邻居送来一小块人造黄油，说孩子们先吃。
  _effects: food_

- **1944.10** `p9-social-tenement-neighbors.stove-and-line/stove-and-line.first-come-order` tags: housing, neighbors, tenement, daily, money, reputation
  Josef把四只洗衣盆按到厨房的先后排成一列，用粉笔在门后写下各家的时辰：六点到七点半、七点半到九点。Josef Bauer起初嘟囔，看到自家名字排在第二格，也就把水壶提了回去。晾衣绳按同样次序分两段，谁家的床单先上谁先占。
  _effects: mood 0.040000000000000036; relation acquaintance with person-josef-bauer_

- **1945.01** `vienna-life-1945-liberation/survives` tags: war, liberation, 1945
  苏联士兵进城时，Josef躲在门后听见坦克履带压过石板路。几天后街口贴出告示，Josef去领了一份汤和一块面包。
  _effects: role; food; cash -2 (replacement documents（其中2克朗记入债务）)_

- **1945.01** `office16-hire.protege::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/hired-competent` tags: office, hire, patronage, bureaucracy, work, power, vienna, urban
  Josef把Maria Berg的名字写在编制表的空行上，又在旁边注了“试用三个月”。Franz Berg提醒说预算只够半个位置，Josef还是把名字留下了。第二天，Maria Berg提前半小时到，把走廊里的旧档案按年份重新排了一遍。
  _effects: position 助理文书 @zoo:person-phase15-Josef-world-vienna-1912-evt:world-vienna-1912:007785; organization_funds; relation colleague with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000151:child; reputation 0.03; flag +hired_protege_

  十二赫勒换来两杯淡啤酒，找零是四枚一赫勒的铜币。Josef把铜币收好，听师兄讲那年冬天的事，讲到一半师兄停了停，说“你别到处讲”。酒馆里有人拉手风琴，声音盖过了后半句。
  _effects: cash -1 (请师兄喝一杯啤酒（其中1克朗记入债务）); relation colleague with person-phase15-Rosa-world-vienna-1912; skill_

  Grete Weiss把押金收据和住院单钉在一起，让Josef去三楼东侧等床。走廊里全是折叠床，Rosa给Josef一条洗得发硬的毯子，说今晚先别喝水。Josef把鞋塞在枕头下面，听着隔壁的呻吟睡了过去。
  _effects: cash -8 (住院处窗口的押金（其中8克朗记入债务）); health 0.05999999999999999 (住院处窗口的押金); condition; flag +hospitalized_third_class; relation acquaintance with person-phase15-Rosa-world-vienna-1912_

- **1945.03** `p9-social-tenement-neighbors.rent-note/rent-note.look-for-cheaper-bed` tags: housing, neighbors, tenement, daily, money, reputation
  Josef没去事务所，只在洗衣房打听更便宜的床位。有人提到利奥波德城另一条街上有一间合住的屋子，一个月少一克朗半，只是要跟三个人共用一间。Josef去看了两趟，量了窗子和床的位置，回来把箱子底下的旧报纸铺开算账。
  _effects: flag +looking_for_cheaper_housing; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-84_

- **1946.01** `office16-budget.sign-or-block::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/signed-for-a-favor` tags: office, budget, permit, administration, power, economy, vienna, urban
  Rosa在签字前把一张剧院包厢的票根留在桌上，说是“给孩子的”。Josef把票根推回去，却在许可证上签了名；排水图仍缺一页，但窗口当天就盖了章。扩建开工后，第一场雨把后院淹了半尺。
  _effects: organization_funds; relation friend with person-phase15-Rosa-world-vienna-1912; flag +permit_for_favor; knowledge “许可证少了一页排水图，盖章时没有补。”; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1946.01** `agency:attend_meeting` tags: agency, agency:attend_meeting
  Josef去了Berggasse 19, Wohnung und Ordination，坐在后排听完了关于Wiener Psychoanalytische Vereinigung在Berggass的争论；散会时带走一张油印提纲。
  _effects: historical development:initiative-org-wednesday-psychological-society-1936 as participant; knowledge “我参加了Wiener Psychoanalytische Vereinigung在Berggasse 19,。”; mood 0.030000000000000027_

- **1946.04** `vienna-market-household.market-credit/market-credit.pay-later` tags: home, market, food, social
  Josef捏着三个克朗硬币站在玛丽亚的菜摊前，土豆和洋葱已经装进布袋。周五结清时，玛丽亚用粉笔在小黑板上划掉名字，Josef Weiss点点头。
  _effects: cash -3 (偿还市场赊账（其中3克朗记入债务）); food; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-8_

- **1946.08** `vienna-legacy-worklife.union-office/union-office.write-off` tags: legacy, work, family, reform, ordinary_life
  窗外有人来问入会，接待的人一时不知道怎么开口，Josef替他说“进来坐”。老成员把账本拿回去，用一块旧布包好，放进抽屉最里面。抽屉关上的声音比平时响。
  _effects: mood -0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-60; flag +union_debt_written_off_

- **1947.01** `office16-rival.removal-motion::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/motion-carries` tags: office, rivalry, politics, removal, power, intrigue, vienna, urban
  投票结果是十九比十七。Josef把印章、钥匙和一本写满批注的账本放进纸箱，最后一圈走廊走得比平时慢。Gustav Winter在门口等着接手，手里已经拿着新的编制表；表上第一个被划掉的名字是Josef的秘书。
  _effects: position_end 夜班饲养员 @zoo:person-phase15-Josef-world-vienna-1912-evt:world-vienna-1912:007785; reputation -0.05; mood -0.030000000000000027; condition; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-12; flag +removed_by_vote; knowledge “新编制表第一个划掉的是我的秘书。”_

- **1947.01** `vienna-social-movement.recruitment/join-and-carry` tags: development, social_movement, organization, politics
  Josef在楼梯下面接到一张折成四折的传单。纸上没有署名，只写着下一次集会的地址。Josef去了，在昏暗的屋里报上姓名，成为第二区夜班出租合作社的一员，并答应负责自己所在街区的联络。
  _effects: organization_membership; role; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-84_

- **1947.01** `p10-migration-tasks.move-district/move-stay-and-bargain` tags: adulthood, migration, housing, family, travel, task, moving, district
  Josef最终没有搬，把箱子又拖回楼上，跟旧房东坐在厨房桌边谈了半个钟头。按月付、煤账分两次清，房东要他每周扫一次楼道。孩子不用换学校，母亲的药也还在原来的药房抓，只是房租每月多了八十赫勒。
  _effects: cash -3 (按月先付一部分房租与旧欠煤账); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-74_

- **1947.04** `p9-social-family-conflict-care.sick-parent-night/shifts-with-sibling` tags: family, care, siblings, inheritance, housing, money
  Josef和Emil把值班时辰写在门后的粉笔板上：Emil守前半夜，Josef守后半夜，医生说静养二十一天。煤油灯芯拧小，药粉分成小包摆在床头，便盆换水时尽量不碰出响。二十一天熬下来，Josef的眼皮肿了，走路发飘，但Josef Bauer每次起身总有人扶。
  _effects: relation kin with person-phase15-Emil-world-vienna-1912; flag +night_care_duty_

- **1948.01** `vienna-family-same-sex-male/denounced-to-police` tags: family, intimacy, same_sex, persecution, lifecycle, partnership, unemployment
  旅店伙计记下了Josef和Ferdinand Hofer 2的名字。几天后警察来敲门，把两人带去问话；Josef丢了工位，罚了八克朗，Ferdinand Hofer 2的家里把人接走。Josef在档案里留下一条警察记录，手指上还留着问话时磕出的淤青。
  _effects: relation partner with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-119; reputation -0.2; unemployment (dismissed after police visit); cash -8 (fine and lost work（其中8克朗记入债务）); role; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-119_

- **1948.01** `state_tick:wish-craft/craft-pressure/put-away` tags: wish-craft, wish, wish:craft, craft, work, mastery, state, state_tick
  Josef暂时把那件事收进抽屉；日子还有别的东西要应付。
  _effects: mood -0.020000000000000018_

- **1948.01** `kernel-crisis.task-rescue-neighbor::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/rescue-pulls-neighbor-out` tags: task, rescue, fire, neighbor, betrayal, vienna, urban, postwar
  烟从阁楼窗口往外翻，Josef用湿毛巾捂住口鼻，摸到楼梯口。Alois Lechner瘫在门边，鞋底已经烧软；Josef把人拖到院子里，自己的手掌粘掉一层皮。消防队来后，把烧黑的楼梯封了，谁也不能再上去。邻居的鞋底在门槛上留下两块黑印。
  _effects: condition; relation acquaintance with person-alois-lechner; role; cash -2 (bandages, ointment and a new shirt（其中2克朗记入债务）); knowledge “阁楼起火时烟比火先到，湿毛巾只能顶一会儿；把人拖出来以后手掌会粘掉一层皮。”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-66_

- **1948.07** `r1-civ.cellar-press::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/distributed-via-church-box` tags: politics, press, underground, print, risk, vienna, urban, postwar
  Hermann Winter 2没有按时来。Josef抱着纸包在电车站等到收班，最后把纸塞进教堂门口的意见箱。第二天，纸上的一段话被抄在面包店的包装纸上，没有人知道是谁印的，Josef却认出了自己的错字。
  _effects: cash -0.5 (地窖里的脚踏印刷机（其中0.5克朗记入债务）); mood 0.050000000000000044; reputation 0.03; flag +distributed_sheet_via_church_box; knowledge “the church suggestion box is emptied only once a week”_

- **1948.08** `vienna-social-movement.meeting/speak-and-organize` tags: development, social_movement, organization, meeting
  Josef在后屋的会议上报出自己街区的名字：谁家缺煤，谁被房东催租，谁知道哪间印刷厂还在招人。环城大道写字楼管理处把这些事记进一本旧账本，散会时每个人带走一叠更小的纸条。
  _effects: skill; reputation 0.02_

- **1949.01** `r13-fam.join-club::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/voice-rejected` tags: task, social, club, choir, organization, belonging, vienna, urban
  Josef唱到第二段就被Elisabeth Bauer打断，说声音太直，会把整排带跑。Freie Gewerkschaft der graphischen Arbeiter Wiens的人没有笑，只是把歌本收回去。Josef在门口等到散场，问能不能先搬椅子、烧炉子；Elisabeth Bauer说缺的是嗓子，不是手。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-elisabeth-bauer; knowledge “the choir chooses by voice, not by willingness”_

- **1949.05** `vienna-postwar-housing-1950s/waiting-list` tags: adulthood, postwar, housing, family
  Josef去住房局问结果，办事员翻过卡片说分数不够：家里人口少，现住房虽挤但还没到拆除标准。回程的电车上，Anton 2劝他明年再递一次申请。
  _effects: role; cash -2 (application and tram fare（其中2克朗记入债务）); mood -0.030000000000000027; relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:002801:child_

  复核结果下来，申诉驳回，理由是“未提出新事实”。Josef Lechner把新的离境日期念了一遍，Anton Moser站在楼梯口，说愿意作证Josef在这里住了九年。Gustav Winter把证词夹进档案，说“九年不算新事实”。
  _effects: mood -0.020000000000000018; condition; flag +appeal_dismissed; relation friend with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:001330:child; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-2; knowledge “nine years of residence is not new evidence”_

  Josef没有填表，只把半块面包和一张电车票塞进孩子的口袋。Gustav Winter敲了敲桌子，叫下一个。Luise 2追到门口，问姓名；Josef摆摆手，说名字写在票背面。其实票背面什么也没有。
  _effects: cash -1 (gave away a tram ticket（其中1克朗记入债务）); flag +helped_refugee_without_papers; mood 0.020000000000000018_

  Josef和Josef Stein 2在第一个酒棚就坐下了，要了一壶新酒，Alois Lechner摸出一副磨损的纸牌。三人打到下午，太阳偏西也没再往上爬；Josef输了几个铜板，连酒带牌局一共一克朗五十赫勒，倒赢回一肚子笑话。回程的电车上，Josef靠着窗，酒意上来，差点坐过站。
  _effects: cash -1.5 (酒棚的新酒和牌局输的铜板); relation acquaintance with person-alois-lechner; mood 0.040000000000000036_

- **1950.02** `vienna-parish-aid.rent-box/rent-box.accept-all` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  门铃是根铁丝拉的，响得发闷。Hermann Lechner提篮进门先看水槽里泡着的碗，再看墙角的煤箱，说价钱她心里有数。Josef把印泥盒推到桌边，按下去时指尖凉了一下。
  _effects: cash 6 (互助会补上本月房租差额); food; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-25; flag +parish_registered; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-25_

- **1950.06** `vienna-courtship-courtyard.sunday-walk/separate-coffee` tags: adulthood, courtship, partner, social, family
  两人拐进街角的咖啡馆，站到锌皮柜台前。Elisabeth Moser点了牛奶咖啡，Josef要了黑咖啡，十二赫勒一杯。侍者把两杯推过来时，两人几乎同时把钱币按在柜台上，各付各的，谁也没说什么；那一声铜板响，把客气摆得比咖啡还清楚。
  _effects: cash -0.12 (自己那杯黑咖啡); relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:002529:child_

  Ferdinand Hofer 2把门开了一条缝，说自家也不够烧。Josef提着空筐站了一会儿，听见门后有孩子在哭，便没有再敲。回屋后，他把椅子拆了一条腿，塞进炉子。
  _effects: flag +refused_neighbour_help; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-119_

- **1951.08** `vienna-social-movement.recruitment/report-to-official` tags: development, social_movement, organization, politics
  Josef把传单交给楼里的门房。门房没有接，只让Josef自己去找警察局。那天傍晚，两个穿便衣的人在街口站了很久。
  _effects: reputation 0.02_

- **1951.10** `vienna-legacy-worklife.union-office/union-office.write-off` tags: legacy, work, family, reform, ordinary_life
  账本的纸边已经发毛，Josef翻到那一页时，手指沾上一层灰。有人说“三十四克朗够买两袋面粉”，没人接话。最后举手表决，赞成销账的手比反对的多两只，老成员把手放下去时，椅子又晃了一下。
  _effects: mood -0.040000000000000036; relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:001982:child; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-152_

  货场的煤烟落在肩上。Elisabeth Moser帮他扛第一袋时教他把腰沉下去。站务员在名册边上记了日期，说干得好才有正式名分，Josef点头，把袋子往肩上一顶。

  两张车票分开买，Josef在月台这头，Emil Berg在那头。火车开动后两人才在车厢连接处站到一起，谁也没有说话，只把行李往同一张座位底下推。

- **1952.08** `vienna-parish-aid.funeral-society/funeral-society.quit` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  Josef说互助会先退了，这笔钱要留给煤和面包。Josef Stein 2没争辩，在蓝皮本上把名字划了一道，问清楚是不是全家都退，然后把划掉的那行折进本子夹缝。她说以后想再进，要重新排三个月。门关上，Josef听见铅笔在楼梯上轻轻敲了两下栏杆。
  _effects: cash 0.4 (省下本周会费留作煤钱); flag +funeral_society_left_

  信封没封牢，Josef在灯下把它掀开，里面是维也纳联合银行的催款单，欠了三十四克朗。她认出那名字是楼上常穿黑裙的女人，便把纸原样叠回去，用米汤把封口粘好，放到门房窗台上。
  _effects: mood -0.040000000000000036; flag +knows_upstairs_debt_

  _effects: cash -0.6 (三杯 Melange 的咖啡钱); mood 0.050000000000000044; skill_

  _effects: flag +movement_list_holder; reputation 0.029999999999999995_

  Josef和Ferdinand Stein买了影戏棚最便宜的边座，边座和两粒糖一共五十赫勒。一刻钟的活动影戏里火车朝观众冲过来，棚里有人叫出了声。散场后两人还站在门口看下一场的招牌，Josef把剩下几个铜板在口袋里攥得发热，又买了两粒糖，一人一粒含回家。
  _effects: cash -0.5 (影戏棚边座和两粒糖); mood 0.050000000000000044; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-98; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-98_

  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:018201_

  _effects: cash 6 (互助会补上本月房租差额); food_

  _effects: cash -0.14 (两只 Semmel（其中0.14克朗记入债务）); food; mood 0.030000000000000027_

  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-45; mood 0.040000000000000036; flag +tenant_collective; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-45_

- **1955.05** `vienna-market-debt.coal-credit/coal-part-cash-part-furniture` tags: market, debt, money, home, shop
  你数出四十赫勒搁在秤盘上，说剩下的一克朗用那张旧折叠桌抵。老板撇了撇嘴，让伙计跟你上楼搬桌子。桌子腿有点晃，但木料还结实。邻居在楼道里搭了把手，账本上这一页只写了一行，没写利息。

  _effects: mood 0.030000000000000027; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-154; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-154_

- **1956.06** `p9-social-tenement-neighbors.flour-loan/flour-loan.full-bag` tags: housing, neighbors, tenement, daily, money, reputation
  Josef把一整袋面粉倒进Anton Hofer的空口袋，袋口用麻绳扎了两道，说等她男人发了薪再还。Anton Hofer连声道谢，把写着借煤日期的小纸片折好放回围裙。柜子里只剩一袋面粉，Josef当晚煮汤时多加了一勺水。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-66_

- **1956.08** `vienna-market-debt.pawn-watch/pawn-whole-watch` tags: market, debt, money, home, shop
  你把银壳怀表推到绒布托盘上，当铺掌柜用放大镜扫了一眼机芯，报出八克朗。你盯着那张窄票上模糊的赎当日期，想起父亲在第十区工棚里上弦的样子。邻居在门外咳嗽，你捏着票和钱走出来，先拐去房东那里付了半个月的欠租。
  _effects: cash 8 (典当银壳怀表所得); mood -0.06000000000000005; flag +watch_pawned_

- **1957.01** `p9-social-daily-culture.last-standing-ticket/p9-social-daily-culture.last-standing-ticket.church-organ` tags: daily, culture, leisure, cafe, music, walk, task, task:culture
  Josef数了数前面攒动的人头，把口袋里的硬币又攥紧了些，转身离开队伍。走到街角的教堂时，管风琴正从敞开的门里传出来，Josef在最后一排长凳上坐下，听完整段前奏曲，一个赫勒也没花，只跪得膝盖发酸。出门时与Anton Hofer道了别，约好下回还来排队。
  _effects: mood 0.030000000000000027; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-66_

  _effects: cash -4 (医生出诊费和药费); health 0.05 (药粉和糖浆缓解症状); relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-102_

### 235 subject events

- **1914.06** `iw18-infancy.first-word::ctx-vienna-1912::milieu-tenement/the-dead-grandmothers-name` tags: infancy, first_word, memory, family, wonder, eerie, vienna, urban
  Rosa扶着桌沿站起来，指着门口的空处，清清楚楚叫出一个名字。屋里没人教过这个词；Alois Lechner手里的铝勺停在半空。那是去年冬天埋掉的老人的名字，窗外正好有送煤的车经过，铁轮压过石缝。
  _effects: mood -0.03; knowledge “孩子说出的第一个词，是去年死去的祖母的名字。”; flag +first_word_dead_name_

- **1916.09** `p9-childhood-household.name-day-gift/passed-gift-to-younger` tags: childhood, family, household, home, siblings, illness, neighbors
  木马在Rosa手里还没捂热，就转到了弟妹怀里。Rosa从纸包底下抽出那本图画书，封面卷了角，边上有人用铅笔写过字。Alois Lechner看看木马，又看看书，把报纸叠好收进抽屉。那晚Rosa把书压在枕头下，借着窗缝的光翻了三页。
  _effects: relation kin with person-alois-lechner; mood 0.04; flag +likes_reading_

- **1918.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.laundry-help/laundry-dragged-docked-pay` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, laundry, work
  “你看看这角。”Josef Bauer把床单拎起来，煤渣簌簌往下掉。Rosa伸手去拍，反而把灰按进布纹里。Josef Bauer从工钱里数回两枚，只留一赫勒在Rosa手心，让重洗那角。晚饭前Rosa一直蹲在井台边，换了两盆水，指甲缝里全是黑泥。
  _effects: cash 0.01 (被扣后只剩一赫勒); mood -0.05; relation acquaintance with person-josef-bauer_

- **1918.03** `p9-childhood-household.fever-night/hospital-deposit` tags: childhood, family, household, home, siblings, illness, neighbors
  医院走廊里全是湿鞋印。Alois Lechner把Rosa放在长椅上，自己去窗口交钱，六克朗换来一张薄薄的收据。护士把Rosa领进观察室，白灯照得人睁不开眼。Rosa抓着Alois Lechner的袖口不放，直到药水推进胳膊才松手。
  _effects: cash -6 (医院急诊押金和电车票（其中6克朗记入债务）); health 0.08 (在医院接受处理); mood -0.04_

- **1919.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.market-line/line-both-families` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, market, food
  队伍拐弯时，Luise Stein朝Rosa招手。Rosa挤过去，替两家把面包领出来，纸包用不同的绳结系住。家里人晚上解开绳结，先掂了掂份量，再问价钱；Rosa把每一包的面包数、找零和欠的铜板都说了一遍，家里人听完才把两包分别收进柜子。
  _effects: food; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-4; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-4_

- **1920.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.laundry-help/laundry-bread-with-friend` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, laundry, work
  Luise Stein家的孩子站在绳子另一头，Rosa递夹子时故意慢半拍，两个人把床单拉得没有一道褶。收工后Luise Stein端出半包隔夜面包，掰开时掉了一桌屑。Rosa把分到的那块用围裙兜住，回家路上闻着麦味，硬是没咬。
  _effects: food; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-4; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-22_

- **1921.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.market-line/line-sold-out-potatoes` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, market, food
  “卖完了。”三个字从窗口里传出来，Rosa后面的队伍立刻散了一半。Rosa没走，趴在窗台上看伙计扫面屑，直到人家挥手赶人。旁边摊子的土豆带着泥，Rosa用围裙兜住，一枚一枚数铜板，回家报账时连找零都说清了。
  _effects: food; mood -0.03; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-1; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-24_

- **1921.03** `statistical_infection`
  Rosa在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang病倒了，肺炎让身体发热、虚弱，接下来的日子只能卧床。

- **1921.06** `statistical_recovery`
  Rosa从肺炎中慢慢恢复，能下床喝水，但身体还虚弱。

- **1921.09** `vienna-childhood-friends.courtyard-race/fell-on-wet-stones` tags: childhood, social, school, street, injury
  晒衣绳上的水一滴一滴落在石板上。Rosa跑到第二圈，鞋底一滑，膝盖先着地。Alois Lechner从屋里端出半盆清水，蹲下冲掉伤口里的沙，又用手帕扎紧，说今天别跑了。
  _effects: health -0.04 (膝盖在湿石板上磕破); relation acquaintance with person-alois-lechner_

- **1922.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.laundry-help/laundry-three-heller` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, laundry, work
  床单浸了碱水，沉得拽不动。Rosa站在木凳上，把湿布一点点递过绳，水顺着肘弯滴进袖口。Friedrich Huber在绳头数夹子，最后一条挂好，从围裙口袋里摸出三枚赫勒，按进Rosa掌心。晚上洗手时，虎口那道口子被水一蜇，Rosa才看见。
  _effects: cash 0.03 (帮邻居晾床单挣的三赫勒); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-5; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-5_

- **1922.03** `vienna-childhood-street.market-errand/errand-bullied` tags: childhood, street, social, home
  市场拱廊下晒不到太阳，Rosa却被堵出一头汗。带子越挣越紧，肩膀在柱子上磕了一下。Maria Berg从人群里挤过来，先看了看Rosa的肩，又看了看柱子上的结，拿出小刀割断。两个大孩子骂了一句，Maria Berg把Rosa拉到菜篮后面。
  _effects: relation friend with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000151:child; mood -0.05; condition_

- **1923.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.market-line/line-half-flour` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, market, food
  面粉袋压在胸口，越抱越沉。Rosa前面的大婶一直往后靠，Rosa就把膝盖顶住对方的篮子，硬留出半步。买到半袋面粉后，Rosa的鞋跟留在人堆里，找回来时鞋面全是泥。回家路上布袋换了两次肩，赤脚踩过石板时脚底发麻。
  _effects: food; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-19; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-18_

- **1924.11** `vienna-youth-threshold.sibling-departure/coins-into-box` tags: adolescence, school, work, family, transition
  Franz Lechner的箱子摊在床板上，里面是两件衬衫、一双旧靴和母亲塞的一小袋面粉。Rosa把攒了半年的零钱数到第三遍，一共一克朗二十赫勒，全塞进箱角的袜子里。Franz Lechner看见了，没说话，只把箱子扣上，铜扣响了一声。
  _effects: cash -1.2 (把攒下的零钱塞给离家的兄姐); relation kin with person-franz-lechner; mood -0.03_

  Rosa把铁皮箱绑在自行车后座，骑了三十里到寄宿学校。舍监检查行李，把家里的腌菜罐没收，说会招老鼠。Rosa在熄灯后偷偷把罐子从垃圾堆捡回来，藏在床底，第一顿饭就着腌菜吃完了两碗粥。第一个星期Rosa把腌菜分给同屋，换回三张邮票和一支不漏水的钢笔。

- **1925.08** `vienna-adolescence.brothers-torn-jacket/sew-it-yourself` tags: adolescence, school, work, family, social
  Rosa在厨房找到一截粗线，颜色和外套差了一截。缝到一半，线打结，Rosa用牙咬断，重新来。Alois Lechner坐在对面，没问外套的事，只把汤碗推过来，说“先吃”。Rosa把最后一针打完，汤面已经结了一层薄皮。
  _effects: skill; food; relation kin with person-alois-lechner_

- **1926.01** `vienna-life-apprenticeship/kept-at-home` tags: adolescence, work, apprenticeship
