  _effects: mood 0.01999999999999999; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-23_

- **1926.11** `r13-turn.exam-failure::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement::reform-open-secondary/retake-fee` tags: education, exam, failure, school, work, reform, medical, schooling
  成绩单上差两分。Franz Lechner说可以补考，但要先交一克朗卷子费，还得让Alois Lechner来签字。Alois Lechner在办公室门口把帽子捏变了形，说家里拿不出这份钱。Rosa把错题抄到烟盒背面，第二天照样去上课，只是不再举手。
  _effects: cash -1 (落榜名单上的第三个名字); mood -0.15000000000000002; condition; flag +failed_entrance_exam; skill; relation guardian with person-alois-lechner_

- **1927.01** `kernel-edu.join-study-circle::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/edu-circle-argues` tags: task, adolescence, study, friendship, culture, reform, medical, schooling
  Rosa在讨论里顶了Franz Lechner一句，说工人要的不是别人替他们说话。屋里静了一下，Elisabeth Bauer在桌下踢了Rosa一脚。Franz Lechner没有生气，只让Rosa下周带材料来。回家的路上，Rosa还在想自己刚才那句话。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-franz-lechner; skill; flag +argued_in_study_circle; mood -0.02999999999999997_

- **1927.06** `r2-leisure.servant-trial::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/hired-after-trial` tags: domestic_service, servant, hiring, household, reference, reform, medical, schooling
  试用日的白围裙浆得发硬。Anna Moser 2让Rosa先擦餐厅的十二把椅子，再用手背试炉门温度；Die Fackel戴着戒指的手指在椅背上一划，举到窗边看。Rosa说上一家做了两年，Die Fackel问为什么离开，Anna Moser 2在门后轻轻咳了一声。
  _effects: cash 1 (试用日的白围裙); flag +hired_after_trial; role; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-55; knowledge “the mistress reads references closely and tests dust with a ”; employment Dienstmädchen @ cinema:person-luise-kral-evt:world-vienna-1912:002198; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-55; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-34_

- **1928.01** `p9-childhood-youth-aspiration.join-a-club/joined-and-borrowed` tags: adolescence, education, aspiration, school, books, club, exam, task
  Rosa跟着Hedwig走进印刷厂后面的屋子，交了十赫勒会费，在借书簿上按了手印。他挑了一本讲蒸汽机的册子，封面烫金已经磨掉；管理员说下月十赫勒要按时交，书才能续借。
  _effects: cash -0.1 (工人青年会月费); skill; relation friend with person-phase15-Hedwig-world-vienna-1912; flag +workers_youth_member; mood 0.03999999999999998_

- **1928.05** `yf18-dare.prater-slide::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/ride-in-the-dark` tags: dare, prater, fairground, comic, friendship, reform, medical, schooling
  普拉特的木滑道在闭园后还亮着一盏值班灯。Rosa和Mitzi从围栏底下钻进去，用一块麻袋垫着滑下来，木槽里的蜡味呛得人直笑。滑到底时，Luise Kral提着灯站在出口，问他们要不要再买一张票。
  _effects: mood 0.04999999999999999; cash -1 (a late ticket paid to the watchman); reputation -0.010000000000000002; flag +yf18_prater_stunt_

- **1928.11** `r14-edu.leaving-school::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/sent-to-country` tags: education, school_dropout, child_labour, poverty, reform, medical, schooling, home
  Rosa没有进厂，也没有留在家里，而是搭了去乡下的火车。姨妈家在葡萄园边上，缺人摘果子。Rosa把课本塞进箱底，白天干活，晚上在油灯下认几个字。秋天寄回家的信里，Rosa说这里的星星比城里多。车票两克朗，是姨妈先垫的。
  _effects: cash -2 (书包挂在门后的钉子上); flag +left_school_for_country; move place-klosterneuburg-aunt-house_

- **1929.01** `r13-civ.learn-instrument::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/self-taught` tags: culture, music, instrument, learning, craft, reform, medical, schooling
  Rosa没有钱请老师，就趴在窗边听隔壁的留声机，再把听到的调子按在琴弦上。手指磨出茧子，音还是不准，但她第一次拉完了一整支曲子。
  _effects: mood 0.030000000000000027; flag +learned_instrument_by_ear; skill_

- **1929.03** `r2-faith.parish-school-fee::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/expelled-for-arrears` tags: faith, school, charity, education, fees, reform, medical, schooling
  欠了兩個月学费，Franz Lechner把Rosa的座位调到最后一排，说书本先留下。Anna Weiss 2在门口拦住Rosa，说学校不是救济院。Rosa回家没有说，第二天照常出门，在火车站坐到放学时间。Alois Lechner后来在书包里发现了一张空白的退学单。
  _effects: mood -0.14; condition; flag +expelled_from_parish_school_for_arrears; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-39; knowledge “two months of unpaid fees can cost a child his seat”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-39_

- **1929.07** `yf18-friend.inkwell::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/name-the-friend` tags: exam, cheating, friendship, betrayal, school, reform, medical, schooling
  Rosa说Friedrich Winter把答案抄在墨水瓶底，还答应分给自己一半。Franz Lechner把Friedrich Winter的座位调到第一排；第二天，Rosa的课桌里多了一张纸条，上面只有一行字：“你以后别走夜路。”Rosa把纸条交给Franz Lechner，事情从此不再是两个人的事。
  _effects: reputation 0.02; flag +yf18_named_friend; mood -0.019999999999999962_

- **1929.09** `yf18-runaway.cousin-letter::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/answer-in-secret` tags: runaway, letter, cousin, work, plan, reform, medical, schooling
  格拉茨的表姐来信，说旅馆缺一个叠床单的女孩，管吃住，工钱少但能攒下。Rosa把信藏在算术本里，用铅笔在背面写回信；邮票是从Alois Lechner的抽屉里偷的，信封上不敢写自己的名字，只写了“内详”。
  _effects: state_add yf18-runaway-plan state:world-vienna-1912:132; cash -1 (a stolen stamp and writing paper); flag +yf18_cousin_reply; mood 0.030000000000000027; flag +yf18_runaway_plan_active_

- **1929.10** `r15-work.master-takes-on::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/unpaid-trial` tags: work, craft, apprentice, trade, poverty, reform, medical, schooling
  Luise Kral先说试三个月，不给工钱，只管一顿午饭。家里同意了，因为多一张嘴比多一双手更难。Rosa每天最早到，最晚走，三个月后师傅才把契约拿出来。
  _effects: food; mood -0.03999999999999998; state_add apprenticeship-contract state:world-vienna-1912:131; education apprenticeship; role_

- **1930.01** `oj18-perfume.apprentice::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/nose-returns` tags: perfume, apprentice, smell, shop, craft, work, odd_job, reform
  Rosa感冒了三天，鼻子像塞了棉花。小巷电影院把十二只小瓶排在柜台上，让Rosa闭眼闻；前十一只都答错了，最后一只，Rosa说出“佛手柑和一点旧木头”。小巷电影院把那只瓶子推到一边，说“记住这个味道，它比你的名字值钱”。
  _effects: state_add oj18-odd-apprenticeship state:world-vienna-1912:160; skill; reputation 0.03; flag +perfume_apprentice; knowledge “第十二只瓶子是佛手柑和旧木头，师傅说这个味道比名字值钱。”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-48; role; flag +odd_job_apprentice_

- **1930.01** `state_reaction:yf18-runaway-plan/a-family-question` tags: yf18-runaway-plan, escape, youth, plan, home, travel, risk, state
  饭桌上又为房租吵起来。Rosa把面包掰成很小的块，听见父亲说“这个家养不起闲人”；Rosa没有回嘴，只在心里把格拉茨的地址又背了一遍。
  _effects: state_parameter yf18-runaway-plan.suspicion 5 -> 13; state_parameter yf18-runaway-plan.resolve 60 -> 58_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:003655_

- **1930.01** `state_tick:apprenticeship-contract/workshop-year/learned` tags: apprenticeship-contract, education, craft, apprentice, work, state, state_tick
  Rosa又学会了一道工序。师傅没有夸，只把下一块料推到面前。
  _effects: state_parameter apprenticeship-contract.progress 8 -> 28; skill; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1930.01** `state_tick:yf18-runaway-plan/three-months-of-preparation/route-memorised` tags: yf18-runaway-plan, escape, youth, plan, home, travel, risk, state
  Rosa把去车站的路又走了一遍，记住了哪一段有煤气灯、哪一段住着巡夜的警察。回家后，Rosa在旧日历背面画了一条线，标出换乘的站名；那条线短得可笑，却比课本上的任何地图都清楚。
  _effects: state_parameter yf18-runaway-plan.preparation 45 -> 57; state_parameter yf18-runaway-plan.suspicion 13 -> 15; mood 0.010000000000000009_

- **1930.01** `p9-history-public-action.organize-relief/church-hall` tags: politics, union, party, activism, public_action, relief, leaflet, task
  教堂侧厅的长凳被挪到墙边，旧锅架在砖灶上，Otto Lechner 2 蹲着添柴，Rosa 把补好的账页贴在门板。第一锅汤滚起来时，排队的人从门口一直站到院里。
  _effects: cash -4 (垫付首日面粉和柴火); food; reputation 0.07; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-51; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-51_

- **1930.03** `major-education-night-technical-school/exhausted-dropout` tags: education, night_school, work, exhaustion, major
  白天在机器旁站十个小时，晚上再听两个钟头制图，Rosa在第三周开始打瞌睡。结业考试那天，Rosa把圆规掉在地上，捡起来时忘了题目。九克朗学费没有退，工头又记了一次迟到；第二个月，夜校的座位换成了别人。
  _effects: cash -9 (non-refundable evening-school fee); condition; flag +night_school_dropped_out; mood -0.06000000000000005_

- **1930.10** `vienna-housing-move.rent-notice/rent-notice-pay` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money
  Rosa把通知念给Anna Weiss 2听，Anna Weiss 2摇头说是楼梯间那几块新玻璃的账。Rosa去楼下杂货铺换开一张五克朗的纸币，回屋一枚一枚摆开数，煤箱底多留了二十赫勒。
  _effects: cash -2 (按月多付两克朗房租); food; mood -0.030000000000000027_

- **1930.11** `vienna-legacy-worklife.sick-fund/sick-fund.fill-form` tags: legacy, work, family, reform, ordinary_life
  表格有四栏，第四栏要写“工种及所属行会”。Rosa问工友平时怎么称呼自己的活，工友靠在机器旁边说“就是上油”。办事员的印章是木柄的，按下去时咔的一声。
  _effects: relation colleague with person-luise-kral; skill_

- **1931.01** `yf18-runaway.north-station::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/board-the-train` tags: runaway, station, train, papers, escape, reform, medical, schooling
  北站的钟指到十一点四十，Rosa把包袱塞进头顶的行李架，票根攥在手心。Josef Lechner在检票口翻看Rosa的工票，问去格拉茨做什么；Rosa说去看表姐，声音没有抖。汽笛响时，Rosa看见站台上没有一张熟悉的脸，忽然笑了一下。
  _effects: state_remove yf18-runaway-plan state:world-vienna-1912:132; move place-wien; cash -4 (the fare and a platform coffee); flag +yf18_left_vienna; mood 0.04999999999999999; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-2; flag -yf18_runaway_plan_active_

- **1931.01** `state_tick:apprenticeship-contract/workshop-year/mistake` tags: apprenticeship-contract, education, craft, apprentice, work, state, state_tick
  Rosa废掉了一块料，师傅扣了工钱。晚饭时谁也没提这件事。
  _effects: state_parameter apprenticeship-contract.failures 0 -> 1; cash -1 (wasted material); mood -0.03999999999999998_

- **1931.01** `state_tick:oj18-odd-apprenticeship/strange-year/blamed-for-the-noise` tags: oj18-odd-apprenticeship, work, odd_job, apprenticeship, night, trust, secrecy, state
  楼上住户投诉夜里有人拖椅子，管事的把巡查簿翻到Rosa的那一页，说签字太潦草，看不出巡过几趟。Rosa想辩解，却想起那晚确实在楼梯间多站了一刻钟；扣掉的工钱刚好够买一双不漏水的鞋。
  _effects: state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.trust 35 -> 27; state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.reliability 55 -> 50; mood -0.030000000000000027; flag +odd_job_scare_

- **1931.01** `task-vienna-activist.join-union/join-union-foreman-warning` tags: politics, activism, union, party, leaflet, meeting, task, task:politics
  入会的事第二天就传到车间。工头在过道里拦住他，说厂里不反对工人交朋友，但下个月的排班要看表现。他回到机器旁，发现自己的工具柜被挪到了最靠门的位置，冷风直往领口灌。
  _effects: role; mood -0.06000000000000005; flag +foreman_watching_union_activity_

- **1931.05** `vienna-women-housework.coal-stove/coal-stove.buy-coal` tags: gender, housework, home, labour, family
  炉门一开，黑烟先扑出来，Rosa眯着眼把纸团往里塞。火苗舔着碎煤慢慢亮起来，Karl Kraus隔着门喊当心一氧化碳。水壶开始咕噜作响，屋里才有点热乎气。
  _effects: cash -0.6 (购买一小筐碎煤); food_

- **1931.07** `bc18-ghost.death-certificate::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/collect-the-benefit` tags: bureaucracy, legal, death, identity, mistake, paperwork, absurd, reform
  邻居出主意说，死者家属可以领一笔丧葬补助。Rosa让Alois Weiss冒充远房表姐，自己在旁边按了手印；钱到手后，两人在厨房里分成三份，第三份留着买煤。第二个星期，Gustav Huber寄来一封信，说抚恤金已经核准，请家属来拍一张遗像；Rosa把信压在盐罐下面，盐受潮结了块。
  _effects: cash 4 (冒领的丧葬补助分成); state_add bc18-bureaucratic-ghost state:world-vienna-1912:187; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-14; condition; flag +collected_own_funeral_benefit; mood 0.010000000000000009; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-11; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-14; flag +bureaucratic_ghost; mood -0.010000000000000009_

- **1931.09** `vienna-housing-move.flat-viewing/flat-viewing-too-damp` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money
  Rosa在单间里站了一刻钟，脚底发潮，墙纸底下透出一股霉味。看门人说刷一层石灰就好，Friedrich Winter 2却在门口轻轻咳了一声，提醒这背面没有阳光。Rosa把定金收回口袋，谢过看门人，宁可多付三克朗，也不愿把湿气带进夜里。
  _effects: mood -0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-48; flag +still_looking_for_room_

- **1932.01** `vienna-life-depression/short-time` tags: adulthood, depression, work
  Rosa改成每周上三天班，工钱按日结算。家里的面包从每天一条变成隔天一条，Rosa开始把午饭省下来带回家。
  _effects: cash -3 (short-time work); condition_

- **1932.01** `oj18-perfume.recipe::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/recipe-stolen` tags: perfume, shop, recipe, theft, customer, work, odd_job, reform
  对街的店员Josef抄走了Rosa的配方：前调多放一滴橙花，后调少放半滴麝香，成本就能降一成。Rosa在市场上看见同样的瓶子，标签换了名字；店主没有去告，只在门口挂了一块小牌：本店不卖便宜的味道。
  _effects: cash -2 (lost sales to the copied scent); reputation -0.03; relation rival with person-phase15-Josef-world-vienna-1912; flag +recipe_stolen; knowledge “对街的瓶子用了我的配方，前调多一滴橙花，后调少半滴麝香。”_

- **1932.01** `p9-gap-adult-solitude.leaking-shoe/leaking-shoe.gave-up` tags: adulthood, daily, solitude, ordinary, home, repair, shoe, poverty
  Rosa缝了半个钟头，线老是从针眼里脱出来，最后把鞋往床底下一踢。第二天清早她穿着这双鞋站在厂门口等开门，湿石头地的凉气从裂缝里钻进来，脚趾冻得发麻，只能来回换脚。
  _effects: mood -0.040000000000000036_

  Rosa又学会了一道工序。师傅没有夸，只把下一块料推到面前。

  楼上住户投诉夜里有人拖椅子，管事的把巡查簿翻到Rosa的那一页，说签字太潦草，看不出巡过几趟。Rosa想辩解，却想起那晚确实在楼梯间多站了一刻钟；扣掉的工钱刚好够买一双不漏水的鞋。
  _effects: state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.trust 27 -> 19; state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.reliability 50 -> 45; mood -0.030000000000000027_

- **1932.01** `state_tick:bc18-bureaucratic-ghost/paper-trail-year/kept-the-files-apart` tags: bc18-bureaucratic-ghost, bureaucracy, identity, paperwork, death, mistake, legal, absurd
  这一年，Rosa学会了同时带三张纸出门：一张证明活着，一张证明死了，一张证明自己从没搬过家。窗口后面的人各自只看自己那一张，偶尔还会夸Rosa的材料齐全。年底，Rosa从补发的配给里省下一小袋面粉，觉得自己像个熟练的走私犯。
  _effects: state_parameter bc18-bureaucratic-ghost.fileDepth 35 -> 39; state_parameter bc18-bureaucratic-ghost.risk 40 -> 35; cash 1 (省下的配给和车钱); mood 0.010000000000000009_

- **1932.01** `p9-social-daily-culture.pictures-or-concert/p9-social-daily-culture.pictures-or-concert.box-seat` tags: daily, culture, leisure, cafe, music, walk, task, task:culture
  Rosa咬牙买了音乐厅楼座的票，两克朗五十赫勒，又向人借来一件硬领衬衫，领子磨得后颈发红。演奏到慢板时，Rosa手心里的汗把节目单边角浸软，却连咳嗽都憋着，生怕在周围体面人中间出声。散场后Otto Lechner 2等在门口，问值不值，Rosa只把节目单折好收进口袋。
  _effects: cash -2.5 (音乐厅楼座票价); mood 0.07999999999999996; reputation 0.020000000000000004; skill_

- **1932.04** `vienna-historical-social-movement.public-meeting/stay-and-tidy-chairs` tags: history, historical_process, social_movement, organization, politics
  Rosa整场没上台，散会后留下把折叠椅叠好，扫掉地上的瓜子壳。Alois Lechner帮着把借来的煤气灯还给店主，顺手记下谁走得最早。Wiener Psychoanalytische Vereinigung的人问Rosa为何不发言，Rosa说先把椅子还齐。
  _effects: skill; relation acquaintance with person-alois-lechner; mood -0.020000000000000018_

  _effects: mood -0.09999999999999998; flag +failed_journeyman_test; state_parameter apprenticeship-contract.failures 1 -> 2; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-36_

- **1933.01** `bc18-ghost.quiet-correction::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/new-ration-book` tags: bureaucracy, paperwork, identity, absurd, legal, quiet, comic, reform
  为了把死亡记录销掉，Friedrich Hofer在同一个日期上开了一张出生证明，说这样账目最干净。于是Rosa在官方文件里成了一个刚出生的婴儿，领到一本新配给簿和一张婴儿体检券。Rosa把体检券给了邻居的孩子，自己拿着配给簿去煤店；店员看了看年龄栏，又看了看Rosa的胡子，最后什么也没问。
  _effects: cash 2 (新生儿配给簿换到的煤和面粉); state_parameter bc18-bureaucratic-ghost.fileDepth 39 -> 47; state_parameter bc18-bureaucratic-ghost.risk 35 -> 45; state_parameter bc18-bureaucratic-ghost.duplicates 2 -> 3; state_parameter bc18-bureaucratic-ghost.officialStatus 被冒领的死者 -> 在更正后成为新生儿; condition; flag +officially_reborn; mood 0.030000000000000027_

  一个税务员把Rosa的死亡记录和一张新房租收据订在一起，寄到了另一个区。三个星期后，Rosa收到一封要求“已故纳税人”补缴暖气税的信；去问时，窗口说档案正在路上，让Rosa先等通知。通知没有来，房租却涨了。
  _effects: state_parameter bc18-bureaucratic-ghost.fileDepth 47 -> 54; state_parameter bc18-bureaucratic-ghost.risk 45 -> 55; cash -1 (跑窗口的车钱); flag +heating_tax_for_the_dead; mood -0.030000000000000027_

- **1933.01** `agency:attend_meeting` tags: agency, agency:attend_meeting
  Rosa去了Arbeiter-Zeitung im Vorwärts-Haus，坐在后排听完了关于维也纳社会民主党在工人街区、工会和报刊网络中持续活动，争取普选、劳动保护与社会救济的争论；散会时带走一张油印提纲。
  _effects: historical development:seed-vienna-social-democracy-1912 as participant; knowledge “我参加了维也纳社会民主党在工人街区、工会和报刊网络中持续活动，争取普选、劳动保护与社会救济。。”; mood 0.030000000000000027_

- **1933.02** `r3-mig.deportation-appeal::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/appeal-filed-on-last-day` tags: migration, deportation, law, appeal, papers, reform, medical, schooling
  上诉期的最后一天，Rosa把申诉书交到区公所。Friedrich Hofer看了一眼钟，说邮戳是昨天的，算数。驱逐令暂缓，等上面复核。Josef Lechner在门口等着，听见“暂缓”两个字，把已经掏出来的手铐又放回口袋。
  _effects: mood 0.040000000000000036; condition; flag +appeal_filed_on_last_day; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-2; knowledge “a postmark from the day before keeps the appeal alive”_

- **1933.07** `oj18-lift.stuck::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/hand-crank` tags: lift, building, work, night, passenger, odd_job, suspense, reform
  电梯停在四楼半，门缝里只看见一截电线。Rosa让Alois Berg抓紧扶手，自己摇动手柄，一格一格把轿厢放下来。门开时，Alois Berg的白大褂后背全湿了；他塞给Rosa两张钞票，说手术室里还有人等着。
  _effects: organization_found 环城大道写字楼管理处 (other); position 电梯员 @building:person-phase15-Rosa-world-vienna-1912-evt:world-vienna-1912:005046; state_add oj18-odd-apprenticeship state:world-vienna-1912:237; cash 2 (the doctor's reward); reputation 0.03; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-1; flag +lift_operator; knowledge “电梯停在四楼半时，手动柄要一格一格放，不能一次松到底。”_

- **1933.08** `mb17-border.permit-window::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/turned-back` tags: migration, border, papers, permit, station, official, belonging, reform
  Friedrich Hofer把材料推回来，说出生纸缺了教区印章。Rosa解释自己是从乡下赶来的，对方只指了指墙上的告示；排在后头的Alois Berg递来半块面包，两个人坐在候车室里等下一班回去的车，谁也没有先说话。
  _effects: cash -2 (the return ticket and a wasted day); mood -0.06000000000000005; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-1; flag +mb17_turned_back_at_border; knowledge “边境窗口只承认带教区印章的出生证明。”_

- **1934.01** `oj18-perfume.recipe::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-printshop/recipe-stolen` tags: perfume, shop, recipe, theft, customer, work, odd_job, vienna
  对街的店员Josef抄走了Rosa的配方：前调多放一滴橙花，后调少放半滴麝香，成本就能降一成。Rosa在市场上看见同样的瓶子，标签换了名字；店主没有去告，只在门口挂了一块小牌：本店不卖便宜的味道。
  _effects: cash -2 (lost sales to the copied scent); reputation -0.03; relation rival with person-phase15-Josef-world-vienna-1912; knowledge “对街的瓶子用了我的配方，前调多一滴橙花，后调少半滴麝香。”_

  院子里晾着三条床单，风一过就拍到人脸上。Rosa把备用钥匙和一张照相馆拍的合影交还，Luise Bauer把那件借来的外套叠好递回。两人说好各找各的住处，谁都没有提高声音；分手分得干净，可两个人心里都空了一块。
  _effects: relation_end partner with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-22; mood -0.08999999999999997; flag +courtship_ended_cleanly_

  _effects: cash -6 (被盗的买煤钱); relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-14; mood -0.009999999999999898_

  _effects: state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.strangeKnowledge 15 -> 25; knowledge “九层从一九三四年起封了，里面堆着被撤掉的人留下的纸箱和一只茶杯。”; condition; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-48; flag +sealed_floor; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-88_

  _effects: state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.trust 35 -> 27; state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.reliability 60 -> 55_

- **1938.10** `vienna-courtship-courtyard.family-table/wage-question-silence` tags: adulthood, courtship, partner, social, family, partnership
  Alois Lechner问起工钱和房租，Rosa报了个数，又补了一句冬天活少。桌上一时只剩刀叉声。Berta Bauer低着头把面包掰成小块，Alois Lechner往汤里又加了一勺盐，谁都没再开口；这顿饭把两个人的将来问成了一个没答完的数目。
  _effects: relation kin with person-alois-lechner; relation partner with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-100; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-100_

- **1939.01** `vienna-family-divorce/reconcile-for-now` tags: family, marriage, divorce, housing, lifecycle, partnership
  Rosa和Berta Bauer在楼道里吵到半夜，最后因为下个月的房租又坐回同一张桌。谁都没有提和好，只把账本重新摊开；Berta Bauer把煤钱推过来，Rosa接过去数了两遍，手指碰到一起又分开，屋里的灯亮到很晚。
  _effects: relation partner with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-100_

- **1939.01** `state_reaction:ld18-public-desire/a-wedding-invitation` tags: ld18-public-desire, desire, public, scandal, reputation, body, rumor, notoriety
  请柬上写着Rosa的名字，旁边用铅笔注了一句“一个人来也可以”。婚礼上，新娘把手捧花直接递给她，笑着说“下一个该你了”；同桌的人都在等Rosa怎么回答。她把花放在膝上，说这花很香。
  _effects: state_parameter ld18-public-desire.spectacle 42 -> 50; state_parameter ld18-public-desire.shame 44 -> 49_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:008359_

- **1939.01** `office16-scandal.public-hearing::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-printshop/weathered` tags: office, scandal, media, reputation, politics, power, vienna, urban
  报纸在头版登了Rosa和承包商在一张桌子上的照片，配文只有一句“谁签的字”。质询会开了三个小时，Rosa把合同、比价单和验收记录一页页摊开；记者第二天没有再写，但把照片留在了档案里。
  _effects: reputation -0.030000000000000006; state_add office-under-siege state:world-vienna-1912:421; flag +scandal_weathered; knowledge “照片是真的，合同也是真的；报纸没有再追，但档案里留了底片。”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-60; flag +office_under_siege_

- **1939.01** `state_reaction:ld18-public-desire/a-moral-society-letter` tags: ld18-public-desire, desire, public, scandal, reputation, body, rumor, notoriety
  道德协会寄来一封措辞客气的信，请Rosa去“谈谈自己的生活方式”。她把信读完，折好，垫在桌脚下；桌子还是晃。第二天，她照常去上班，只是把头发剪短了一截，短到所有人都能看出她不在乎。
  _effects: state_parameter ld18-public-desire.protection 5 -> 0; state_parameter ld18-public-desire.defiance 5 -> 13_

- **1939.01** `state_tick:ld18-public-desire/the-story-changes-hands/a-new-version` tags: ld18-public-desire, desire, public, scandal, reputation, body, rumor, notoriety
  故事又换了一个版本：有人说Rosa是为了钱，有人说是为了爱，还有人说她只是喜欢看别人议论。Rosa在咖啡馆听见第三种说法，没有更正，只把杯子往窗边挪了挪；阳光照进来，她第一次觉得这些版本都离自己很远。
  _effects: state_parameter ld18-public-desire.rumorHeat 44 -> 40; state_parameter ld18-public-desire.spectacle 50 -> 52; mood 0.010000000000000009_

- **1939.03** `p9-social-tenement-neighbors.stairwell-rumor/stairwell-rumor.warn-the-latch` tags: housing, neighbors, tenement, daily, money, reputation
  Rosa没传话，只在二楼门口站了一会儿，提醒Alois Berg夜里把门闩好，说楼梯间有人爱嚼舌头。Alois Berg点点头，把门闩推过去，发出很轻的一声。此后送活的人还是照来，闲话却渐渐没人接。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-1; mood 0.030000000000000027; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-95_

  审计员在顾问的考勤卡上看见连续四十个“外勤”，而外勤地点写的是同一家咖啡馆。Rosa把考勤卡收进抽屉，补了一张“社区联络”的说明；Alois Lechner的工资没有停，但办公室门口多了一张审计通知。
  _effects: reputation -0.06; state_add office-under-siege state:world-vienna-1912:422; flag +sinecure_audited; knowledge “考勤卡上的四十次外勤都指向同一家咖啡馆。”; flag -office_under_siege; flag +office_under_siege_

- **1940.01** `oj18-watch.second-job::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/double-shift-holds` tags: work, night, watchman, office, scandal, double_life, suspense, vienna
  白天在部里盖章，夜里去仓库守门，Rosa把两套制服挂在同一只衣柜里。Luise Hofer在走廊里闻见煤油味，只问了一句“家里还好吧”，就把一叠待签的文件推过来。Rosa签完字，赶最后一班电车去换岗。
  _effects: state_add oj18-odd-apprenticeship state:world-vienna-1912:445; cash 2 (second-job wages); skill; state_parameter office-under-siege.exposure 45 -> 50; flag +double_job; knowledge “我把两套制服挂在同一只衣柜里，煤油味和印泥味混在一起。”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-91; role; flag +odd_job_apprentice_

  _effects: state_parameter ld18-public-desire.rumorHeat 40 -> 52; state_parameter ld18-public-desire.protection 0 -> 0; cash -2 (the first payment to a blackmailer)_

  _effects: state_parameter office-under-siege.support 40 -> 45; state_parameter office-under-siege.exposure 50 -> 46; state_parameter office-under-siege.months 0 -> 1; mood 0.010000000000000009_

- **1940.02** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.shared_stove_fire/fire_burns_curtain` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Rosa在公用灶台上热猪油，油星子溅到旁边晾着的床单上，火苗顺着床单爬上木隔板。Alois Berg拎起水桶泼过去，火灭了，但床单烧掉半幅，隔板熏黑一片。房东扣了那户人家两克朗押金，那家的女人在楼道里哭了一下午。
  _effects: cash -2 (赔给房东的隔板熏黑损失); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-1_

- **1940.09** `vienna-market-household.soup-kitchen/soup-kitchen.sell` tags: home, market, food, social
  鞋匠敲后门问有没有热汤，Rosa把剩下的半锅倒进他的罐子里，收下两个十字币。Katharina Berg提着空碗上来时，只看见Rosa把硬币塞进围裙口袋。
  _effects: cash 2 (卖出半锅扁豆汤); food; relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000823:child_

- **1940.11** `vienna-medical-treatment.night-fever/corner-doctor` tags: health, medical, family, money
  施特劳斯医生用酒精棉擦手，把药粉倒进小纸袋，折口时指甲掐出印子。他说夜里别再喝醋水，那东西烧胃。Friedrich Hofer把炉火拨旺，在灶上坐了一壶水，答应第二天替Rosa去铺子里告假。
  _effects: cash -4 (医生出诊费和药费); health 0.05 (药粉和糖浆缓解症状); relation friend with person-friedrich-hofer_

- **1941.01** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.laundry_line_dispute/neighbor_yanks_clothes` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Alois Berg嫌Rosa的床单滴水滴到Alois Berg家衬衫上，一把扯下床单扔在院子的石板上。Rosa下楼捡起来时，床单角沾了泥，重洗又要费肥皂。两人在院子当中对喊，楼上有人推开窗看热闹。Rosa把床单重新搭上绳子时，故意把水拧在Alois Berg那侧。
  _effects: relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-1; cash -0.15 (重洗床单的肥皂钱)_

  名单上添的是Mitzi Weiss，一个连公文格式都分不清的远房亲戚。Luise Hofer把考勤表放在桌上，没有问原因；Rosa也没有解释，只在签字栏里写得比平时慢。第一个月，Mitzi Weiss迟到了七次，工资照发。

  Rosa为环城大道写字楼管理处在Wien经过数年的招募、会议和互助，把分散的行动整理成公开纲领；这不出了力：整理名单、传递消息或替同伴作证，名字被记进参与记录。
  _effects: historical development:initiative-building:person-phase15-Rosa-world-vienna-1912-evt:world-vienna-1912:005046-1935 as supporter; historical_influence; reputation 0.014999999999999986_

- **1941.02** `ml18-luck.strongbox-in-the-wall::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-factory/the-inflation-ate-them` tags: money, luck, housing, windfall, inflation, ownership, vienna, urban
  铁盒里的纸币已经作废，银扣也只值几个铜板。Josef把纸币一张张摊在窗台上，说战前的纸现在只能糊墙；Berta Bauer笑得直不起腰，说这盒子在他家墙里藏了二十年，原来是一堆废纸。Rosa把当票收好，去当铺换回一只银勺，勺柄上刻着外祖母的姓。
  _effects: cash 2 (the silver spoon redeemed with the old pawn ticket); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-100; flag +worthless_strongbox; knowledge “墙里的旧纸币已经作废，当票还能换回一只银勺。”; mood -0.020000000000000018_

- **1941.06** `vienna-women-housework.coal-stove/coal-stove.borrow-fire` tags: gender, housework, home, labour, family
  Rosa端着一只旧锅去敲Otto Berg 2的门，想借几块烧红的炭。Otto Berg 2让她从自家炉膛里夹了一块，又抓了一小把引火的木片。Rosa连声道谢，回来把炭放进炉栅，火是有了，屋里却还是冷得能呵出白气。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-96; food; mood 0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-96_

  _effects: reputation -0.010000000000000009; mood -0.020000000000000018; flag +deferred_maintenance; knowledge “屋顶的修理费被挪去补煤款，煤款又被挪去付印刷费。”_

  _effects: food; skill; relation acquaintance with person-josef-bauer; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1942.12** `p9-social-family-conflict-care.sick-parent-night/hospital-deposit` tags: family, care, siblings, inheritance, housing, money

  电梯停在四楼半，门缝里只看见一截电线。Rosa让Gustav Kral抓紧扶手，自己摇动手柄，一格一格把轿厢放下来。门开时，Gustav Kral的白大褂后背全湿了；他塞给Rosa两张钞票，说手术室里还有人等着。

  _effects: organization_funds; reputation -0.06; state_add office-under-siege state:world-vienna-1912:612; knowledge “考勤卡上的四十次外勤都指向同一家咖啡馆。”; mood -0.050000000000000044; flag +office_under_siege_

  _effects: skill; relation colleague with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000828:child_

- **1944.11** `df18-dream.water-in-the-cellar::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-factory/tell-the-landlord` tags: dream, omen, home, water, coincidence, eerie, everyday, vienna
  Rosa把梦告诉房东。房东笑了一声，说老房子下水慢，做梦都带水汽。第二天，房东带人来查，真在墙里找到一条裂缝，顺手把地窖的租金减了一克朗。Rosa把省下的钱买了一盏新灯，放在楼梯口。
  _effects: cash 1 (房东减掉的一克朗地窖租金); reputation 0.020000000000000004; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-100; knowledge “房东因为我的梦查了墙，减了地窖租金。”; flag +df18_landlord_fixed_crack; mood 0.020000000000000018_

  _effects: role; food; cash -2 (replacement documents)_

  Rosa和Rosa Weiss按原计划沿葡萄园间的土路往上走，鞋子沾满黄泥，到山顶时风把汗湿的衬衫吹得冰凉。下山在第一个酒棚坐下，两人合点了一壶新酒和一块面包，一共一克朗二十赫勒；Rosa喝到第二杯时脸颊发烫，把剩下的半块面包包进手帕带回家，第二天腿酸，却睡得很沉。
  _effects: cash -1.2 (卡伦山酒棚的新酒和面包); food; health 0.019999999999999997 (星期天登山远足); relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-64; mood 0.06000000000000005; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-64_

  Rosa把五克朗夹在护照第七页，Maria Berg 2翻开时手指停了一下，随即把护照合上，说“走吧”。走出十步，Rosa回头看见他正把钞票塞进袖口，另一只手在笔记本上写名字。那本子已经写了大半页。
  _effects: cash -5 (夹在护照里的那张钞票); condition; flag +paid_customs_bribe; knowledge “the customs officer records bribe payers in a notebook”_

  _effects: cash 1 (新会员的入会费先记在桌上); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-84; skill_

- **1947.06** `major-crisis.press-accident/hand-crushed` tags: crisis, work, accident, disability, death, printshop, health, unemployment

  _effects: cash 2 (爱国债券的第六期); flag +war_bonds_redeemed_at_fraction; role; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-88; relation colleague with person-berta-weiss; knowledge “war bonds were redeemed at a fraction of face value”_

  历史进程《环城大道写字楼管理处在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang经过数年的招募、会议和互助，把分散的行动整理成公开纲领；》从 emerging 进入 mobilizing。
  _effects: knowledge “我亲历或知晓「环城大道写字楼管理处在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang经”; knowledge “我亲历或知晓「环城大道写字楼管理处在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang经”_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:015195_

- **1949.03** `vienna-medical-treatment.toothache/barber-pull` tags: health, medical, family, money
  理发铺里还坐着等刮脸的老主顾，肥皂味和烟味混在一起。弗朗茨把围布往Rosa胸前一系，说忍着点。钳子第一次滑脱，第二次才夹稳，牙出来时带着一小块肉，他用明矾水按住了。
  _effects: cash -1.2 (理发师拔牙收费); health 0.03 (病牙被拔除但手法粗糙)_

- **1949.07** `r13-rel.caring-for-sick-partner::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement::caring-for-sick-partner-war/sent-to-the-country` tags: relationship, marriage, care, illness, obligation, vienna, urban, postwar
  医生说城里的空气不适合养伤，Rosa把Berta Bauer送到乡下的亲戚家。Berta Bauer走后，Rosa每周坐火车去看，带着家里的汤和干净的布。有一次火车误点，Rosa在站台上坐到半夜。
  _effects: cash -8 (夜里的第三遍水); role; relation partner with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-100_

- **1950.01** `curse15-betrayal.signature::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/the-seal-was-used` tags: betrayal, office, power, politics, curse, institution, vienna, urban
  Rosa Berg用Rosa留在抽屉里的印章签了一份采购单，把库房里最后一批煤卖给了城外的砖厂。Rosa发现时，账已经做平，签字栏里是Rosa自己的名字。Rosa Berg说只是替Rosa省了麻烦，还把那支用旧的印泥放回了原处。
  _effects: cash -3 (为平账先垫出去的钱); reputation -0.09999999999999998; relation rival with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000953:child; condition; flag +seal_used_by_trusted_hand_

- **1950.01** `agency:support_process` tags: agency, agency:support_process
  Rosa想帮忙，但组织者不信任新来的人，只让其在外围等消息。
  _effects: mood -0.020000000000000018_

- **1950.04** `vienna-legacy-worklife.hours-ledger/hours-ledger.sign` tags: legacy, work, family, reform, ordinary_life
  老工人凑近看那张表，说这纸比工资袋还薄。Rosa把拇指蘸进印泥，按下去时听见工头说了句“下一个”。学徒在旁边数少掉的铜板，数到四十赫勒时不出声了。
  _effects: cash -1 (星期六下午减少的计件工钱); mood 0.050000000000000044_

- **1950.04** `vienna-parish-aid.rent-box/rent-box.accept-all` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  煤袋落在台阶上，扬起细细一层黑末。Josef Berg 3用铅笔头在蓝格册上写门牌号，问房东姓什么，又叮嘱别把面包票弄丢。灶上水壶开始叫，Rosa才想起屋里连只干净的杯子都没有。
  _effects: cash 6 (互助会补上本月房租差额); food; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-84_

- **1950.12** `bc18-crime.inept-blackmail::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/pay-hush-money` tags: crime, blackmail, photograph, mistake, humiliation, absurd, reputation, vienna
  Rosa先给了两克朗，Ferdinand Hofer 2又要四克朗；第三次来收钱时，Rosa把他带到了警察局。警员在Ferdinand Hofer 2的口袋里找到三张别人的照片，其中一张是市议员在酒馆后门。Rosa拿回自己的照片，也花掉了一个下午，回家时晚饭已经凉了。
  _effects: cash -4 (付给勒索者的封口钱); reputation -0.020000000000000018; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-119; condition; flag +paid_then_reported_blackmail; mood -0.030000000000000027_

- **1951.01** `state_reaction:mb17-remittance-web/family-emergency` tags: mb17-remittance-web, migration, remittance, household, obligation, money, diaspora, state
  故乡来了一封电报，说屋顶塌了一角，老人暂时住到邻居家。Rosa当晚就去邮局，把本来留着交学费的钱先汇了出去；回家后，他把墙上的缴费单取下来，折好放进抽屉。
  _effects: state_parameter mb17-remittance-web.obligation 64 -> 74; state_parameter mb17-remittance-web.arrears 3 -> 4; cash -3 (an emergency money order sent home)_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:015882_

- **1951.01** `office16-scandal.public-hearing::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/weathered` tags: office, scandal, media, reputation, politics, power, vienna, urban
  报纸在头版登了Rosa和承包商在一张桌子上的照片，配文只有一句“谁签的字”。质询会开了三个小时，Rosa把合同、比价单和验收记录一页页摊开；记者第二天没有再写，但把照片留在了档案里。
  _effects: reputation -0.030000000000000027; state_add office-under-siege state:world-vienna-1912:832; knowledge “照片是真的，合同也是真的；报纸没有再追，但档案里留了底片。”; flag +office_under_siege_

  报纸的社会版把Rosa家的门牌号和那笔钱的数目印在一起，标题是“一笔不该有的好运”。第二天门口多了两个陌生人，一个说是远亲，一个说代表一家孤儿院。
  _effects: state_parameter ml18-sudden-fortune.public false -> true; state_parameter ml18-sudden-fortune.claims 1 -> 3; reputation -0.020000000000000018; flag +windfall_went_public_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:016096_

- **1951.01** `state_tick:raising-child/school-year/learns` tags: raising-child, family, child, care, school, household, state, state_tick
  Rosa把Anton 2的旧课本包上书皮，又在本子上签了名。Anton 2今年学会了自己系鞋带，也学会了在饭桌上说“不”；Rosa听着，觉得屋子比去年更满。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 55 -> 63; state_parameter raising-child.caregiverBurden 62 -> 59; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1951.01** `state_tick:ml18-sudden-fortune/fortune-year/money-slips-away` tags: ml18-sudden-fortune, money, windfall, sudden_fortune, household, luck, inheritance, lottery
  钱在一年里薄了一层：屋顶要修，姐夫来借，彩票站的老主顾说有个稳赚的买卖。Rosa把账本翻到最后一页，发现写下的支出比记得的多出四克朗；窗外有人在敲铁皮，声音像在催账。
  _effects: state_parameter ml18-sudden-fortune.principal 10 -> 5; state_parameter ml18-sudden-fortune.spent 0 -> 5; cash -1 (the account does not balance); mood -0.020000000000000018_

  厂里补发了一笔拖欠的工钱。Rosa没有先还杂货铺的账，而是去邮局多填了一张汇款单；回来的路上，他给自己买了一支最便宜的烟，站在桥上看了一会儿水，才把剩下的钱数清楚。
  _effects: cash 1 (the factory paid part of the arrears); state_parameter mb17-remittance-web.supportSent 7 -> 8; state_parameter mb17-remittance-web.obligation 74 -> 72; mood 0.010000000000000009_

  老师托人带来一张纸条，说Anton 2这周主动擦了黑板。Rosa把纸条压在桌玻璃下面；那张玻璃下面已经有三张纸条了。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 63 -> 66; mood 0.020000000000000018_

  边境又收紧了，往故乡的列车要换乘三次。邮局说汇款还能走，但信可能压在路上；Rosa还是把两张单子一起递进窗口，像把一句话交给一个不保证送达的人。
  _effects: state_parameter mb17-remittance-web.arrears 4 -> 6; flag +mb17_border_slow_letters_

  Rosa在楼梯下面接到一张折成四折的传单。纸上没有署名，只写着下一次集会的地址。Rosa去了，在昏暗的屋里报上姓名，成为圣马克斯殡葬合作社的一员，并答应负责自己所在街区的联络。
  _effects: organization_membership; role; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-121; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-121_

- **1951.08** `state_reaction:ml18-sudden-fortune/a-relative-remembers` tags: ml18-sudden-fortune, money, windfall, sudden_fortune, household, luck, inheritance, lottery
  一个多年没走动的亲戚提着一盒点心上门，先问Rosa的身体，再问那笔钱是怎么来的。临走时他说起小时候替Rosa家垫过一冬天的煤钱，数字记不清了，但意思很清楚。
  _effects: state_parameter ml18-sudden-fortune.claims 0 -> 1; flag +windfall_claim_by_relative_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:015906_

  Rosa没有拆穿，跟着队伍走完了全程。Ferdinand Moser 2把每一座教堂都说成“全城最古老”，把每一条巷子都说成“莫扎特走过”。Katharina Winter低声说，这条巷子昨天还叫另一个名字。Rosa把指南合上，说反正雨这么大，有屋顶就行。
  _effects: flag +followed_fake_tour; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-143; knowledge “the guide changes street names to fit the tour”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-143_

- **1951.09** `r13-rel.missing-partner::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement::missing-partner-postwar/found-a-witness` tags: relationship, war, missing, grief, institution, vienna, urban, postwar
  一个从同一战俘营回来的人找到Rosa，说最后见Berta Bauer是在转运站，之后就不知道了。Rosa请对方坐下，把每一句话都记下来，连说话时的停顿也记。那张纸后来被Rosa折成四折，放在贴身的口袋里。
  _effects: role; relation partner with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-100; knowledge “a returned prisoner last saw the partner at a transit camp”_

- **1952.01** `vienna-life-injury-recovery/home-care` tags: health, work, family
  Rosa没有去诊所，只用热水和旧布洗了伤口。第二天伤口周围发红，家里人把仅剩的干净床单撕成条。
  _effects: cash -2 (clean cloth); condition_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:015438_

  Rosa把一份扩建许可证压在抽屉最下面，理由是排水和消防通道不合规。申请人Emil Lechner 3在走廊里等到下班，最后只拿到一张写着“补件”的纸条。Rosa知道这份许可明年还会再来，但今年不会从自己的桌上出去。
  _effects: reputation 0.020000000000000018; skill; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-150; flag +blocked_permit; knowledge “扩建申请的排水图与消防通道不符合现行规定。”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-150_

  Rosa把工钱摊在桌上，先数出房租和煤钱，再把剩下的换成一张汇款单。邮局职员在单子上敲了章，说“两周能到”；回家的路上，他买了一张最便宜的明信片，写了两行字又划掉一行。

  婚礼在借来的房间里举行，证人是洗衣房的Helene Bauer。戒指是从当铺买的，誓词写在洗衣单背面；Helene Bauer念到“无论贫穷还是疾病”时，楼下有人敲门，三个人同时屏住呼吸。门开了，是来收煤气费的。
  _effects: state_add ld18-impossible-attachment state:world-vienna-1912:869; cash -1 (the pawnshop rings and the gas bill); mood 0.06000000000000005; flag +private_wedding_rite; flag +impossible_attachment_active_

  介绍所的墙上钉着上百块木牌，大半翻过去露出灰白的背面。Helene Bauer 2收了一克朗，从抽屉里摸出纸条，墨水还没干透。第二天Rosa站在工厂门口，门房说清扫工昨天就齐了。
  _effects: cash -1 (职业介绍所登记费); mood -0.030000000000000027_

  Helene Huber 3说证据不足，建议双方回去冷静。Berta Bauer在走廊里问Rosa要不要一起回家；Rosa说再想想。那天晚上，Rosa回到Elisabeth Bauer的住处，发现门缝里塞着一封信，只有一句：“我不催你。”
  _effects: relation partner with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-100; state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.pressure 15 -> 30; state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.discretion 55 -> 45; mood -0.040000000000000036; flag +divorce_dismissed; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-161_

  亲戚的婚礼上，Rosa被安排和{link:beloved}分开坐。司仪问有没有人反对，Rosa低头喝汤；{link:beloved}在另一桌替别人倒酒，手很稳，酒没有洒出来。散席时，两个人在衣帽间碰见，只说了一句“外面下雨”。
  _effects: state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.pressure 30 -> 40; state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.grief 0 -> 4_

  户籍窗口换了一张新表格，配偶一栏不能空着。Rosa最后写了“同住人”，办事员抬头看了一眼，说这不作数。回家后，Rosa把表格折成很小的一块，塞进针线盒；{link:beloved}问今天顺不顺利，Rosa说和平时一样。
  _effects: state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.pressure 48 -> 54; state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.discretion 41 -> 38_

- **1953.02** `p9-social-tenement-neighbors.gramophone-wall/gramophone-wall.cotton-in-ears` tags: housing, neighbors, tenement, daily, money, reputation
  Rosa忍到天亮，第二天去药房买了五分钱的棉花，撕成两小团塞在耳里。夜里的进行曲还剩一点闷响，翻个身也就过去了。此后在楼梯间遇见Mitzi Winter只是点头，再没有进过那家的门。
  _effects: cash -0.05 (在药房买棉花塞耳朵); relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:001365:child_

- **1953.03** `state_reaction:mb17-remittance-web/family-emergency` tags: mb17-remittance-web, migration, remittance, household, obligation, money, diaspora, state
  故乡来了一封电报，说屋顶塌了一角，老人暂时住到邻居家。Rosa当晚就去邮局，把本来留着交学费的钱先汇了出去；回家后，他把墙上的缴费单取下来，折好放进抽屉。
  _effects: state_parameter mb17-remittance-web.obligation 69 -> 79; state_parameter mb17-remittance-web.arrears 5 -> 6; cash -3 (an emergency money order sent home)_

- **1953.05** `r3-mig.residency-registration::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement::reform-new-register/landlady-refused-signature` tags: migration, registration, housing, papers, bureaucracy, vienna, urban, postwar
  Gustav Winter把登记表推过来，要房东签字证明Rosa住在那里。Anna Weiss 4在窗口外听见，立刻说自己只是合租，不能替人担责。Gustav Winter把表收回，在户籍卡上盖了“地址不明”。Rosa当晚回去，门锁已经换了，房东隔着门说登记的事找别处。
  _effects: condition; flag +registration_refused_by_landlady; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-108; knowledge “a landlord’s signature is required for registration”_

- **1953.07** `vienna-housing-move.moving-day/moving-day-sell-up` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money
  收破烂的把两克朗数在门槛上，顺手敲了敲桌板看木料。Rosa把铁盒扣好，那里面只有照片和几封旧信。Hermann Lechner替Rosa扶着门，说旧床架值不了几个钱，能脱手就不亏。
  _effects: cash 2 (卖掉旧床架和桌子收进的钱); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-25; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-25_

- **1954.01** `ld18-future.two-rooms::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/two-rooms-one-wall` tags: love, future, unregistered, commitment, ordinary, queer, secret_intimacy, vienna
  Rosa和Berta Bauer在同一个院子里租下两间相邻的小房，中间只隔一道薄墙。晚上收工后，一个人敲三下，另一个就敲两下；谁也没有提婚书，但每个月的房租都各付一半，煤也买双份。
  _effects: state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.devotion 70 -> 80; state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.improvisation 60 -> 75; state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.pressure 54 -> 49; relation partner with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-100; cash -2 (two rents and double coal); mood 0.050000000000000044; flag +two_rooms_one_wall_

- **1954.01** `state_tick:mb17-remittance-web/remittance-year/posted-in-time` tags: mb17-remittance-web, migration, remittance, household, obligation, money, diaspora, state
  Rosa把工钱摊在桌上，先数出房租和煤钱，再把剩下的换成一张汇款单。邮局职员在单子上敲了章，说“两周能到”；回家的路上，他买了一张最便宜的明信片，写了两行字又划掉一行。
  _effects: cash -2 (the money order and postage); state_parameter mb17-remittance-web.supportSent 10 -> 12; state_parameter mb17-remittance-web.arrears 7 -> 6; state_parameter mb17-remittance-web.obligation 89 -> 86; mood 0.010000000000000009_

  户籍窗口换了一张新表格，配偶一栏不能空着。Rosa最后写了“同住人”，办事员抬头看了一眼，说这不作数。回家后，Rosa把表格折成很小的一块，塞进针线盒；{link:beloved}问今天顺不顺利，Rosa说和平时一样。
  _effects: state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.pressure 49 -> 55; state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.discretion 38 -> 35; mood -0.010000000000000009_

- **1954.06** `r1-cul.zoo::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/saw-the-pacing-wolf` tags: culture, zoo, animals, family, captivity, vienna, urban, postwar
  Rosa在狼笼前看见一只狼来回走同一条线，爪印把泥地磨出一道沟。Berta Huber 3说它关了十二年；Mitzi Lechner问为什么不放，Berta Huber 3说放出去也活不了。回家后Rosa梦见那道沟，第二天去图书馆查了一下午狼的产地。
  _effects: mood -0.050000000000000044; condition; flag +saw_stereotypic_wolf; role; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-137; knowledge “the wolf has paced the same line for twelve years”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-137_

  _effects: role; relation rival with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:002255:child; flag +reference_letter_stolen; knowledge “朋友拿着偷来的推荐信，写了自己的名字。”_

- **1954.10** `vienna-medical-treatment.toothache/drops-and-endure` tags: health, medical, family, money
  夜里疼醒时，Rosa摸黑往棉球上又滴药水，手抖得洒在枕头上。Anna Weiss 4第二天一早来敲门，看见肿脸就说别硬撑了，可Rosa只是摆摆手，说再熬一天也许就好了。
  _effects: cash -0.6 (牙痛药水); condition_

- **1955.01** `curse15-betrayal.signature::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/the-files-went-first` tags: betrayal, office, power, politics, curse, institution, vienna, urban
  Grete 2先把Rosa抽屉里的往来信件搬空，再在会议上说Rosa从来没有保存过记录。没有人能证明那些信存在过，除了Rosa自己。散会后，有人让Rosa回家休息几天，工资照发。Rosa知道这不是照顾，是让Rosa别再回来。
  _effects: reputation -0.06; relation rival with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:004382:child; knowledge “我经手的信件在会议前被人搬空了。”; condition; flag +records_removed_

- **1955.01** `state_tick:ld18-impossible-attachment/invented-anniversary/the-date-that-is-not-on-paper` tags: ld18-impossible-attachment, love, intimacy, queer, unregistered, commitment, secret, desire
  Rosa和{link:beloved}把认识的那一天当作纪念日。没有登记，没有证人，只有一块从旧货摊买来的蛋糕和两杯兑了水的酒；Rosa在日历上画了一个别人看不懂的记号，第二年又画在同一个格子里。
  _effects: state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.devotion 80 -> 84; state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.pressure 55 -> 52; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1955.01** `agency:support_process` tags: agency, agency:support_process
  Rosa想帮忙，但组织者不信任新来的人，只让其在外围等消息。
  _effects: mood -0.040000000000000036_

  厂里补发了一笔拖欠的工钱。Rosa没有先还杂货铺的账，而是去邮局多填了一张汇款单；回来的路上，他给自己买了一支最便宜的烟，站在桥上看了一会儿水，才把剩下的钱数清楚。
  _effects: cash 1 (the factory paid part of the arrears); state_parameter mb17-remittance-web.supportSent 12 -> 13; state_parameter mb17-remittance-web.obligation 86 -> 84; mood 0.010000000000000009_

  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-170; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-170_

  边境又收紧了，往故乡的列车要换乘三次。邮局说汇款还能走，但信可能压在路上；Rosa还是把两张单子一起递进窗口，像把一句话交给一个不保证送达的人。
  _effects: state_parameter mb17-remittance-web.arrears 6 -> 8_

  会议室里有人念了一份动议，说Rosa把预算当成人情。Emil把签名页传了一圈，最后停在自己手里：支持票比反对票少三张。Rosa没有发言，只把下一季度的支出表放在桌上，表上每一笔都有收据。
  _effects: reputation 0.03999999999999998; relation rival with person-phase15-Emil-world-vienna-1912; knowledge “动议只拿到少数支持，三名原本签名的委员在投票前撤回了。”; mood 0.030000000000000027; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-155_

- **1955.09** `vienna-housing-move.rent-notice/rent-notice-neighbors-together` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money
  Rosa把Katharina Berg和楼里另两户人家叫到楼道，把那张纸摆在窗台上传看。看门人在楼梯拐角听着，说房东礼拜三来收钱。几家人合计好，到那天一起等在门厅，先把克朗递过去，再把修补的事一件件摆出来。Rosa把纸折进围裙口袋，觉得人凑齐了话也硬气些。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000823:child; mood 0.040000000000000036; flag +tenant_collective_

  Otto Huber在门口堵了三天，第四天把Rosa的辞职信全文登了出来。信只有四行，最后一行是“为避免机构继续为我的判断付出代价”。Rosa把办公室的钥匙交给Josef Winter 2，没有带走任何文件。

  办公室有人问Rosa，为什么总有一个女人在楼下等她。Rosa说那是表妹；对方笑了一下，说现在的表妹真体贴。下午，Rosa把桌上的相框扣倒，又在没人时翻回来，用袖子擦了擦玻璃上的灰。
  _effects: state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.pressure 52 -> 60; state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.discretion 35 -> 31; reputation -0.020000000000000018_

  铅笔头短得捏不住，Emil把它夹在两指之间，在表格背面写下两个名字。被漏掉的人一个叫扬，一个叫弗兰茨，都是每天清早从火车站走来的临时工。工头看了一眼背面，没说话，把表贴回墙上。
  _effects: skill; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-2; reputation 0.04_

- **1936.01** `state_tick:wish-love/love-pressure/press-on` tags: wish-love, wish, wish:love, love, family, intimacy, state, state_tick
  Emil又把那件没实现的事翻出来想了一遍；这一次没有告诉任何人。
  _effects: mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1936.01** `r13-fam.seek-friend::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement::seek-friend-war/raid-friend` tags: task, social, friendship, loneliness, belonging, vienna, urban, crisis
  Gustav Kral在防空洞口分给Emil半支烟，两人背靠潮湿的砖墙，听远处炮声。Emil说自己怕的不是炮，是回家后没人说话；Gustav Kral把烟头按灭，说那就每天这个时辰在这儿等。
  _effects: condition; flag +air_raid_friend; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-74; knowledge “the cellar entrance is quiet after the all-clear”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-74_

- **1936.08** `vienna-market-household.market-credit/market-credit.delay` tags: home, market, food, social
  三个克朗拖了九天。玛丽亚收下时数都没数，只把Emil的名字从木牌上抹去，粉笔灰落在卷心菜上。Luise Berg 4拎着篮子从旁边过去，没停脚。
  _effects: cash -3 (延迟偿还市场赊账（其中3克朗记入债务）); food; reputation -0.05; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-92; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-92_

- **1936.10** `vienna-medical-treatment.night-fever/corner-doctor` tags: health, medical, family, money
  施特劳斯医生用酒精棉擦手，把药粉倒进小纸袋，折口时指甲掐出印子。他说夜里别再喝醋水，那东西烧胃。Alois Weiss把炉火拨旺，在灶上坐了一壶水，答应第二天替Emil去铺子里告假。
  _effects: cash -4 (医生出诊费和药费（其中4克朗记入债务）); health 0.05 (药粉和糖浆缓解症状); relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-14_

- **1937.01** `office16-admin.quiet-ledger::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-government/small-surplus` tags: office, administration, budget, competence, quiet, power, vienna, urban
  账目连续第二年没有超支，Luise Stein在报告里加了一句“未发现异常”。Emil用结余给夜班守卫换了炉子，又把旧账本按年份装进箱子；没有人写报道，但来办事的人发现窗口开得比从前早。
  _effects: organization_funds; reputation 0.06; skill; mood 0.030000000000000027; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-4; flag +quiet_surplus; knowledge “审计报告里只有一句“未发现异常”，但窗口提前开门被办事的人记住了。”_

- **1937.01** `agency:attend_meeting` tags: agency, agency:attend_meeting
  Emil走到Arbeiter-Zeitung im Vorwärts-Haus门口，看见便衣和拥挤的人群，最终没有进去。
  _effects: mood -0.040000000000000036; reputation -0.010000000000000002_

- **1937.07** `r15-love.borrowed-room::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-government/one-time` tags: love, intimacy, secret, courtship, secret_intimacy, vienna, urban, crisis
  Emil只去了那一次。房间是借的，床单洗得发白；两个人事后都没有提下一次。Emil回到街上时，天还没黑，卖报的孩子正在喊一个陌生的名字。
  _effects: relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-64; cash -1 (the borrowed room and the coffee); mood 0.030000000000000027; flag +one_borrowed_night; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-64_

- **1937.07** `vienna-housing-move.rent-notice/rent-notice-demand-repair` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money
  Emil没有立刻答话，先去敲Rosa Berg 2的门。两人一起数落墙上的水渍和门框里灌进来的风，楼梯间的窗户修好了，自家墙皮还在剥落。Rosa Berg 2提议一起把话带给看门人：先修好漏水的墙角，涨租的事再谈。Emil把纸重新折好，请他把话带到门外。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-60; mood 0.030000000000000027; flag +rent_dispute; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-60_

- **1937.12** `vienna-market-debt.rent-arrears/rent-sell-furniture-move` tags: market, debt, money, home, shop
  邻居用麻绳把锅和铺盖捆在一起，打了个死结。他说城南有更便宜的屋子，但离厂远。你蹲在地上把最后几件衣服塞进布袋，听见看门人在楼下把垃圾桶挪来挪去。
  _effects: cash 2.8 (卖旧家具所得); mood -0.06000000000000005_

- **1938.01** `p9-work-jobsearch.negotiate-hours/negotiate-hours.half-day` tags: work, job_search, negotiation, wages, employment, task, hours, saturday
  工时表铺在柜台上，边角卷起。Emil指着星期六，Magistrat der k. k. Reichshaupt- und Residenzstadt Wien的手指在数字上敲了两下，说下午歇可以，工钱按日扣。礼拜六中午，他推门出来，街上的影子短得像另一来处。
  _effects: cash -2 (星期六下午工钱按日扣减（其中1.6克朗记入债务）); health 0.03 (终于有半天空闲); mood 0.050000000000000044_

- **1938.04** `p9-social-tenement-neighbors.flour-loan/flour-loan.two-slices-of-bread` tags: housing, neighbors, tenement, daily, money, reputation
  Emil说自家也快见底了，不能再借面粉，临关门前往Grete Moser手里塞了两块面包，说先给孩子垫一垫。Grete Moser攥着面包站了一会儿，说了声谢谢就走了，楼梯上传来脚步和纸袋的磨蹭声。
  _effects: food; mood -0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-102_

- **1938.07** `vienna-church-community.saint-vincent-box/box-request-refused` tags: faith, social, family, home
  登记员翻遍册子，说这户上个月已经领过。楼里的人围着看，谁也没先走开。
  _effects: reputation -0.04; mood -0.050000000000000044_

- **1939.01** `vienna-life-anschluss-1938/complies` tags: politics, persecution, 1938
  Maria Berg 2在街口设了登记桌，要求住户交出姓名、职业和宗教。Emil排队填了表，回家把门牌擦干净。楼里有人连夜搬走，也有人站在阳台上挥手。
  _effects: role; cash -3 (new permits and stamps（其中2.5克朗记入债务）); condition_

- **1939.01** `agency:support_process` tags: agency, agency:support_process
  Emil想帮忙，但组织者不信任新来的人，只让其在外围等消息。
  _effects: mood -0.040000000000000036_

- **1939.06** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.shared_stove_fire/fire_scald_hand` tags: home, social, family, housing, injury
  Emil在合租房走廊的公用灶台上煮土豆，转身取盐时袖子扫翻了铁锅，滚水浇在左手背上。Luise Kral从隔壁冲出来，扯下自己的围裙裹住烫伤处，又跑下楼叫来住在二楼的接生婆。接生婆用蛋清和旧亚麻布包扎，嘱咐三天不能沾水。
  _effects: health -0.05 (左手背烫伤); relation friend with person-luise-kral_

- **1939.09** `p9-social-tenement-neighbors.stove-and-line/stove-and-line.move-to-line-end` tags: housing, neighbors, tenement, daily, money, reputation
  Emil把自己泡着的衣服端到绳子最末一段，把靠近炉子的位置让给Anton Bauer，条件是对方看着火，别让水烧干。Anton Bauer点头，往炉膛里添了一块煤，说中午以前一定把炉子空出来。两家的盆就挨着放在窗下。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-27; mood 0.030000000000000027; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-27_

- **1940.01** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.laundry_line_dispute/subject_yields_line` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Emil看Luise Kral盆里那几件小衬衫，把自家床单卷起来抱回屋，说等下午再晾。Luise Kral愣了一下，闷头把衣服挂好，收工回来时在Emil门口放了一小包用旧报纸裹的洗衣碱。Emil打开报纸，碱面里还掺着几粒粗盐。
  _effects: relation friend with person-luise-kral; food_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:008346_

- **1940.01** `vienna-life-injury-recovery/clinic-treatment` tags: health, work, family
  Emil在受伤后的第三天去了诊所。医生清洗伤口、换药，收下诊费，嘱咐不要急着回机器旁。Emil把药方折好，回家路上买了纱布。
  _effects: health 0.04999999999999999 (wound treatment); cash -8 (clinic and bandages（其中8克朗记入债务）)_

  上一任在离任前签了一份四十人的任命名单，最后一行没有日期。Emil把Gustav Kral的名字添在第四十一行，注明“补录”；发薪日，会计问这四十一个人里为什么有六个已经去世。
  _effects: position 编外顾问 @building:person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-1-evt:world-vienna-1912:003504; organization_funds; flag +forty_first_name; knowledge “上一任留下的四十人名单里，有六个已经去世。”; mood -0.020000000000000018_

  Emil暂时把那件事收进抽屉；日子还有别的东西要应付。
  _effects: mood -0.009999999999999898_

- **1940.05** `vienna-housing-move.rent-notice/rent-notice-pay` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money
  Emil把通知念给Maria Kral 2听，Maria Kral 2摇头说是楼梯间那几块新玻璃的账。Emil去楼下杂货铺换开一张五克朗的纸币，回屋一枚一枚摆开数，煤箱底多留了二十赫勒。
  _effects: cash -2 (按月多付两克朗房租（其中1.2克朗记入债务）); food; mood -0.030000000000000027_

- **1940.08** `vienna-parish-aid.funeral-society/funeral-society.defer` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  Emil把抽屉拉开又推上，铜币在木板上滚了半圈。Otto Lechner 2合上本子说，拖欠的人不少，可规矩就是规矩。她走下两级台阶又回头提醒，发工钱那天她会在教堂侧门等着。
  _effects: mood -0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-51_

  _effects: mood -0.12_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:009066_

- **1941.01** `af17-wish.family-inheritance::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/kept-the-shop-and-the-sisters` tags: wish, love, family, inheritance, shop, debt, irony, vienna
  遗嘱把烟铺、楼上的两位老小姐和十四克朗的赊账一起留给Emil。Otto Berg第一次见面就叫Emil“侄子”，把钥匙、账本和一只总在柜台下打盹的猫交过来。Emil原本只想要一个家，现在有了一个需要每天清晨六点开门的家；Hermann Lechner 2在门口抽完一支雪茄，提醒Emil月底先还利息。
  _effects: role; cash -2 (先还一部分烟铺赊账（其中2克朗记入债务）); relation kin with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-37; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-95; reputation 0.02; condition; flag +kept_the_tobacco_shop; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1941.01** `agency:organize_meeting` tags: agency, agency:organize_meeting
  Emil原定在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang开会，但来的人不够，或者有人临时退缩；最后只留下几张没发完的纸条。
  _effects: mood -0.030000000000000027_

- **1941.05** `p9-gap-adult-solitude.misdelivered-letter/misdelivered-letter.opened` tags: adulthood, daily, solitude, ordinary, home, letter
  信封没封牢，Emil在灯下把它掀开，里面是维也纳联合银行的催款单，欠了三十四克朗。她认出那名字是楼上常穿黑裙的女人，便把纸原样叠回去，用米汤把封口粘好，放到门房窗台上。
  _effects: mood -0.020000000000000018; flag +knows_upstairs_debt_

  后屋的桌上摊着旧报纸，Emil用尺子画格子，一格一户，旁边写清煤价和面包房欠条。Franz Lechner指着第三格说那家孩子咳了整夜，先划过去。蜡烛烧到桌沿时，名单终于能看懂了。
  _effects: skill; relation acquaintance with person-franz-lechner; mood 0.030000000000000027_

  Emil在预算表上签了字，把冬季煤房的款项从三笔并成一笔，又给夜班守卫加了半份津贴。Gustav Winter算出省下的钱刚好够修屋顶；没有人鼓掌，但一月的会议室不再需要穿大衣。
  _effects: organization_funds; reputation 0.039999999999999994; skill; mood 0.020000000000000018; flag +signed_balanced_budget; knowledge “把冬季煤房的款项并成一笔后，省下的钱够修会议室屋顶。”_

  Emil想帮忙，但组织者不信任新来的人，只让其在外围等消息。

- **1942.03** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.laundry_line_dispute/compromise_split_line` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Emil把洗好的床单搭上院子里的公用晾衣绳，占了整条。Luise Weiss 2抱着一盆湿衣服下来，说自家孩子明天要穿干净衬衫上学。两人在绳子两头扯了一阵，最后Emil把床单对折，腾出一半，Luise Weiss 2用木夹子把衬衫一件件排上去。谁也没道歉，但第二天见面都点了头。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-99; mood 0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-99_

- **1942.10** `vienna-medical-treatment.toothache/barber-pull` tags: health, medical, family, money
  Mitzi Kral 2在门口替Emil捏着帽子，听见里面一声闷哼。弗朗茨把牙扔进搪瓷盘，说瞧这牙根都黑了。收钱时他多要了二十赫勒，算明矾水的钱，说下次别再拖成这样。
  _effects: cash -1.2 (理发师拔牙收费（其中1.2克朗记入债务）); health 0.03 (病牙被拔除但手法粗糙); mood -0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-71_

- **1942.12** `r2-law.credit-union::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-government/employer-blocks-deduction` tags: finance, credit_union, wages, debt, factory, vienna, urban, crisis
  Magistrat der k. k. Reichshaupt- und Residenzstadt Wien把扣款单退回来，说厂里不替信用合作社收债，除非社里把存款转进厂里的账户。Luise Hofer说那是挪款，Magistrat der k. k. Reichshaupt- und Residenzstadt Wien说那就不扣。Emil站在两边中间，听见Maria Berg 2在后面嘀咕：借钱的时候都说是兄弟，扣钱的时候都說是厂规。
  _effects: condition; flag +employer_refused_payroll_deduction; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-91; knowledge “the employer wants the union’s deposits before collecting it”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-91_

  两名工人把Otto Lechner家的钢琴抬上卡车，琴腿在门框上磕掉一块漆。Luise Hofer把一张纸塞给Emil，说只要签“屋内无人认领”，就不必再搬第二趟。Emil签了名，看见Otto Lechner站在对面窗帘后，手一直按在玻璃上。
  _effects: state_add hs18-street-witness state:world-vienna-1912:577; relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000828:child; knowledge “签字比争辩更快，也比争辩更难忘。”; flag +signed_abandoned_piano_receipt_

  Emil带着成绩单去学校找Franz Lechner，问减免和奖学金。Franz Lechner领到办公室，一位Maria Berg 2翻着册子说可以减一半书本费，服装费仍得自己出，答复要等开学前一周。Emil把两张纸重新折好，回家没有对母亲提准信，只把桌布底下的决定又压了几天。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-franz-lechner; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-88; flag +awaiting_school_aid_

  _effects: historical development:initiative-building:person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-1-evt:world-vienna-1912:003504-1937 as supporter; historical_influence; reputation 0.014999999999999993_

  Emil喝完汤没走，留下来帮修女把大锅刷净，又把木碗按大小摞好。厨房后头有半块面包和一碗剩汤，修女说是帮忙人的午饭。Otto Lechner在门口等着一起回去，说下回还来，洗锅的活总有人要干。
  _effects: food; skill; relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000828:child; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1943.09** `vienna-market-debt.pawn-watch/pawn-whole-watch` tags: market, debt, money, home, shop
  你把银壳怀表推到绒布托盘上，当铺掌柜用放大镜扫了一眼机芯，报出八克朗。你盯着那张窄票上模糊的赎当日期，想起父亲在第十区工棚里上弦的样子。邻居在门外咳嗽，你捏着票和钱走出来，先拐去房东那里付了半个月的欠租。
  _effects: cash 8 (典当银壳怀表所得); mood -0.020000000000000018; flag +watch_pawned_

  Emil把最后一袋黑麦埋进煤堆下面，夜里听见巡逻队从窗前走过。第二天面包房照常开门，但货架上少了三排面包；Emil知道那不是因为城里没有粮。
  _effects: food; mood 0.020000000000000018; flag +hidden_grain_

  _effects: state_parameter hs18-street-witness.certainty 85 -> 77; mood -0.020000000000000018_

  Emil又把那件没实现的事翻出来想了一遍；这一次没有告诉任何人。
  _effects: mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1944.01** `agency:support_process` tags: agency, agency:support_process
  Emil想帮忙，但组织者不信任新来的人，只让其在外围等消息。
  _effects: mood -0.040000000000000036; reputation -0.009999999999999995_

- **1944.02** `r1-hc.alcohol-household::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement::alcohol-crisis-cheap-spirits/alcohol-crisis-methylated` tags: health, alcohol, addiction, household, work, vienna, urban, crisis
  Emil把工业酒精兑上水，颜色像淡茶。第二天眼睛疼得睁不开，吐出来的东西有一股油漆味；Maria Berg把剩下的半瓶倒进阴沟，说这玩意儿能要命；窗台上的半瓶水也变了色。
  _effects: cash -8 (柜台下面那半瓶烧酒（其中5.5克朗记入债务）); condition; role; relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000151:child; knowledge “the cheap bottle was industrial alcohol cut with water”_

- **1944.03** `p9-social-tenement-neighbors.evicted-neighbours/evicted-neighbours.district-office-queue` tags: housing, neighbors, tenement, daily, money, reputation
  区公所的长凳坐满了人。Gustav Winter的笔尖在名册上划了一道，把纸推过来时没有抬眼。Emil把纸折了三折，塞进Luise Weiss 2手里。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-99; mood 0.030000000000000027_

- **1944.10** `oj18-lift.stuck::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/hand-crank` tags: lift, building, work, night, passenger, odd_job, suspense, vienna
  电梯停在四楼半，门缝里只看见一截电线。Emil让Franz Berg抓紧扶手，自己摇动手柄，一格一格把轿厢放下来。门开时，Franz Berg的白大褂后背全湿了；他塞给Emil两张钞票，说手术室里还有人等着。
  _effects: organization_found 环城大道写字楼管理处 (other); position 电梯员 @building:person-phase15-Emil-world-vienna-1912-evt:world-vienna-1912:011454; state_add oj18-odd-apprenticeship state:world-vienna-1912:609; cash 2 (the doctor's reward); reputation 0.03; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-89; flag +lift_operator; knowledge “电梯停在四楼半时，手动柄要一格一格放，不能一次松到底。”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-89; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-124; role; flag +odd_job_apprentice_

- **1945.01** `vienna-life-1945-liberation/survives` tags: war, liberation, 1945
  苏联士兵进城时，Emil躲在门后听见坦克履带压过石板路。几天后街口贴出告示，Emil去领了一份汤和一块面包。
  _effects: role; food; cash -2 (replacement documents（其中2克朗记入债务）); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-30_

- **1945.01** `state_tick:hs18-street-witness/memory-drift/holds` tags: hs18-street-witness, witness, memory, history, testimony, rumor, risk, street
  Emil又把那天的顺序在脑子里走了一遍：先是门响，再是靴子，最后才是那声喊。细节还在，只是每讲一次，旁边就多出一点别人替你补上的颜色。

- **1945.01** `state_tick:oj18-odd-apprenticeship/strange-year/blamed-for-the-noise` tags: oj18-odd-apprenticeship, work, odd_job, apprenticeship, night, trust, secrecy, state
  楼上住户投诉夜里有人拖椅子，管事的把巡查簿翻到Emil的那一页，说签字太潦草，看不出巡过几趟。Emil想辩解，却想起那晚确实在楼梯间多站了一刻钟；扣掉的工钱刚好够买一双不漏水的鞋。
  _effects: state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.trust 30 -> 22; state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.reliability 60 -> 55; flag +odd_job_scare_

- **1945.01** `r13-work.repay-debt::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/pledge-seized` tags: commerce, debt, credit, obligation, household, vienna, urban, crisis
  还款日过了三天，Mitzi Moser 2带着Luise Kral 2上门，把Emil的缝纫机抬走了。Emil说再给一周，Mitzi Moser 2指着还款簿上的红格子说，规矩就是规矩。缝纫机被抬下楼时，踏板在墙上磕掉一块灰；Luise Kral 2站在门口，始终没有抬头。
  _effects: cash -8 (还款簿上的红格子（其中8克朗记入债务）); condition; flag +work13_debt_pledge_seized; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-21; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-131_

- **1945.03** `vienna-life-wwii-homefront/shelter` tags: war, homefront, shortage, injury
  警报响时Emil躲进地下室，和十几户人挤在长凳上。头顶传来闷响，灰尘从梁上落下。警报解除后，街上多了一堆碎瓦。
  _effects: health -0.02 (shelter conditions); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-70_

- **1945.03** `p9-history-medical-daily.sanitation-inspection/sign-only-own-household` tags: health, medical, public_health, family, pharmacy, sanitation, daily
  Anton Winter把表格递过来，说眼神不济。Emil一行行念完，在自家那栏签了名，把笔放下；Gustav Winter问还有谁家没签，走廊里静了一会儿，有人把门关上了。
  _effects: reputation -0.03; relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:002224:child; knowledge “卫生巡查表上每户只能签自己那一栏，代签要负连带责任”_

- **1945.04** `state_reaction:wish-love/love-betrayal` tags: wish-love, wish, wish:love, love, family, intimacy, state, state_reaction

  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:012042_

- **1945.05** `vienna-medical-treatment.sanatorium/home-rest` tags: health, medical, family, money
  炉子封小了，屋里凉得快，Emil把大衣盖在被子上。安娜收碗时看见窗台上一排药瓶，没说什么，第二天多带了半块黑面包。楼下的管道工敲了一下午铁皮，咳嗽声混在里面听不真切。
  _effects: health 0.03 (在家休养但条件有限); food; cash -0.5 (房东要求的清洁费（其中0.5克朗记入债务）); relation friend with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000288:child_

  _effects: cash -2 (clean cloth); condition_

  _effects: state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.strangeKnowledge 10 -> 18; state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.trust 22 -> 19; knowledge “地下室那扇门后面有东西被搬动过，但巡查簿上没有记录。”_

  _effects: cash -5 (退还代理费和赔偿墨水瓶); reputation -0.03; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-124; condition; flag +promotion_typo_reversed_

  Emil把那段关系继续藏在日常的缝隙里。一次擦肩、一张没有署名的纸条、借来的半个钟头，都足够撑过这个月；回家前Emil在街角站了一会儿，把围巾重新系好。
  _effects: state_parameter forbidden-attachment.exposure 10 -> 5; state_parameter forbidden-attachment.reciprocity 55 -> 58; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1949.01** `r13-civ.ration-card::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/ration-card-issued` tags: politics, rationing, food, war, institution, vienna, urban, postwar
  Gustav Winter把配给证从本子上撕下来，填上Emil家的门牌和人数，又用打孔机在角上打了一个洞。Emil把证举到灯下，看见上面印着面粉、糖和煤的数量，每一项都比去年少。
  _effects: food; flag +ration_card_issued; knowledge “the monthly ration is smaller than last year’s”_

- **1949.04** `vienna-medical-treatment.night-fever/sweat-it-out` tags: health, medical, family, money
  Emil说胡话时念着车间的排班表，Otto Lechner 2一句句应着，拿小勺往嘴里送温水。床头那半瓶退烧药早见了底，她没舍得倒最后一滴，留着天亮再说。
  _effects: condition; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-51_

- **1949.06** `vienna-parish-aid.soup-kitchen/soup-kitchen.leave` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  Emil看见熟人排在前面，帽檐压得低低的，便转身拐进旁边的小巷。Elisabeth Weiss追出来问怎么不领了，Emil说人太多，改天再来。回到屋里，灶是冷的，早上剩的半个土豆还在盘子里。Emil把它切成片，就着水咽下去。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-83; food; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-83_

- **1950.01** `vienna-postwar-housing-1950s/stay-with-relatives` tags: adulthood, postwar, housing, family
  分房名单又往后推了一年，Emil一家搬进亲戚家的厨房隔间。Ferdinand Hofer 2送来一块旧帘子当隔断；夜里有人起身，全屋都听得见。
  _effects: cash -3 (shared household costs（其中3克朗记入债务）); condition; mood -0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-119; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-119_

- **1950.01** `office16-office.lose-the-chair::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-government/carried-out` tags: office, removal, loss, politics, power, transition, vienna, urban
  最后一次会议没有让Emil发言。保安把纸箱搬到门口，箱子里有半瓶墨水、一只坏掉的台灯和没写完的年度报告。Gustav Winter跟在后面，手里拿着新的门锁；走廊尽头，秘书已经把名牌翻了过去。
  _effects: position_end 助理文书 @building:person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-1-evt:world-vienna-1912:003504; reputation -0.06; condition; flag +carried_out_of_office; knowledge “名牌在会议结束前就被翻了过去。”_

- **1950.01** `oj18-lift.stuck::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/blackout-lift` tags: lift, building, work, night, passenger, odd_job, suspense, vienna
  停电来得毫无预兆，轿厢里只剩一盏应急蜡。Emil用手动闸把轿厢停在最近的楼层，又用肩膀顶开门；乘客一个个跨出去，最后一个人回头说“你该去当消防员”。Emil的胳膊酸了三天，管理处的维修单上却只写“正常”。
  _effects: reputation 0.04; cash 1 (blackout shift pay); state_add oj18-odd-apprenticeship state:world-vienna-1912:794; flag +blackout_lift; knowledge “停电时轿厢里只剩一盏应急蜡，手动闸要停在最近的楼层。”; role; flag +odd_job_apprentice_

- **1950.01** `state_tick:forbidden-attachment/discretion-year/seen-in-the-street` tags: forbidden-attachment, love, intimacy, secret, courtship, secret_intimacy, state, state_tick
  Emil和那人在剧院后门分开时，被楼上的住户看见了。第二天，邻居的问话里多了一个不该出现的名字；Emil没有承认，只把外套挂到柜子最里面。
  _effects: state_parameter forbidden-attachment.exposure 5 -> 20; state_parameter forbidden-attachment.jealousy 5 -> 10; reputation -0.04_

- **1950.01** `agency:attend_meeting` tags: agency, agency:attend_meeting
  Emil去了Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang，坐在后排听完了关于第二区夜班出租合作社在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsam的争论；散会时带走一张油印提纲。
  _effects: historical development:initiative-taxi:person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-12-evt:world-vienna-1912:003514-1939 as participant; knowledge “我参加了第二区夜班出租合作社在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Ga。”; mood 0.030000000000000027_

- **1950.11** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.laundry_line_dispute/compromise_split_line` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Emil把洗好的床单搭上院子里的公用晾衣绳，占了整条。Anton Winter 2抱着一盆湿衣服下来，说自家孩子明天要穿干净衬衫上学。两人在绳子两头扯了一阵，最后Emil把床单对折，腾出一半，Anton Winter 2用木夹子把衬衫一件件排上去。谁也没道歉，但第二天见面都点了头。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-130; mood 0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-130_

- **1951.01** `office16-budget.sign-or-block::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-government/signed-with-margin` tags: office, budget, permit, administration, power, economy, vienna, urban
  Emil在预算表上签了字，把冬季煤房的款项从三笔并成一笔，又给夜班守卫加了半份津贴。Helene Bauer 2算出省下的钱刚好够修屋顶；没有人鼓掌，但一月的会议室不再需要穿大衣。
  _effects: reputation 0.04; skill; mood 0.020000000000000018; knowledge “把冬季煤房的款项并成一笔后，省下的钱够修会议室屋顶。”_

- **1951.01** `state_tick:forbidden-attachment/discretion-year/seen-in-the-street` tags: forbidden-attachment, love, intimacy, secret, courtship, secret_intimacy, state, state_tick
  Emil和那人在剧院后门分开时，被楼上的住户看见了。第二天，邻居的问话里多了一个不该出现的名字；Emil没有承认，只把外套挂到柜子最里面。
  _effects: state_parameter forbidden-attachment.exposure 20 -> 35; state_parameter forbidden-attachment.jealousy 10 -> 15; reputation -0.04_

- **1951.01** `state_tick:oj18-odd-apprenticeship/strange-year/blamed-for-the-noise` tags: oj18-odd-apprenticeship, work, odd_job, apprenticeship, night, trust, secrecy, state
  楼上住户投诉夜里有人拖椅子，管事的把巡查簿翻到Emil的那一页，说签字太潦草，看不出巡过几趟。Emil想辩解，却想起那晚确实在楼梯间多站了一刻钟；扣掉的工钱刚好够买一双不漏水的鞋。
  _effects: state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.trust 35 -> 27; state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.reliability 65 -> 60; mood -0.030000000000000027_

- **1951.01** `p9-history-public-action.organize-relief/credit-own-name` tags: politics, union, party, activism, public_action, relief, leaflet, task
  市场掌柜把账本翻到空白页，铅笔写上“月底清”，Emil 把拇指按进印泥，红印盖得有点歪。Maria Bauer 扛着半袋面粉往回走，袋底漏出的粉在石板路上留下白印。
  _effects: cash -5 (以自家名义赊购面粉); food; mood -0.050000000000000044; relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:004449:child_

- **1951.02** `p10-work-career.wage-shortfall/p10-work-career.wage-shortfall.refuse` tags: adulthood, work, career, wages, injury, pension, employment, deduction
  Emil把工资袋推回柜台，说不讲清楚就不收。第二天他被调去洗料间，工钱按日算，水汽熏得眼睛发红。Helene Bauer 2路过门口看了一眼，没敢多站。
  _effects: employment 洗料间日工 @ org-wien-magistrat; health -0.03 (洗料间水汽和碱水刺激); condition_

- **1951.03** `vienna-housing-move.rent-notice/rent-notice-neighbors-together` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money
  Emil把Rosa Huber和楼里另两户人家叫到楼道，把那张纸摆在窗台上传看。看门人在楼梯拐角听着，说房东礼拜三来收钱。几家人合计好，到那天一起等在门厅，先把克朗递过去，再把修补的事一件件摆出来。Emil把纸折进围裙口袋，觉得人凑齐了话也硬气些。
  _effects: mood 0.040000000000000036; flag +tenant_collective_

- **1951.12** `vienna-medical-treatment.sanatorium/home-rest` tags: health, medical, family, money
  安娜的汤锅外面包着旧毛巾，端进来时还烫手。Emil靠着枕头喝，喝一半咳一半。房东太太在走廊里故意大声说，肺病可不是闹着玩的，安娜探出头去，说医生讲了不传染。
  _effects: health 0.03 (在家休养但条件有限); food; cash -0.5 (房东要求的清洁费); relation friend with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:001330:child_

- **1952.01** `vienna-life-injury-recovery/clinic-treatment` tags: health, work, family
  Emil在受伤后的第三天去了诊所。医生清洗伤口、换药，收下诊费，嘱咐不要急着回机器旁。Emil把药方折好，回家路上买了纱布。
  _effects: health 0.05 (wound treatment); cash -8 (clinic and bandages（其中8克朗记入债务）)_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:016085_

  Maria Berg 2在门口堵了三天，第四天把Emil的辞职信全文登了出来。信只有四行，最后一行是“为避免机构继续为我的判断付出代价”。Emil把办公室的钥匙交给Josef Winter 2，没有带走任何文件。
  _effects: position_end 电梯员 @building:person-phase15-Emil-world-vienna-1912-evt:world-vienna-1912:011454; reputation -0.08; mood -0.030000000000000027; flag +resigned_over_scandal; knowledge “辞职信被全文刊登，接任者当天就搬进了办公室。”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-155_

  夜班结束前，Emil听见地下室传来拖拽声。下去时，门开着，地上只有一道湿拖把的痕迹；第二天，所有人都说那间屋子锁了十年。
  _effects: state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.strangeKnowledge 15 -> 23; state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.trust 27 -> 24; knowledge “地下室那扇门后面有东西被搬动过，但巡查簿上没有记录。”_

  遗嘱把烟铺、楼上的两位老小姐和十四克朗的赊账一起留给Emil。Katharina Hofer第一次见面就叫Emil“侄子”，把钥匙、账本和一只总在柜台下打盹的猫交过来。Emil原本只想要一个家，现在有了一个需要每天清晨六点开门的家；Maria Berg 2在门口抽完一支雪茄，提醒Emil月底先还利息。

- **1953.07** `vienna-parish-aid.rent-box/rent-box.refuse` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  Emil说这个月还能撑，房租差的那几克朗打算替人洗衣补上，请Maria Berg 2把篮子提回去给更困难的人家。Maria Berg 2在门口停了一会儿，把一小块肥皂留在窗台上，说不是救济，是教区多出来的。门关上后，屋里只剩煤箱底刮出的响声。
  _effects: mood -0.030000000000000027; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-88; flag +parish_refused_aid_

  Emil把一份扩建许可证压在抽屉最下面，理由是排水和消防通道不合规。申请人Gustav Winter在走廊里等到下班，最后只拿到一张写着“补件”的纸条。Emil知道这份许可明年还会再来，但今年不会从自己的桌上出去。

- **1954.05** `ld18-room.rented-hour::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/the-landlady-knows` tags: love, room, rent, secret, queer, intimacy, secret_intimacy, vienna
  Maria Berg 2在楼梯上撞见两个人牵着手，第二天把房租涨了三克朗。她说自己什么也没看见，只是煤贵了。Emil把钱数出来，Luise Weiss把窗帘换成更厚的一层；从那以后，她们进门先听楼下的脚步声。
  _effects: state_add ld18-impossible-attachment state:world-vienna-1912:956; cash -3 (the rent rose after the landlady saw them); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-88; reputation -0.01999999999999999; mood -0.020000000000000018; flag +impossible_attachment_active_

- **1954.06** `p9-gap-adult-ordinary-day.flea-market-cup/flea-market-cup.buy-cracked` tags: adulthood, daily, leisure, neighborhood, ordinary, flea_market, object
  杯口描着金边，金已经磨掉了一半，露出底下的白瓷。摊主用一块脏布把杯子擦了擦，说这是老太太们喝咖啡用的。Emil把它对着光看，裂纹里有一条淡淡的褐线，像渗进去的茶渍。
  _effects: cash -0.4 (买下有裂纹的描金边杯子); mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1954.12** `vienna-legacy-worklife.apprentice-wage/apprentice-wage.beer-with-senior` tags: legacy, work, family, reform, ordinary_life
  十二赫勒换来两杯淡啤酒，找零是四枚一赫勒的铜币。Emil把铜币收好，听师兄讲那年冬天的事，讲到一半师兄停了停，说“你别到处讲”。酒馆里有人拉手风琴，声音盖过了后半句。
  _effects: cash -1 (请师兄喝一杯啤酒); skill_

- **1955.01** `state_reaction:ld18-impossible-attachment/a-question-at-the-office` tags: ld18-impossible-attachment, love, intimacy, queer, unregistered, commitment, secret, desire
  办公室有人问Emil，为什么总有一个女人在楼下等她。Emil说那是表妹；对方笑了一下，说现在的表妹真体贴。下午，Emil把桌上的相框扣倒，又在没人时翻回来，用袖子擦了擦玻璃上的灰。

Born 1912.01, died 1934.01 (age 22).
Education: none 0; occupation: none; roles: infant, child, job_seeker, wife, movement_member.

### 46 subject events

- **1914.02** `statistical_infection`
  Lena在Hofer-Wohnung, Leopoldstadt病倒了，肺炎让身体发热、虚弱，接下来的日子只能卧床。

- **1914.03** `statistical_illness_worsened`
  Lena的肺炎没有完全好转，咳嗽和发热又拖了一个月，家里人轮流守着。

- **1914.06** `statistical_illness_worsened`
  Lena的肺炎没有完全好转，咳嗽和发热又拖了一个月，家里人轮流守着。

- **1914.08** `statistical_recovery`
  Lena熬过了这场肺炎，能坐起来喝一点汤，但身体还很虚。

- **1918.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.market-line/line-half-flour` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, market, food
  面粉袋压在胸口，越抱越沉。Lena前面的大婶一直往后靠，Lena就把膝盖顶住对方的篮子，硬留出半步。买到半袋面粉后，Lena的鞋跟留在人堆里，找回来时鞋面全是泥。回家路上布袋换了两次肩，赤脚踩过石板时脚底发麻。
  _effects: food; health -0.020000000000000018 (赤脚走石板路磨伤脚底); skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-24_

- **1918.10** `vienna-early-childhood.stairwell-fall/landlord-complaint` tags: childhood, family, home, street

  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-berta-weiss; mood -0.07; flag +stairs_accident_noted_

  _effects: cash -0.02 (赊账添的两赫勒利息（其中0.01克朗记入债务）); food; relation acquaintance with person-josefine-hofer_

  Lena把母亲每月去郊区诊所的事告诉了Anton，包括那张写着“神经衰弱”的纸和父亲夜里摔杯子的声音。Anton听完，只把口袋里的玻璃弹珠全倒出来，说换着玩，谁也别提。那枚弹珠在Lena的枕头下放了十几年；母亲后来再没提过那张纸，Anton也再没问。

- **1926.01** `p9-childhood-apprenticeship.cover-for-sibling/delivery-paid` tags: adolescence, work, apprenticeship, labour, training, family, task, task:family
  Lena背着床单筐走过两条街，肩膀被带子磨出一道红印。第二户人家的女佣数了件数，把工钱用纸包好。Lena回家时Anton还躺着，Lena把纸包塞到枕头底下，没叫醒人。晚饭时Anton自己坐起来端碗，Lena才觉得肩膀没那么沉。
