  _effects: cash 0.01 (被扣后只剩一赫勒); mood -0.049999999999999996; relation acquaintance with person-friedrich-hofer; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-3_

- **1920.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.fetch-coal/coal-half-credit` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, coal, errand
  “月底一起结。”掌柜说完，把半筐煤推到柜台外。Anton用胸口顶住筐沿往家挪，煤灰落进领口，手心被麻绳磨得发亮。Josefine Hofer在Haus der Tante in Klosterneuburg门口等到人，先看了看筐底，再看了看Anton的手，把漏掉的碎渣扫进簸箕。
  _effects: food; relation kin with person-josefine-hofer_

- **1920.10** `iw18-school.bell-rope::ctx-klosterneuburg-rural-1912::milieu-tenement/rings-it` tags: childhood, school, bell, comic, play, injury, rural, agriculture
  铃绳比Anton高出一截，孩子把煤箱拖到墙边，踩上去，指尖才够到绳头。铃声响得整条街都回头，Berta Lechner从办公室出来，先皱眉，随后忍不住笑，说下次搬凳子记得叫人扶。
  _effects: reputation 0.02; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-18; mood 0.05; flag +rang_the_school_bell; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-18_

- **1920.11** `kernel-child.punishment::ctx-klosterneuburg-rural-1912::milieu-tenement/edu-punish-belt` tags: childhood, family, punishment, neighbor, conflict, rural, agriculture, small_town
  Anton打碎了厨房的水罐，碎片扫进簸箕时还在响。Friedrich Hofer解下皮带，让Anton转过身。三下之后，Friedrich Hofer的手停在半空，最后把皮带扔回椅背，自己去院里提水。Anton站着没动，听见水桶碰井沿的声音。
  _effects: relation kin with person-friedrich-hofer; flag +beaten_for_broken_jar; mood -0.10000000000000002_

- **1920.12** `iw18-school.ink-pot::ctx-klosterneuburg-rural-1912::milieu-tenement/the-blame-sticks` tags: childhood, school, cruelty, shame, teacher, ink, rural, agriculture
  墨水瓶从桌角翻下去，蓝黑的一摊漫过Anton的抄写本。Grete Winter从讲台后抬起头，先看见Anton手上的墨，便让Anton站到墙角。真正碰倒瓶子的Lena低着头，把脚尖缩进凳子下面，一句话也没有说。
  _effects: mood -0.06; reputation -0.03; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-24; relation acquaintance with person-phase15-Lena-world-vienna-1912; flag +ink_pot_blamed; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-24_

- **1921.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.laundry-help/laundry-dragged-docked-pay` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, laundry, work
  床单太长，Anton拖着一角往绳子走，布边扫过煤渣，立刻黑了一道。Anna Moser弯腰看了看，脸沉下来，说这角得重洗，工钱扣一半。Anton接过一赫勒，蹲在盆边用清水搓那一道灰，搓到指节发红，水面上还浮着煤末。
  _effects: cash 0.01 (被扣后只剩一赫勒); mood -0.04999999999999999; relation acquaintance with person-anna-moser; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-21_

- **1921.06** `kernel-child.first-errand::ctx-klosterneuburg-rural-1912::milieu-tenement/edu-errand-good-change` tags: childhood, family, market, money, errand, rural, agriculture, small_town
  Friedrich Hofer把两枚十字币放进Anton手心，让去街角买盐和半块面包。Anton把钱攥出汗，先数了一遍，又在柜台前重数一遍。Anna Moser把面包用纸包好，找回一枚铜币；Anton把铜币含在嘴里跑回家，咸味一直留到门口。
  _effects: food; skill; relation kin with person-friedrich-hofer; flag +ran_first_errand_

- **1922.01** `iw18-play.toy-theatre::ctx-klosterneuburg-rural-1912::milieu-tenement/the-parent-sells-the-theatre` tags: childhood, play, theatre, family, wonder, comic, rural, agriculture
  Friedrich Hofer把鞋盒戏台和旧纸板一起卖给了收废纸的人，换回几枚硬币和一小筐煤。Anton放学回家，看见床底空了，没有哭，只把剩下的硬纸狗藏进课本里。那天晚上，Friedrich Hofer多给孩子盛了半勺汤。
  _effects: cash 1 (the toy theatre was sold as waste paper); mood -0.04999999999999999; flag +toy_theatre_sold_

- **1922.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.fetch-coal/coal-neighbor-speaks` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, coal, errand
  “记我名下。”Anna Moser说完，掌柜才把秤砣挪开。账本上多出两赫勒的利息，煤筐却只装了半下。Anton跟着Anna Moser走出门，Anna Moser低声嘱咐明天先把钱送来，别让掌柜再找到话头。
  _effects: cash -0.02 (赊账添的两赫勒利息（其中0.02克朗记入债务）); food; relation acquaintance with person-anna-moser_

- **1922.03** `vienna-childhood-street.washhouse-fever/fever-recover` tags: childhood, street, social, home
  Anton在洗衣房的长凳上睡着，醒来时喉咙像塞了棉花。Josefine Hofer把最后的干净布巾浸了凉水，敷在Anton额头上，整夜换了七八回。第四天Anton能坐起来喝粥，下嘴唇脱下一小片皮，Josefine Hofer用指尖替Anton拈掉。
  _effects: condition; relation kin with person-josefine-hofer_

- **1922.09** `vienna-childhood-friends.courtyard-race/won-the-race-lost-a-button` tags: childhood, social, school, street
  晒衣绳下挂着湿床单，孩子们绕着跑，鞋底把水甩到墙上。Anton在第三圈超过Hermann Lechner，手先碰到墙根。两颗玻璃弹珠进了Anton口袋，外套第二颗纽扣却崩进下水道。Hermann Lechner从二楼窗口骂了一句，扔下一根别针。
  _effects: mood 0.04999999999999999; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-25; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-25_

- **1923.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.market-line/line-sold-out-potatoes` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, market, food
  木窗“咔”地合上，Anton的布袋还敞着口。伙计隔着板缝说下星期再来。Anton在墙根站了一会儿，把硬币排在掌心数了两遍，走到旁边土豆摊前，挑了几个小的，让摊主当面过秤。家里人晚上把钱和土豆倒在桌上，重新数了一遍。
  _effects: food; mood -0.030000000000000027; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-33_

- **1923.02** `p9-childhood-household.strap-by-the-door/strap-raised-then-dropped` tags: childhood, family, household, home, siblings, illness, neighbors
  皮带从门后抽出来，Friedrich Hofer举到半空，停住了。Anton闭着眼等，只听见皮带扣碰在桌沿上。Friedrich Hofer把它放下，说晚饭后不许出门，把碎碗片捡干净。Anton蹲在地上一片片捡，窗外传来同伴的笑声，指尖被瓷片划出一道白口。
  _effects: mood -0.03999999999999998; relation kin with person-friedrich-hofer_

- **1923.09** `vienna-childhood-street.cigarette-card/card-stolen` tags: childhood, street, social, home
  巷口有人跑过来，肩膀撞在Anton的胳膊上。烟画卡脱手，被风卷到路中间。Anton追了两步，鞋底在湿石板上打滑，手掌先着地。Lena从后面追上去，在拐角抓住那男孩的袖子，布条撕下来一截，人还是跑了。
  _effects: mood -0.08000000000000002; condition_

- **1925.05** `vienna-adolescence.workshop-apprentice/apprenticeship-accepted` tags: adolescence, school, work, family, social
  作坊里全是松木味。Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne把一块方木夹进台钳，让Anton推刨子。Anton推了十分钟，木花卷到脚背上，刨面总算平了。Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne用拇指摸了摸，收下Anton，从钱盒里数出三克朗，说是头一周的饭钱。Josefine Hofer在门口把围裙攥得很紧。
  _effects: cash 3 ({subject}学徒首周的膳食预付款); education apprenticeship; reputation 0.1; relation kin with person-josefine-hofer_

- **1926.01** `yf18-body.danube-cabin::ctx-klosterneuburg-rural-1912::milieu-tenement/stay-behind-the-curtain` tags: body, desire, river, swimming, shame, first_love, rural, agriculture
  Anton把浴巾裹到下巴，说自己怕水。Lena在门外笑，说去年还见Anton从木塔上跳下去；Anton没有解释，只隔着板缝看Hedwig把水撩到肩上。回家时，Anton的泳衣还是干的，衣角被手攥得起了皱。
  _effects: state_add yf18-first-awakening state:world-vienna-1912:66; flag +yf18_stayed_dry; mood -0.020000000000000018; flag +yf18_first_awakening_active_

- **1926.01** `p9-childhood-youth-aspiration.ask-a-mentor/asked-to-come-back-later` tags: adolescence, education, aspiration, school, books, club, exam, task
  Maria Winter看了看Anton抄的字母表，说大小不一，让他回去用直尺画格子重抄一遍，下个月再来。少年把纸折好，在咖啡馆门口站了一会儿，走回印刷厂时绕到排字房窗外看了很久。
  _effects: mood -0.030000000000000027; skill; flag +mentor_pending_retry; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-9; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-9_

- **1926.01** `state_reaction:yf18-first-awakening/exam-week-distraction` tags: yf18-first-awakening, love, youth, first_love, desire, school, awakening, state
  考试周里，Anton把课本摊在桌上，眼睛却一直看着窗外那条电车线。笔记的页边多了几个没有写完的名字，Anton用橡皮擦掉，纸面起了毛。
  _effects: state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.intensity 40 -> 37; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.talk 0 -> 2_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:002403_

- **1926.12** `r13-turn.exam-failure::ctx-klosterneuburg-rural-1912::milieu-tenement/retake-fee` tags: education, exam, failure, school, work, rural, agriculture, small_town
  成绩单上差两分。Franz Lechner说可以补考，但要先交一克朗卷子费，还得让Friedrich Hofer来签字。Friedrich Hofer在办公室门口把帽子捏变了形，说家里拿不出这份钱。Anton把错题抄到烟盒背面，第二天照样去上课，只是不再举手。
  _effects: cash -1 (落榜名单上的第三个名字); mood -0.15000000000000002; condition; flag +failed_entrance_exam; skill; relation guardian with person-friedrich-hofer_

- **1927.01** `yf18-dare.bridge-rail::ctx-klosterneuburg-rural-1912::milieu-tenement/fall-on-the-ice` tags: dare, bridge, river, friendship, risk, rural, agriculture, small_town
  Anton走到第四步，鞋底在霜上滑了一下，整个人挂在栏杆外侧。Hedwig抓住Anton的手腕，Lena跑去叫桥头卖烤栗子的老人；Anton被拉上来时，膝盖已经湿透，第二天走路一瘸一拐。
  _effects: health -0.08000000000000002 (a fall on the bridge rail); state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.intensity 37 -> 43; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.nerve 0 -> 0; relation friend with person-phase15-Hedwig-world-vienna-1912; flag +yf18_bridge_scare; mood -0.030000000000000027_

- **1927.01** `state_tick:yf18-first-awakening/half-year-of-glances/talked-about` tags: yf18-first-awakening, love, youth, first_love, desire, school, awakening, state
  Anton只把Hedwig的名字写在一张草稿纸背面，第二天那张纸就在教室后排传了一圈。有人把名字念出来，有人问Anton是不是要请人喝咖啡；Anton说那是表姐的名字，声音大得连自己都吓了一跳。
  _effects: state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.talk 2 -> 14; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.nerve 0 -> 0; reputation -0.03; mood -0.030000000000000027_

- **1927.01** `p9-childhood-youth-aspiration.trade-school-application/switched-to-church-class` tags: adolescence, education, aspiration, school, books, club, exam, task
  Anton凑不够十二克朗的正班学费，在门口站了一会儿，转身去了两条街外的教会补习班。修女收下他，发了一本旧算术册子，说每晚七点来，不要钱，但要帮着擦黑板。
  _effects: mood 0.030000000000000027; skill; flag +church_tutoring; relation acquaintance with person-franz-lechner_

- **1928.01** `state_tick:yf18-first-awakening/half-year-of-glances/answered-glance` tags: yf18-first-awakening, love, youth, first_love, desire, school, awakening, state
  Anton在教堂门口或学校走廊里又遇见Hedwig。这一次对方先点了头，Anton准备好的整段话全忘了，只把手里的书换到另一只手上；回家后Anton才发现，自己把书拿倒了。
  _effects: state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.intensity 43 -> 48; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.nerve 0 -> 8; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.reciprocated false -> true; relation friend with person-phase15-Hedwig-world-vienna-1912; mood 0.030000000000000027_

  煤油烧到一半时灯芯冒起黑烟，Anton只好借面包房后窗透出的光背公式。后半夜他靠在墙上睡着了，醒来已经七点。考场上脑子发沉，算术卷子只答了一半，Franz Lechner收卷时敲了敲桌面。
  _effects: health -0.06 (熬夜且睡眠不足); mood -0.07999999999999996; relation acquaintance with person-franz-lechner_

- **1928.01** `state_reaction:yf18-first-awakening/exam-week-distraction` tags: yf18-first-awakening, love, youth, first_love, desire, school, awakening, state
  考试周里，Anton把课本摊在桌上，眼睛却一直看着窗外那条电车线。笔记的页边多了几个没有写完的名字，Anton用橡皮擦掉，纸面起了毛。
  _effects: state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.intensity 48 -> 45; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.talk 14 -> 16_

- **1928.12** `p9-childhood-adolescence.church-club/club-follow-without-paying` tags: adolescence, friendship, identity, work, school, secret, church
  Anton口袋里没有十赫勒，却还是跟着队伍走到车站。修女在栅栏边拦住Anton，翻了翻名册，说没交钱的孩子不能上车；Hedwig在车厢门口回头看。
  _effects: reputation -0.03; mood -0.030000000000000027_

- **1929.01** `yf18-firstlook.parish-dance::ctx-klosterneuburg-rural-1912::milieu-tenement/leave-before-the-last-dance` tags: first_love, dance, church, courtship, jealousy, rural, agriculture, small_town
  Anton在最后一支曲子前溜出礼堂，沿着教堂外墙走了一圈。窗子里传来手风琴声，Anton数到第三遍副歌才往家走；家里问舞会好不好，Anton说人太多，脚疼。
  _effects: state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.intensity 45 -> 39; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.nerve 8 -> 5; mood -0.020000000000000018_

- **1929.01** `yf18-politics.latin-pamphlet::ctx-klosterneuburg-rural-1912::milieu-tenement/pass-it-on` tags: politics, school, pamphlet, censorship, awakening, rural, agriculture, small_town
  Franz Lechner把一本拉丁文课本推到Anton面前，说第三十四页的译文错了。Anton翻到那一页，夹在里面的是一张油印纸，写着面粉配给和工厂检查的数字。放学后，Anton把纸抄了一遍，塞进Lena的历史作业本；那天晚上，Anton第一次觉得自己住的街区有一张看不见的地图。
  _effects: knowledge “配给数字与官方公告并不一致”; flag +yf18_read_pamphlet; relation acquaintance with person-franz-lechner; mood 0.030000000000000027_

- **1929.01** `state_tick:yf18-first-awakening/half-year-of-glances/rehearsed-conversation` tags: yf18-first-awakening, love, youth, first_love, desire, school, awakening, state
  Anton在厨房里对着镜子练了七遍开场白，第二天却在离Hedwig三步远的地方拐进面包店。Anton买了一袋并不需要的盐，回家路上把开场白又想了一遍；这一次，连标点都更熟练了。
  _effects: state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.intensity 39 -> 45; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.talk 16 -> 19; mood 0.010000000000000009_

- **1929.01** `p9-childhood-youth-aspiration.join-a-club/went-once-and-left` tags: adolescence, education, aspiration, school, books, club, exam, task
  Anton交了第一个月的会费，进去坐了一刻钟。屋里太吵，下棋的争论盖过了念书的声音，他借的书也被人翻得卷了边。下个月他路过门口，没再进去交十赫勒。
  _effects: cash -0.1 (只交了一个月会费); relation acquaintance with person-phase15-Rosa-world-vienna-1912; flag +workers_youth_quit; mood -0.020000000000000018_

- **1930.01** `state_expire:yf18-first-awakening` tags: yf18-first-awakening, love, youth, first_love, desire, school, awakening, state
  Anton的「第一次心动」到期；相关安排结束。
  _effects: state_remove yf18-first-awakening state:world-vienna-1912:66; flag -yf18_first_awakening_active_

- **1930.01** `kernel-health.task-pharmacy-hunt::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/pharmacy-last-bottle` tags: health, task, pharmacy, shortage, black_market, reform, medical, schooling
  Anton跑了四家药房，最后在火车站附近的小店找到最后一瓶奎宁。掌柜先问给谁用，又看了看Anton的鞋，才把瓶子从柜台底下拿出来；价格是官价的三倍。他把瓶子塞进内袋，路上每隔一会儿摸一下。
  _effects: cash -25 (last bottle of quinine at three times the official price); health 0.03999999999999998 (quinine obtained and taken); condition; role; knowledge “奎宁这类药会藏在柜台底下，先问给谁用，再看买药人的鞋；价格是官价三倍。”_

- **1930.02** `vienna-social-movement.recruitment/join-and-carry` tags: development, social_movement, organization, politics
  Anton在楼梯下面接到一张折成四折的传单。纸上没有署名，只写着下一次集会的地址。Anton去了，在昏暗的屋里报上姓名，成为Die Fackel的一员，并答应负责自己所在街区的联络。
  _effects: organization_membership; role; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-51; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-51_

- **1931.01** `vienna-life-injury-recovery/wound-infection` tags: health, work, family, injury
  Anton的伤口在几天后肿起来，手腕发热。医生说要切开引流，费用比上次更高；Anton看着账单，先问能不能分两次付。
  _effects: health -0.12 (wound infection); cash -14 (drainage and medicine); condition_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:003834_

- **1931.01** `major-work-first-employment/hired-regular` tags: work, employment, unemployment, major
  清晨六点，印刷厂门口已经站了十几个人。Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne的工头让Anton把手摊开，看了看指节，又问能不能读一页纸。Anton读完，工头在名册上写了名字：明天六点，带工票和一双干净的手。第一周洗墨辊，月底工资三十克朗，柜上先预支四克朗。
  _effects: employment Druckerhelfer @ org-winter-print; role; cash 4 (first wage advance); relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-69; flag +first_regular_job; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-69_

- **1931.01** `vienna-family-courtship/ask-to-share-a-home` tags: family, courtship, household, social, lifecycle
  Anton在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang靠窗的桌边，把一只空咖啡杯转了半圈，说房租两人合付、桌子两人共用。Grete Kral的手停在糖罐盖上，问了一句“要是过不下去呢”，没有当场答应，只说要想一想；杯里的咖啡凉了，话还悬在桌面上。
  _effects: interaction; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-72_

- **1931.01** `vienna-courtship-courtyard.family-table/nod-and-pots` tags: adulthood, courtship, partner, social, family, partnership
  汤喝到一半，Friedrich Hofer放下勺子点了头，但说得很清楚：先攒出一套锅碗、一只挂钟，再把话往下说。Anna Weiss 3在桌下用膝盖碰了碰Anton，Friedrich Hofer把最后一块煮牛肉推过来，饭桌上的空气总算松了；这一顿让对方家里管了饭，自家灶上省下两克朗的吃食，也吃出一个带条件的准信。
  _effects: relation partner with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-52; relation kin with person-friedrich-hofer; food; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-52_

- **1931.01** `r13-civ.hide-fugitive::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/hid-for-three-nights` tags: politics, fugitive, shelter, risk, solidarity, reform, medical, schooling
  Friedrich Winter在阁楼的煤箱后面躲了三天，Anton每天夜里送一次面包和水。第三天，楼下有人问起生人味，Anton说那是煤烟。Friedrich Winter走时，把一枚磨亮的纽扣留在窗台上。
  _effects: cash -1 (阁楼上的脚步声); food; flag +hid_fugitive_three_nights; relation friend with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000135:child; knowledge “the fugitive crossed the yard before the morning roll call”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-2_

- **1931.02** `p9-social-courtship-friendship.fasching-dance/dance-first-polka` tags: courtship, friendship, social, adulthood, reputation, leopoldstadt
  小提琴手拉错了一个音，满屋人笑出声。Elisabeth Bauer在人群后头朝Anton比了个手势，意思是问到了街名；Anton没有回头，只把舞步又踩准了一拍。
  _effects: cash -1 (付给乐队的小费与舞会入场费); relation acquaintance with person-phase15-Emil-world-vienna-1912_

- **1932.01** `interaction_response` tags: interaction
  Grete Kral没有答应，只说现在不合适。Anton把这件事收回去，之后见面仍点头。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-72; interaction_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:004065_

- **1932.01** `vienna-life-work-injury/crushed-hand` tags: adulthood, labour, injury
  Anton在换纸时左手被压板夹住。工友关掉机器，工头拿来急救箱，把伤手包起来送去医院；厂里先垫了十克朗医药费，事故报告写的是“操作不慎”。Anton的左手从此握不紧拳，指尖总是凉的，天阴时骨缝里发疼。
  _effects: health -0.24 (crushed left hand); condition; cash 10 (employer medical advance)_

- **1932.01** `vienna-family-divorce/partner-moves-to-shared-housing` tags: family, marriage, divorce, housing, lifecycle, separation, household
  Anna Weiss 3把一只箱子和工票搬去合租房，带走一半锅碗。Anton留在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang，把空出来的床租给一个学徒，用租金补房租；搬家花掉四克朗，Anton在户籍上成了分居的人，夜里听见隔壁床板响，总要愣一下。
  _effects: relation_end partner with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-52; household; cash -4 (moving and separate goods（其中4克朗记入债务）); mood -0.05999999999999994; role_

- **1932.01** `r13-civ.join-choir::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/voice-rejected` tags: culture, choir, music, community, belonging, reform, medical, schooling
  Franz Lechner听完，说音准还行，但声音太薄，撑不起礼拜天。Anton问能不能先站在最后一排，Franz Lechner说等下次有空位。回家的路上，Anton在无人的巷子里把那首歌唱完。
  _effects: mood -0.07999999999999996; flag +choir_audition_rejected; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-50_

- **1932.10** `p9-social-family-conflict-care.sick-parent-night/hospital-deposit` tags: family, care, siblings, inheritance, housing, money
  Anton跑了一趟医院，把Friedrich Hofer送进病房：押金三克朗，搪瓷杯和旧毛巾一克朗二十赫勒，头三天的药费三克朗八十赫勒，一共八克朗。护士收走便盆，换上带刻度的玻璃壶。Anton回到空了的床边，把塌下去的椅子扶正，才发现自己一整天只喝了一杯咖啡。
  _effects: cash -8 (住院押金和住院用品（其中8克朗记入债务）); health 0.05999999999999994 (父亲住进医院得到治疗); mood -0.06000000000000005; flag +parent_hospitalized; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-10_

- **1932.11** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.sick_child_doorstep/borrows_coal_risk` tags: home, social, family, housing, injury
  Anton把自家煤箱里仅剩的四块煤分了两块给Rosa，自己家那一夜没生火，裹着大衣睡的。孩子烧退了，Rosa第二天还煤时多塞了一把粗盐。Anton当天下午就开始流清鼻涕，第三天咳得直不起腰。
  _effects: health -0.08000000000000002 (受凉咳嗽); food; relation friend with person-phase15-Rosa-world-vienna-1912_

- **1933.01** `vienna-life-depression/laid-off` tags: adulthood, depression, work, unemployment
  厂里把一张短工名单贴在厂门口。Anton的名字在第三行，旁边写着“下周等通知”。等到月底，通知没有来，工票也停了。
  _effects: unemployment (laid off in the depression); cash -5 (lost wages); condition_

- **1933.01** `vienna-family-courtship/ask-to-share-a-home` tags: family, courtship, household, social, lifecycle
  Anton在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang靠窗的桌边，把一只空咖啡杯转了半圈，说房租两人合付、桌子两人共用。Berta Hofer的手停在糖罐盖上，问了一句“要是过不下去呢”，没有当场答应，只说要想一想；杯里的咖啡凉了，话还悬在桌面上。
  _effects: interaction; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-30_

- **1933.01** `r13-fam.reconcile-parent::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/reconciled` tags: task, family, parent, reconciliation, estrangement, reform, medical, schooling
  Anton把带来的面包放在窗台上，Josefine Hofer正在削一块木头，刨花落在鞋面上。两人先说了几句天气，Anton才提起那年摔门的事。Josefine Hofer没有抬头，只把刨子递过来，让Anton替他磨一磨；磨刀石响起来时，谁也没有再提旧账。
  _effects: flag +reconciled_with_parent; role; relation kin with person-josefine-hofer; knowledge “the parent kept the old plane sharpened”_

- **1933.06** `vienna-historical-social-movement.district-list/demand-open-books` tags: history, historical_process, social_movement, organization, politics
  负责人说这是不信任，Anton回说数目对得上才叫信任。Friedrich Hofer把卷角的账页压平，指着一行重复的支出，屋里安静得能听见煤气灯嘶声。最后决定下次集会再报账。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-friedrich-hofer; reputation 0.039999999999999994; skill_

- **1933.09** `p9-gap-adult-solitude.remake-coat/remake-coat.short-jacket` tags: adulthood, daily, solitude, ordinary, home, mending, clothing, household
  Anton把外套的肩线拆开，往里收了四指，袖口剪掉一截，改出一件到胯骨的短上衣。剩下的布头拼成两只手套，针脚粗，戴着能挡风。她在镜子前转了一圈，肩还是有点空，但总算能穿出门。
  _effects: skill; mood 0.050000000000000044_

- **1934.01** `interaction_response` tags: interaction, partnership, household
  Berta Hofer在几天后找到Anton，说可以试试。两人开始合付房租，也把各自欠的账摊在桌上。
  _effects: relation partner with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-30; household; role; role; interaction_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:005030_

- **1934.01** `p9-work-jobsearch.ask-trial/ask-trial.accepted` tags: work, job_search, negotiation, wages, employment, task, trial, factory
  厂门口的砖墙被太阳晒得发烫。Josef Lechner在旁听着，插了一句这后生看着手稳。工头把烟灰弹进阴沟，说试半天，午饭他管。Anton点头时，肚子先响了一声。
  _effects: flag +trial_work_pending; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-71; mood 0.050000000000000044; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-71_

- **1934.04** `r2-rural.flood-meadow::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/cellar-flooded` tags: rural, flood, neighbor, institution, land, vienna, urban, crisis
  Friedrich Hofer划着小船来发沙袋，说堤上的缺口不归村里管。Anton把沙袋堆在自家门口，水还是从墙根渗进来，地窖里的土豆全泡了。第二天量水位，墙上的泥线比去年高出一掌。
  _effects: cash -7 (河滩上的水印); flag +flooded_cellar; relation rival with person-friedrich-hofer_

- **1934.07** `p9-gap-adult-solitude.window-argument/window-argument.closed` tags: adulthood, daily, solitude, ordinary, home, neighbor, overheard, injury
  Anton把窗关上，争吵声立刻变成闷响，像隔着一层棉被。可那男人的一句话还是从缝里钻进来，她躺到床上翻来覆去，数着墙上的水渍，到很晚才睡着。
  _effects: health -0.030000000000000027 (被楼下争吵搅得没睡好); mood -0.030000000000000027_

- **1935.01** `vienna-life-injury-recovery/clinic-treatment` tags: health, work, family
  Anton在受伤后的第三天去了诊所。医生清洗伤口、换药，收下诊费，嘱咐不要急着回机器旁。Anton把药方折好，回家路上买了纱布。
  _effects: health 0.050000000000000044 (wound treatment); cash -8 (clinic and bandages); condition_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:005549_

- **1935.01** `state_tick:raising-child/school-year/learns` tags: raising-child, family, child, care, school, household, state, state_tick
  Anton把Mitzi Bauer的旧课本包上书皮，又在本子上签了名。Mitzi Bauer今年学会了自己系鞋带，也学会了在饭桌上说“不”；Anton听着，觉得屋子比去年更满。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 20 -> 28; state_parameter raising-child.caregiverBurden 45 -> 42; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1935.02** `ml18-luck.wrong-horse::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/the-wrong-horse-wins` tags: gambling, luck, money, horse_race, absurd, windfall, vienna, urban
  Anton把“蓝多瑙”看成了“蓝星期一”，在柜台前把五克朗押在一匹没人看好的马上。最后一道弯，那匹马从外道切进来，Josef Berg 3手里的铅笔停在票面上。赔率是一赔十二；Berta Hofer把赢来的钱数了两遍，Anton还在看自己的票，像不相信上面的名字。
  _effects: cash 60 (winnings from the wrong horse at long odds); state_add ml18-sudden-fortune state:world-vienna-1912:304; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-84; reputation 0.03; flag +wrong_horse_won; mood 0.08999999999999997; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-84; flag +sudden_fortune; reputation 0.020000000000000004; mood 0.030000000000000027_

- **1935.06** `oj18-cinema.reel::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/show-the-reel` tags: cinema, film, projection, night, censorship, work, odd_job, vienna
  Anton没有剪，让那卷新闻片继续走。观众先以为这是加映，直到有人站起来喊“这不是我们买的票”。警察在散场前堵住侧门；Sozialdemokratische Arbeiterpartei Österreichs (SDAP)说是放映员接错了本，Anton被记了名字。
  _effects: reputation 0.020000000000000004; cash -2 (lost wages and paid a fine); condition; state_add oj18-odd-apprenticeship state:world-vienna-1912:305; mood -0.030000000000000027; flag +showed_banned_reel; knowledge “那卷新闻片里有一张被按下去的脸，散场前警察就堵住了侧门。”; role; flag +odd_job_apprentice_

- **1935.06** `r3-crime.neighborhood-patrol::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement::party-men-at-the-lamp/beating-at-the-corner` tags: crime, neighborhood, patrol, violence, community, vienna, urban, crisis
  巡逻队把那个收破烂的老人按在墙上，说他半夜撬门。Anton看见老人手里只有一根铁钩，想上去拦，Gustav Winter拉住他的袖子，说“别管”。Josef Lechner的警棍落下去时，马灯晃了一下，墙上的影子也跟着晃。第二天，老人没有再来这条街。
  _effects: mood -0.19999999999999996; reputation -0.05; condition; flag +saw_patrol_beat_an_innocent_man; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-12; knowledge “the old ragpicker was beaten for carrying a hook”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-12; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-75_

- **1936.01** `state_reaction:oj18-odd-apprenticeship/the-thing-in-the-basement` tags: oj18-odd-apprenticeship, work, odd_job, apprenticeship, night, trust, secrecy, state
  夜班结束前，Anton听见地下室传来拖拽声。下去时，门开着，地上只有一道湿拖把的痕迹；第二天，所有人都说那间屋子锁了十年。
  _effects: state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.strangeKnowledge 30 -> 38; state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.trust 20 -> 17; mood -0.030000000000000027; knowledge “地下室那扇门后面有东西被搬动过，但巡查簿上没有记录。”_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:006517_

- **1936.01** `state_reaction:ml18-sudden-fortune/a-relative-remembers` tags: ml18-sudden-fortune, money, windfall, sudden_fortune, household, luck, inheritance, lottery
  一个多年没走动的亲戚提着一盒点心上门，先问Anton的身体，再问那笔钱是怎么来的。临走时他说起小时候替Anton家垫过一冬天的煤钱，数字记不清了，但意思很清楚。
  _effects: state_parameter ml18-sudden-fortune.claims 2 -> 3; flag +windfall_claim_by_relative; mood -0.020000000000000018_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:006520_

- **1936.01** `vienna-family-fatherhood/child-born-at-home` tags: family, childbirth, death, lifecycle, birth, injury, state, state:raising-child
  Berta Hofer在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang生下一个孩子，取名Rosa Bauer。Anton在厨房和走廊之间来回，接生的人让烧水、拿布、付六克朗；孩子天亮后睡着了，Anton从此在户籍表上多了“父亲”一栏，手背上还留着烫水壶的红印。
  _effects: birth female; health -0.03999999999999997 (childbirth); cash -6 (midwife fee); role; state_add raising-child state:world-vienna-1912:339_

  星期天的晚饭多摆了一副刀叉。Hedwig带着一本记账簿来，说钱是大家的运气，不该只落在一个人家里。Anton把二十四克朗分成三份，Hedwig数了两遍；Berta Hofer把烤肉端上桌，谁也没有再提那本账簿。
  _effects: cash -24 (the windfall was divided among the family); state_parameter ml18-sudden-fortune.principal 10 -> 0; state_parameter ml18-sudden-fortune.claims 3 -> 5; flag +fortune_split_equally; mood 0.020000000000000018_

  Anton把Grete Bauer的旧课本包上书皮，又在本子上签了名。Grete Bauer今年学会了自己系鞋带，也学会了在饭桌上说“不”；Anton听着，觉得屋子比去年更满。

  钱在一年里薄了一层：屋顶要修，姐夫来借，彩票站的老主顾说有个稳赚的买卖。Anton把账本翻到最后一页，发现写下的支出比记得的多出四克朗；窗外有人在敲铁皮，声音像在催账。
  _effects: state_parameter ml18-sudden-fortune.principal 0 -> 0; state_parameter ml18-sudden-fortune.spent 0 -> 5; cash -1 (the account does not balance); mood -0.020000000000000018_

  楼上住户投诉夜里有人拖椅子，管事的把巡查簿翻到Anton的那一页，说签字太潦草，看不出巡过几趟。Anton想辩解，却想起那晚确实在楼梯间多站了一刻钟；扣掉的工钱刚好够买一双不漏水的鞋。

  麻疹先从耳后起，后来全身都是红点。Anton把窗帘拉上，怕光刺着孩子的眼睛；Berta Hofer从药房带回一包退热粉，Rosa Bauer喝下去就吐了。药房的人说退热粉不能多用，Berta Hofer还是买了第二包。下葬那天下着雨，Anton把一只小鞋留在坟边，鞋底还沾着屋里的煤灰。

  Luise Stein把Anton的失业证明翻过来，对着灯看水印，又在名册上找到前一份工作的注销章。Anton领到五克朗，钱是新印的，边角还粘在一起。Mitzi Kral 2在门口等，问够不够交房租，Anton说够买面包，房租再想办法。
  _effects: cash 5 (救济窗口的第九十七号); flag +received_unemployment_benefit; role; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-4; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-4_

- **1937.01** `state_tick:raising-child/school-year/struggles` tags: raising-child, family, child, care, school, household, state, state_tick
  Anton被叫到学校，老师说Mitzi Bauer这学期总在课上看窗外。回家的路上，Anton没有骂，只把买好的铅笔又数了一遍；晚饭时，两个人都吃得很少。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 28 -> 23; state_parameter raising-child.caregiverBurden 57 -> 62; cash -2 (extra lessons and replacement pencils（其中2克朗记入债务）)_

- **1937.01** `state_tick:ml18-sudden-fortune/fortune-year/money-keeps-working` tags: ml18-sudden-fortune, money, windfall, sudden_fortune, household, luck, inheritance, lottery
  一年过去，钱没有变多，却也没有一下花完。Anton把一部分存进邮局储蓄所，一部分借给楼下的鞋匠；鞋匠每月送来一双补好的鞋，利息就夹在鞋垫下面。街口的杂货商开始把好面粉留到Anton来的时候。
  _effects: cash 2 (interest and small repayments on the windfall); state_parameter ml18-sudden-fortune.claims 5 -> 6; mood 0.010000000000000009_

  一年过去，Anton已经能闭着眼说出哪块地板会响、哪盏煤气灯该在几点关。师傅把备用钥匙交下来，没有解释为什么第三把钥匙磨得最亮；Anton也没有问，只在巡查簿最后一页画了一个小小的记号。
  _effects: state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.trust 9 -> 14; state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.strangeKnowledge 38 -> 43; state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.nightsWorked 0 -> 12; cash 1 (night-shift wages); skill_

  舅父在里屋翻账簿半天没出来，舅母趁空从围裙里掏出一卷私房钱，数出十克朗塞给他，说到了家乡先给她写封信。他把钱收下，出门时在门口站了一会儿，才把帽子戴正。
  _effects: cash 10 (舅母私下给的路费); relation kin with person-berta-weiss; mood 0.030000000000000027; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-26; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-60_

  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 28 -> 31; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1937.03** `r1-fam.missing-relative::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/missing.unresolved` tags: family, missing, migration, letters, grief, vienna, urban, crisis
  Friedrich Hofer说没有死亡证明，就不能算死了，也不能领任何东西。Anton拿回一张盖了“查无此人”的纸，在邮局门口站了一会儿。回家后，他把弟弟的房间收拾干净，床单换了新的；母亲问是不是有消息了，他说还没有。
  _effects: cash -2 (邮局没有回音); flag +missing_relative_unresolved; role; knowledge “没有死亡证明，家里什么也领不到。”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-79_

- **1937.12** `vienna-church-community.tenement-prayer/rent-prayer-argument` tags: faith, social, family, home
  有人抱怨守夜人太多，踩坏了楼梯木板。楼里的人围着看，谁也没先走开。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-josef-bauer; reputation -0.03_

- **1938.01** `vienna-life-injury-recovery/clinic-treatment` tags: health, work, family
  Anton在受伤后的第三天去了诊所。医生清洗伤口、换药，收下诊费，嘱咐不要急着回机器旁。Anton把药方折好，回家路上买了纱布。
  _effects: health 0.05 (wound treatment); cash -8 (clinic and bandages（其中8克朗记入债务）)_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:007188_

  Luise Hofer在街口设了登记桌，要求住户交出姓名、职业和宗教。Anton排队填了表，回家把门牌擦干净。楼里有人连夜搬走，也有人站在阳台上挥手。
  _effects: role; cash -3 (new permits and stamps（其中3克朗记入债务）); condition; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-91_

- **1938.01** `p10-migration-tasks.move-district/move-stay-and-bargain` tags: adulthood, migration, housing, family, travel, task, moving, district
  Anton最终没有搬，把箱子又拖回楼上，跟旧房东坐在厨房桌边谈了半个钟头。按月付、煤账分两次清，房东要他每周扫一次楼道。孩子不用换学校，母亲的药也还在原来的药房抓，只是房租每月多了八十赫勒。
  _effects: cash -3 (按月先付一部分房租与旧欠煤账（其中3克朗记入债务）); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-89; mood -0.030000000000000027; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-89; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-19_

- **1938.03** `r13-rel.parental-objection::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/counted-the-repairs` tags: relationship, courtship, family, conflict, marriage, vienna, urban, crisis
  Anton没有吵，只把Berta Hofer这些年在Anton家修过的门窗一一数给Friedrich Hofer听。Friedrich Hofer沉着脸抽完一袋烟，最后让Berta Hofer进屋吃饭。桌上多摆了一副碗筷，谁也没有先动。
  _effects: cash -3 (长辈把门闩上（其中3克朗记入债务）); role; relation partner with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-30; relation kin with person-friedrich-hofer_

- **1938.08** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.lost_room_key/key_stolen_by_stranger` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Anton记得出门时钥匙还在，回来却没了。Luise Stein说下午看见一个穿灰外套的男人在楼道里转悠，手里晃着一把铜钥匙。两人下楼查看，房门虚掩，抽屉被拉开，攒着买煤的六克朗不见了。Luise Stein陪着Anton去街角警察岗亭报了案，警察记下灰外套，说会留意。
  _effects: cash -6 (被盗的买煤钱（其中6克朗记入债务）); relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-4; mood -0.050000000000000044_

- **1938.10** `r13-turn.addiction::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/sought-the-doctor` tags: health, addiction, alcohol, family, work, vienna, urban, crisis
  Anton在上班时手抖得拿不住锉刀，被工头送去门诊。医生说再喝下去肝会坏，给了一张戒酒所的地址。Anton去了，门口排队的人比想象中多。轮到时，Anton说自己只是睡不着，医生说，那就先从今晚不喝开始。
  _effects: cash -3 (酒馆后门的第二瓶（其中3克朗记入债务）); health -0.04 (酒馆后门的第二瓶); condition; flag +sought_help_for_drinking; role; relation friend with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000680:child; knowledge “the clinic offers a supervised withdrawal programme”_

  Anton和房东把租金讲定，每月少三克朗，窗户自己修。Luise Kral 2搭手搬箱子，Maria Berg 2帮着把玻璃裁好装上；省下的钱买了煤、一包铁钉和一扇旧窗框。孩子们争了半晚上，最后按个头排了铺位，朝北的墙上还渗着冷气。

- **1939.07** `vienna-social-movement.meeting/speak-and-organize` tags: development, social_movement, organization, meeting
  Anton在后屋的会议上报出自己街区的名字：谁家缺煤，谁被房东催租，谁知道哪间印刷厂还在招人。圣马克斯殡葬合作社把这些事记进一本旧账本，散会时每个人带走一叠更小的纸条。
  _effects: skill; reputation 0.020000000000000004_

- **1939.10** `vienna-medical-treatment.toothache/dentist-pull` tags: health, medical, family, money
  钳子夹住牙根时，Anton听见自己太阳穴里咯的一声。医生往牙槽里塞了团浸药棉，叫咬住别松。下楼时Alois Lechner扶着胳膊，说嘴里的血味比啤酒苦多了。

  市场里的菜摊一字排开，Hedwig把十五克鲁泽排在掌心，先买了土豆，又去盐摊上要了一小包。摊主用旧报纸把盐裹好，Hedwig把它塞进围裙内袋，土豆兜在身前。回家时Josefine Hofer接过铜板一枚枚数，土豆一颗没少。
  _effects: food; cash -0.15 (市场花销（其中0.15克朗记入债务）); relation kin with person-josefine-hofer_

- **1922.11** `r1-child.change-counted-wrong::ctx-klosterneuburg-rural-1912::milieu-tenement/shortchanged-at-the-counter` tags: numeracy, shop, money, humiliation, family, rural, agriculture, small_town
  Hedwig替Josefine Hofer去买盐，找回的零钱少了一枚。Hermann Lechner说铜币掉在柜台缝里，明天来找。第二天Hedwig去，柜台缝已经被扫干净了。Hedwig站在门口算了两遍，知道自己被少找了，却说不出口。
  _effects: cash -1 (少找的一枚十字币); mood -0.08; flag +shortchanged; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-25_

- **1923.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.market-line/line-sold-out-potatoes` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, market, food
  木窗“咔”地合上，Hedwig的布袋还敞着口。伙计隔着板缝说下星期再来。Hedwig在墙根站了一会儿，把硬币排在掌心数了两遍，走到旁边土豆摊前，挑了几个小的，让摊主当面过秤。家里人晚上把钱和土豆倒在桌上，重新数了一遍。
  _effects: food; mood -0.03; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-39_

- **1923.06** `r14-soc.patron-child::ctx-klosterneuburg-rural-1912::milieu-tenement/invited-to-the-garden` tags: cross_class_childhood, childhood_friendship, charity, shame, class, rural, agriculture, small_town
  Lena从铁栅栏后面招手，让Hedwig从厨房旁的侧门进去。花园里有钢琴、一只吃饱的猫和一盘没动过的奶油蛋糕。Otto Moser盯着Hedwig的鞋，Lena却把最大的那块推到Hedwig面前，说吃不完也是喂猫。那天回家，Hedwig的鞋底沾着花园里的湿土，舍不得刮掉，像把整条巷子关在门外。
  _effects: cash 1 (铁栅栏后面的奶油蛋糕); mood 0.1; flag +cross_class_pen_pal; relation friend with person-phase15-Lena-world-vienna-1912; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-28_

- **1923.09** `kernel-child.secret::ctx-klosterneuburg-rural-1912::milieu-tenement/edu-secret-keeps-pawn-ticket` tags: childhood, family, secret, poverty, knowledge, rural, agriculture, small_town
  Hedwig在床板下找鞋，摸到一张当票。票上的日期是上周，物件写着“银表”。Hedwig把票放回原处，晚饭时什么也没说。夜里Anton问怎么了，Hedwig只说没事。那张纸像一块小石子，硌在枕头下面。
  _effects: knowledge “家里把一只银表送进了当铺，当票藏在床板下。”; relation kin with person-phase15-Anton-world-vienna-1912; flag +kept_pawn_ticket_secret; mood -0.03_

- **1926.01** `agency:care_for_kin` tags: agency, agency:care_for_kin
  Hedwig留在家里照顾Lena，煮水、换布、去药房排队；夜里才想起自己还没吃饭。
  _effects: health 0.03999999999999998 (received nursing and food at home); relation kin with person-phase15-Lena-world-vienna-1912; mood 0.020000000000000004_

- **1926.05** `yf18-runaway.bundle::ctx-klosterneuburg-rural-1912::milieu-tenement/buy-the-ticket-tonight` tags: runaway, escape, family, plan, night_train, rural, agriculture, small_town
  Hedwig趁Josefine Hofer去上夜班，把储钱罐砸在窗台上。硬币滚进床缝，Hedwig趴在地上摸到最后一枚，跑去南站买了一张最便宜的单程票；售票员问要不要往返，Hedwig说不用，声音比自己想象中平静。
  _effects: state_add yf18-runaway-plan state:world-vienna-1912:67; cash -3 (the savings jar was broken for a ticket); flag +yf18_ticket_bought; mood 0.04; flag +yf18_runaway_plan_active_

- **1926.12** `vienna-youth-threshold.night-class/give-up-for-overtime` tags: adolescence, school, work, family, transition, injury
  工头说这个月赶印一批账簿，晚上加班每小时多给二十赫勒。Hedwig站在教室门口听完课程表，转身回厂里搬纸。Franz Lechner追出来说座位可以保留两周，Hedwig点点头，手已经摸上了作坊的门把，夜里机器的声音把课本的事盖了过去。
  _effects: cash 1.6 (夜间加班多挣的工钱); skill; mood -0.04; health -0.03999999999999998 (连续夜间加班)_

- **1927.01** `state_reaction:yf18-runaway-plan/a-family-question` tags: yf18-runaway-plan, escape, youth, plan, home, travel, risk, state
  饭桌上又为房租吵起来。Hedwig把面包掰成很小的块，听见父亲说“这个家养不起闲人”；Hedwig没有回嘴，只在心里把格拉茨的地址又背了一遍。
  _effects: state_parameter yf18-runaway-plan.suspicion 15 -> 23; state_parameter yf18-runaway-plan.resolve 70 -> 68_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:002574_

- **1927.01** `yf18-runaway.companion::ctx-klosterneuburg-rural-1912::milieu-tenement/find-another-companion` tags: runaway, friendship, companion, plan, betrayal, rural, agriculture, small_town
  Hedwig在车站遇见一个也要去格拉茨的年轻女工，两人合买了一张双人票，便宜两成。Lena知道后说Hedwig早就想甩下自己；Hedwig没有解释，只把新同伴的地址写在旧信封背面。
  _effects: state_parameter yf18-runaway-plan.preparation 35 -> 45; state_parameter yf18-runaway-plan.suspicion 23 -> 31; state_parameter yf18-runaway-plan.resolve 68 -> 73; cash -1 (a shared ticket and a platform tea); relation friend with person-phase15-Lena-world-vienna-1912; flag +yf18_new_companion; mood 0.010000000000000002_

- **1927.01** `state_tick:yf18-runaway-plan/three-months-of-preparation/someone-counts-the-savings` tags: yf18-runaway-plan, escape, youth, plan, home, travel, risk, state
  Hedwig藏在糖罐后面的硬币少了一小叠。父亲说家里这个月煤钱不够，先拿去用了；Hedwig看着空出来的那圈灰，什么也没说。那天晚上，Hedwig第一次认真算了一笔账：从维也纳到格拉茨，到底要攒几个冬天。
  _effects: state_parameter yf18-runaway-plan.suspicion 31 -> 46; state_parameter yf18-runaway-plan.resolve 73 -> 67; mood -0.03_

- **1928.01** `yf18-runaway.cousin-letter::ctx-klosterneuburg-rural-1912::milieu-tenement/forge-the-permit` tags: runaway, letter, cousin, work, plan, rural, agriculture, small_town
  Hedwig在市政厅的表格上替Josefine Hofer签了名，又把日期往前挪了三天。办事员看了Hedwig一眼，问父亲怎么没来；Hedwig说他病在床上。印章落下去时，Hedwig的手心全是汗，出门才发现自己把回执捏成了两半。
  _effects: state_add yf18-runaway-plan state:world-vienna-1912:110; cash -2 (stamps and a clerk's fee); flag +yf18_forged_permit; reputation -0.010000000000000002; mood 0.010000000000000002_

- **1928.01** `state_expire:yf18-runaway-plan` tags: yf18-runaway-plan, escape, youth, plan, home, travel, risk, state
  Hedwig的「床底下的包袱」到期；相关安排结束。
  _effects: state_remove yf18-runaway-plan state:world-vienna-1912:67; flag -yf18_runaway_plan_active_

- **1928.01** `p9-childhood-youth-aspiration.join-a-club/worked-for-dues` tags: adolescence, education, aspiration, school, books, club, exam, task
  Hedwig翻遍口袋只有几枚赫勒，便跟管事的说愿替大家抄歌本抵会费。他坐在窗边抄了一下午，手指被墨水染黑，换来的借书权让他捧回一本讲齿轮的旧书，书页里夹着上一个人留下的铅笔字。
  _effects: skill; skill; relation friend with person-phase15-Lena-world-vienna-1912; flag +workers_youth_work_trade; mood 0.029999999999999995_

- **1928.09** `yf18-runaway.mother-bundle::ctx-klosterneuburg-rural-1912::milieu-tenement/tell-the-truth` tags: runaway, family, discovery, lie, plan, rural, agriculture, small_town
  Josefine Hofer把包袱放在桌上，问Hedwig是不是打算走。Hedwig说是，又说不出为什么；Josefine Hofer没有打，只把包袱里的衬衫一件件叠好，说格拉茨的冬天比维也纳冷。那天夜里，Hedwig听见Josefine Hofer在厨房里数钱，数到很晚。
  _effects: state_parameter yf18-runaway-plan.suspicion 20 -> 40; state_parameter yf18-runaway-plan.resolve 65 -> 70; state_parameter yf18-runaway-plan.preparation 60 -> 65; relation kin with person-josefine-hofer; flag +yf18_parent_knows_plan; mood -0.02_

  饭桌上又为房租吵起来。Hedwig把面包掰成很小的块，听见父亲说“这个家养不起闲人”；Hedwig没有回嘴，只在心里把格拉茨的地址又背了一遍。

- **1929.01** `state_tick:yf18-runaway-plan/three-months-of-preparation/route-memorised` tags: yf18-runaway-plan, escape, youth, plan, home, travel, risk, state
  Hedwig把去车站的路又走了一遍，记住了哪一段有煤气灯、哪一段住着巡夜的警察。回家后，Hedwig在旧日历背面画了一条线，标出换乘的站名；那条线短得可笑，却比课本上的任何地图都清楚。
  _effects: state_parameter yf18-runaway-plan.preparation 65 -> 77; state_parameter yf18-runaway-plan.suspicion 48 -> 50; mood 0.009999999999999995_

- **1929.01** `agency:care_for_kin` tags: agency, agency:care_for_kin
  Hedwig想照顾Josef Weiss，但工作脱不开身，只能托邻居带一锅汤回去。
  _effects: mood -0.039999999999999994_

- **1929.07** `r13-turn.exam-failure::ctx-klosterneuburg-rural-1912::milieu-tenement/retake-fee` tags: education, exam, failure, school, work, rural, agriculture, small_town
  成绩单上差两分。Franz Lechner说可以补考，但要先交一克朗卷子费，还得让Josefine Hofer来签字。Josefine Hofer在办公室门口把帽子捏变了形，说家里拿不出这份钱。Hedwig把错题抄到烟盒背面，第二天照样去上课，只是不再举手。
  _effects: cash -1 (落榜名单上的第三个名字); mood -0.15; condition; flag +failed_entrance_exam; skill; relation guardian with person-josefine-hofer_

- **1930.01** `state_expire:yf18-runaway-plan` tags: yf18-runaway-plan, escape, youth, plan, home, travel, risk, state
  Hedwig的「床底下的包袱」到期；相关安排结束。
  _effects: state_remove yf18-runaway-plan state:world-vienna-1912:110_

- **1930.01** `kernel-social.lending::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/lending.borrowed-instead` tags: task, task:social, social, lending, debt, neighbor, money, everyday
  Hedwig没有借出钱，反向Anna Moser借了四克朗，说下个月发薪就还。Anna Moser犹豫了一会儿，还是从围裙里摸出硬币；Hedwig把数目写在日历上，每天路过都看一眼。日历上那一格被铅笔圈了又圈。
  _effects: cash 4 (borrowed four kronen from the neighbour); relation acquaintance with person-anna-moser; role; flag +in_debt; knowledge “欠邻居四克朗，下个月发薪日还。”_

- **1930.02** `vienna-courtship-courtyard.family-table/nod-and-pots` tags: adulthood, courtship, partner, social, family, partnership
  汤喝到一半，Josefine Hofer放下勺子点了头，但说得很清楚：先攒出一套锅碗、一只挂钟，再把话往下说。Katharina Huber在桌下用膝盖碰了碰Hedwig，Josefine Hofer把最后一块煮牛肉推过来，饭桌上的空气总算松了；这一顿让对方家里管了饭，自家灶上省下两克朗的吃食，也吃出一个带条件的准信。
  _effects: relation partner with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-70; relation kin with person-josefine-hofer; food; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-70_

- **1930.04** `vienna-parish-aid.sick-call/sick-call.door-only` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  Hedwig把药包和半块肥皂交给开门的孩子，嘱咐用水冲开、一天两次，就下了楼。楼梯间里孩子一直站在门口看着，手里攥着纸包，没关门。Hedwig走到二层又折回去，帮他把门带上，说自己家里也等着火。
  _effects: health 0.010000000000000009 (没有久留，避开了病人的屋子); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-4; mood -0.03; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-4_

- **1930.06** `major-work-first-employment/turned-away` tags: work, employment, unemployment, major
  Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne的工头翻完工票，问Hedwig上一个东家是谁。Hedwig说厂子关了，工头又问有没有辞工证明；没有证明，名册就不能写。后面的人挤上来，Hedwig被推到台阶下面。中午的钟响了，Hedwig站在街对面，看新来的人把名字一个个写上去。
  _effects: cash -2 (carfare and waiting); reputation -0.03; flag +no_work_references; role; mood -0.06999999999999999; relation acquaintance with person-otto-winter_

- **1930.08** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.lost_room_key/key_found_by_neighbor` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Hedwig傍晚从面粉厂下工回来，发现挂在腰带上的房门钥匙不见了，在楼梯间来回找了四趟。Karl Kraus在二楼拐角的煤堆旁捡到那把铜钥匙，钥匙上缠着红线，一眼就认出来。Karl Kraus敲开Hedwig的门，把钥匙拍在桌上，说自己眼神好使。
  _effects: relation friend with person-karl-kraus; mood 0.05_

- **1931.01** `curse15-betrayal.name-on-the-list::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/sold-the-letter` tags: betrayal, politics, investigation, curse, denunciation, secret, reform, medical
  Gustav Winter把Hedwig托付保管的一封旧信卖给了收旧纸的人。信里提到几个已经不用的名字，买主又把信转给了另一个买主。Hedwig再见到那封信时，它被压在一块玻璃下面，标价是Hedwig半个月的工钱。
  _effects: cash -1 (买回自己的旧信); reputation -0.05; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-12; knowledge “我写的一封旧信被人压在玻璃下面标价出售。”; flag +private_letter_sold_

- **1931.01** `state_reaction:cursed-by-wish/curse-betrayal` tags: cursed-by-wish, wish, curse, supernatural, revenge, special, state, state_reaction
  背叛发生后的夜里，Hedwig听见墙里有一声很轻的响。第二天，Gustav Winter病倒了；没有人把这两件事放在一起。
  _effects: condition; state_parameter cursed-by-wish.triggers 0 -> 1; mood 0.01999999999999999_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:004071_

- **1931.01** `r13-fam.repair-friendship::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/cup-mended` tags: task, social, friendship, repair, trust, reform, medical, schooling
  Hedwig把那只摔掉瓷的杯子放在Friedrich Hofer面前，杯口用锡焊过，焊痕发黑。Friedrich Hofer没有接，只问还记不记得为什么吵。Hedwig把那天的话原样说了一遍，说到一半停住，承认有一句是自己编的。Friedrich Hofer这才把杯子转过来，看杯底的名字。
  _effects: cash -1 (粘回去的搪瓷杯); flag +repaired_friendship; role; relation friend with person-friedrich-hofer; knowledge “the quarrel began with a lie about money”_

- **1932.01** `major-family.proposal-refused/proposal-refused.bad-blood` tags: family, courtship, refusal, major, lifecycle
  Ferdinand Hofer的母亲把Hedwig带来的点心原封退回，说家里不娶有肺病亲戚的人。点心是早上排队买的，油纸还热着，Hedwig把它抱在胸前。Ferdinand Hofer站在门后没有出来，只让妹妹传话。Hedwig把戒指留在门房，走回Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang时天还没黑，楼下的狗一直叫。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-73; role; flag +marriage_refused; cash -2 (pastries and the returned ring（其中2克朗记入债务）); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-73_

- **1932.01** `vienna-family-courtship/wait-for-a-better-year` tags: family, courtship, household, social, lifecycle
  Hedwig把话咽回去，只说等明年工钱稳一点再谈。Gustav Bauer没有追问，两人把桌上的面包分完，各自去上工；出门时Hedwig回头看了一眼，Gustav Bauer正把掉在桌上的面包屑拢进掌心，谁也没有把那个话头重新拾起来。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-62; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-62_

- **1932.01** `p9-work-jobsearch.ask-trial/ask-trial.no-vacancy` tags: work, job_search, negotiation, wages, employment, task, trial, factory
  Friedrich Winter 2没有接话，先从抽屉里撕下一角纸。Hedwig把名字、住址和手艺一样样说完，工头边写边应，写完把纸压回抽屉，说月底来问。
  _effects: mood -0.03999999999999998; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-48; flag +seeking_work; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-48; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-74_

- **1932.07** `p9-social-family-conflict-care.sick-parent-night/hospital-deposit` tags: family, care, siblings, inheritance, housing, money
  Hedwig跑了一趟医院，把Friedrich Hofer送进病房：押金三克朗，搪瓷杯和旧毛巾一克朗二十赫勒，头三天的药费三克朗八十赫勒，一共八克朗。护士收走便盆，换上带刻度的玻璃壶。Hedwig回到空了的床边，把塌下去的椅子扶正，才发现自己一整天只喝了一杯咖啡。
  _effects: cash -8 (住院押金和住院用品（其中8克朗记入债务）); health 0.06000000000000005 (父亲住进医院得到治疗); mood -0.06; flag +parent_hospitalized; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-5_

- **1932.08** `df18-dream.water-in-the-cellar::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/ignore-the-dream` tags: dream, omen, home, water, coincidence, eerie, everyday, reform
  Hedwig醒后只当是晚饭太咸，翻个身又睡了。下午，地窖的水管爆开，煤和旧箱子泡在齐膝的水里。Emil Lechner 2帮着舀水，Hedwig一边舀一边想起梦里的水位，正好到第二个台阶。
  _effects: cash -4 (泡坏的煤和旧箱子（其中4克朗记入债务）); health -0.030000000000000027 (在冷水里舀了一下午); condition; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-67; knowledge “我没有理会梦，地窖真的淹了。”; flag +df18_ignored_flood_dream; mood -0.03999999999999998; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-67_

- **1933.01** `vienna-life-injury-recovery/home-care` tags: health, work, family, injury
  Hedwig没有去诊所，只用热水和旧布洗了伤口。第二天伤口周围发红，家里人把仅剩的干净床单撕成条。
  _effects: health -0.03 (untreated wound); cash -2 (clean cloth（其中2克朗记入债务）); condition_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:004523_

- **1933.01** `vienna-market-household.market-credit/market-credit.delay` tags: home, market, food, social
  三个克朗拖了九天。玛丽亚收下时数都没数，只把Hedwig的名字从木牌上抹去，粉笔灰落在卷心菜上。Katharina Huber拎着篮子从旁边过去，没停脚。
  _effects: cash -3 (延迟偿还市场赊账（其中3克朗记入债务）); food; reputation -0.049999999999999996; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-70_

- **1933.01** `p9-social-relationship-initiative.cut-the-guarantee/pay-small-part` tags: courtship, friendship, family, social, initiative, housing, task, task:social
  替他还了四克朗，剩下的数目不碰，条件是把赌债字据交出来，当天就去戒酒会挂个名。对方把字据给了，人却没去挂名。四克朗从工资袋里出去，母亲那瓶药只好再等十天，晚上咳得更紧。
  _effects: cash -4 (替友人偿还部分赌债（其中4克朗记入债务）); health -0.03 (母亲的药钱被挪去还债，家里几夜没睡好); relation friend with person-franz-lechner; mood -0.04999999999999999; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-14_

- **1933.07** `vienna-courtship-courtyard.family-table/leave-before-dessert` tags: adulthood, courtship, partner, social, family, partnership
  问到后来，Josefine Hofer提起别家有个更稳当的差事。Mitzi Kral 2把餐巾往桌上一放，说吃好了，先走。两人在楼梯间站住，Mitzi Kral 2说不是Hedwig的错，可下次再来还得一样坐这张桌子；Hedwig心里松了一点，也知道这顿饭没吃完，事情并没有过去。
  _effects: relation partner with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-71; relation kin with person-josefine-hofer; mood 0.030000000000000027; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-71_

- **1934.01** `vienna-family-divorce/partner-moves-to-shared-housing` tags: family, marriage, divorce, housing, lifecycle, separation, household
  Mitzi Kral 2把一只箱子和工票搬去合租房，带走一半锅碗。Hedwig留在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang，把空出来的床租给一个学徒，用租金补房租；搬家花掉四克朗，Hedwig在户籍上成了分居的人，夜里听见隔壁床板响，总要愣一下。
  _effects: relation_end partner with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-71; household; cash -4 (moving and separate goods（其中4克朗记入债务）); mood -0.06; role_

- **1934.01** `task-vienna-family-care.ask-relative-money/flour-and-oil` tags: family, care, courtship, siblings, inheritance, housing, task, task:family
  Berta Weiss听完没有去开抽屉，只说钱都压在铺子的货上，转手从厨房拎出一袋面粉和半瓶油，让Hedwig带回去。Hedwig在门口站了一会儿，道谢时嗓子发干，下楼时那袋面粉压着胳膊，回去的路比来时沉。
  _effects: food; mood -0.04999999999999999; relation kin with person-berta-weiss_

- **1934.08** `vienna-legacy-worklife.hours-ledger/hours-ledger.read-for-others` tags: legacy, work, family, reform, ordinary_life
  铅笔头短得捏不住，Hedwig把它夹在两指之间，在表格背面写下两个名字。被漏掉的人一个叫扬，一个叫弗兰茨，都是每天清早从火车站走来的临时工。工头看了一眼背面，没说话，把表贴回墙上。
  _effects: skill; relation colleague with person-phase15-Emil-world-vienna-1912; reputation 0.04_

- **1934.11** `vienna-historical-social-movement.district-list/hold-list-and-watch` tags: history, historical_process, social_movement, organization, politics
  楼梯间的灯泡忽明忽暗，Hedwig让Alois Lechner记下今晚进出院子的人。名单留在桌上没动，像一件没人认领的活。快到午夜，Alois Lechner只说了一句，明早再核一遍。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-alois-lechner; mood -0.030000000000000027; flag +kept_district_list_private_

- **1935.01** `curse15-notice.borrowed-mourning::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/sells-the-mourning` tags: curse, money, fear, client, ritual, supernatural, work, vienna
  Karl Kraus请Hedwig去家里坐一夜，说只要Hedwig在灵堂待到天亮，那个欠钱的人就会自己上门。Hedwig收了钱，在灵堂坐到天亮，什么也没有发生。一个月后，那个人真的来了，送来一袋面粉；Karl Kraus把面粉分给Hedwig一半，没有解释。
  _effects: cash 3 (替人守灵的酬劳); reputation -0.029999999999999992; relation acquaintance with person-karl-kraus; flag +sold_mourning_service; condition_

- **1935.01** `p9-social-daily-culture.cafe-newspaper/p9-social-daily-culture.cafe-newspaper.talk-tram` tags: daily, culture, leisure, cafe, music, walk, task, task:daily
  Hedwig把报纸推到一边，和Otto Berg聊起电车票价要涨的传闻。两人拿过糖罐当算盘，在桌布上比划通勤开销，两杯咖啡四十赫勒，争论得忘了时间，直到窗外的煤气灯亮起来。Hedwig起身时发现大衣下摆沾了咖啡渍，晚饭的点早过了，却约好下回还坐这张桌子。
  _effects: cash -0.4 (两杯咖啡的价钱); mood 0.03999999999999998_

  Hedwig在告解室里把事情说了一遍，没有提名字。神父沉默了很久，只问Hedwig是否愿意把那张纸交出来。Hedwig说没有纸。走出教堂时，Hedwig发现街对面有人一直在等，手里拿着一本新的登记册。
  _effects: state_add suspected-witchcraft state:world-vienna-1912:340; reputation -0.020000000000000004; condition; mood -0.03999999999999998_

  _effects: cash -6 (配给证上的号码（其中5克朗记入债务）); food; flag +bought_black_market_ration_card; knowledge “the clerk noticed the scraped name but stamped the card anyw”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-91_

- **1936.02** `major-crisis.rent-increase/tenant-meeting` tags: crisis, housing, rent, eviction, family, poverty
  巷口洗衣房的老板娘把几张椅子摆开，叫来半条街的租户。Hedwig把涨租单摊在桌上，逐条念出上面的数字；Anna Weiss 3站在门边，既没走，也没替房东说话。大家决定先集体迟交一个月，签名纸传到Hedwig手里时，纸上已经有一块油渍。
  _effects: cash -2 (paper, ink and tram fare for the tenants meeting); role; flag +rent_strike; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-52; reputation 0.03; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-52_

  _effects: mood -0.02999999999999997; knowledge “门框上出现了我的名字和当天的日期。”; flag +df18_name_carved_on_door; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-21_

  _effects: state_parameter suspected-witchcraft.danger 30 -> 40; reputation -0.039999999999999994; cash -1 (绕远路增加的车钱（其中1克朗记入债务）); mood -0.040000000000000036_

  _effects: birth female; cash -6 (midwife fee（其中6克朗记入债务）); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-26; state_add raising-child state:world-vienna-1912:386_

  一年过去，Hedwig能在菜市场用德语讲价，也知道哪家铺子肯赊半块黄油。房东仍叫他“新来的”，但楼下的面包师已经记住了他买哪一种面包，还会把前一天剩的那只小圆面包留出来。
  _effects: state_parameter mb17-newcomer.language 19 -> 23; state_parameter mb17-newcomer.localNetwork 9 -> 14; state_parameter mb17-newcomer.housingStrain 65 -> 63; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1941.01** `agency:support_process` tags: agency, agency:support_process
  Hedwig为环城大道写字楼管理处在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsam出了力：整理名单、传递消息或替同伴作证，名字被记进参与记录。
  _effects: historical development:initiative-building:person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-5-evt:world-vienna-1912:004012-1938 as supporter; historical_influence; reputation 0.014999999999999986_

- **1941.10** `oj18-lift.stuck::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/hand-crank` tags: lift, building, work, night, passenger, odd_job, suspense, vienna
  电梯停在四楼半，门缝里只看见一截电线。Hedwig让Berta Huber 2抓紧扶手，自己摇动手柄，一格一格把轿厢放下来。门开时，Berta Huber 2的白大褂后背全湿了；他塞给Hedwig两张钞票，说手术室里还有人等着。
  _effects: organization_found 环城大道写字楼管理处 (other); position 电梯员 @building:person-phase15-Hedwig-world-vienna-1912-evt:world-vienna-1912:009543; state_add oj18-odd-apprenticeship state:world-vienna-1912:500; cash 2 (the doctor's reward); reputation 0.03; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-79; flag +lift_operator; knowledge “电梯停在四楼半时，手动柄要一格一格放，不能一次松到底。”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-79; role; flag +odd_job_apprentice_

- **1941.10** `state_reaction:mb17-newcomer/workplace-footing` tags: mb17-newcomer, migration, newcomer, language, network, housing, belonging, state
  工头让Hedwig把一箱零件搬到后间，又顺口教了他两个德语词。午休时，一个老工人把多余的汤推过来；Hedwig没有推回去，只把碗底最后一点喝干净，把空碗放回对方手边。
  _effects: state_parameter mb17-newcomer.localNetwork 6 -> 9; state_parameter mb17-newcomer.language 18 -> 19; mood 0.010000000000000009_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:009543_

- **1941.12** `p9-gap-adult-unemployed.labour-exchange/exchange-trial-work` tags: adulthood, unemployment, casual_labour, poverty, daily, labour_exchange, bureaucracy
  洗衣房里蒸汽弥漫，熨斗压在衬衫领子上咝咝作响。老板娘翻了翻Hedwig熨好的第一件，说领口还差些火候，手上倒还稳。两打熨完，三个克朗放在围裙口袋里，铜板还带着体温。
  _effects: cash 3 (洗衣房试工半天按件计酬); skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-57_

- **1942.01** `oj18-mortuary.estate::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/claimant-appears` tags: death, mortuary, inheritance, will, work, odd_job, suspense, vienna
  一个自称死者妹妹的女人Luise Berg 3找来，说铁盒里应有房契和一枚金戒指。Hedwig把遗物清单摊在桌上：只有一只旧表、三张明信片和半包烟。Luise Berg 3不信，坐在殡仪馆门口哭到天黑，最后把旧表拿走了。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-86; state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.trust 30 -> 35; knowledge “遗物清单上只有一只旧表、三张明信片和半包烟，没有房契和金戒指。”; mood -0.030000000000000027; flag +claimant_took_watch; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-86_

- **1942.01** `state_reaction:oj18-odd-apprenticeship/the-thing-in-the-basement` tags: oj18-odd-apprenticeship, work, odd_job, apprenticeship, night, trust, secrecy, state
  夜班结束前，Hedwig听见地下室传来拖拽声。下去时，门开着，地上只有一道湿拖把的痕迹；第二天，所有人都说那间屋子锁了十年。
  _effects: state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.strangeKnowledge 10 -> 18; state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.trust 35 -> 32; mood -0.030000000000000027; knowledge “地下室那扇门后面有东西被搬动过，但巡查簿上没有记录。”_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:010194_

- **1942.01** `state_tick:oj18-odd-apprenticeship/strange-year/blamed-for-the-noise` tags: oj18-odd-apprenticeship, work, odd_job, apprenticeship, night, trust, secrecy, state
  楼上住户投诉夜里有人拖椅子，管事的把巡查簿翻到Hedwig的那一页，说签字太潦草，看不出巡过几趟。Hedwig想辩解，却想起那晚确实在楼梯间多站了一刻钟；扣掉的工钱刚好够买一双不漏水的鞋。
  _effects: state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.trust 32 -> 24; state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.reliability 60 -> 55; mood -0.030000000000000027; flag +odd_job_scare_

- **1942.02** `vienna-market-debt.pawn-watch/pawn-whole-watch` tags: market, debt, money, home, shop
  你把银壳怀表推到绒布托盘上，当铺掌柜用放大镜扫了一眼机芯，报出八克朗。你盯着那张窄票上模糊的赎当日期，想起父亲在第十区工棚里上弦的样子。邻居在门外咳嗽，你捏着票和钱走出来，先拐去房东那里付了半个月的欠租。
  _effects: cash 8 (典当银壳怀表所得); mood -0.05999999999999994; flag +watch_pawned_

- **1942.08** `vienna-housing-move.rent-notice/rent-notice-pay` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money
  Hedwig把通知念给Luise Stein听，Luise Stein摇头说是楼梯间那几块新玻璃的账。Hedwig去楼下杂货铺换开一张五克朗的纸币，回屋一枚一枚摆开数，煤箱底多留了二十赫勒。
  _effects: cash -2 (按月多付两克朗房租); mood -0.030000000000000027_

- **1942.10** `bc18-inherit.forty-seven-franz::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/hearse-as-taxi` tags: inheritance, mistaken_identity, debt, funeral, work, absurd, family, vienna
  四十七个弗朗茨·穆勒里，只有Hedwig拿得出干净的房租簿。公证人Emil把遗嘱念完，遗产是一辆灵车、两匹老马和十四克朗的欠账。Hedwig没有钱下葬，先把灵车租给搬家的人；夜里跑环城大道，白天替人运钢琴。Mitzi Weiss起初等着看笑话，后来开始给他介绍生意。
  _effects: cash 5 (用灵车替人搬家的收入); role; state_add bc18-ridiculous-fame state:world-vienna-1912:535; flag +hearse_became_a_moving_van; mood 0.020000000000000018; flag +ridiculously_famous; reputation 0.020000000000000004; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1943.01** `vienna-life-injury-recovery/clinic-treatment` tags: health, work, family
  Hedwig在受伤后的第三天去了诊所。医生清洗伤口、换药，收下诊费，嘱咐不要急着回机器旁。Hedwig把药方折好，回家路上买了纱布。
  _effects: health 0.05 (wound treatment); cash -8 (clinic and bandages（其中8克朗记入债务）)_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:008350_

- **1943.01** `office16-budget.sign-or-block::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/signed-with-margin` tags: office, budget, permit, administration, power, economy, vienna, urban
  Hedwig在预算表上签了字，把冬季煤房的款项从三笔并成一笔，又给夜班守卫加了半份津贴。Maria Berg 2算出省下的钱刚好够修屋顶；没有人鼓掌，但一月的会议室不再需要穿大衣。
  _effects: reputation 0.04; skill; mood 0.020000000000000018; flag +signed_balanced_budget; knowledge “把冬季煤房的款项并成一笔后，省下的钱够修会议室屋顶。”_

- **1943.01** `state_tick:mb17-newcomer/first-year/still-a-guest` tags: mb17-newcomer, migration, newcomer, language, network, housing, belonging, state
  Hedwig又搬了一次房，箱子里多了一把缺齿的梳子和一叠没寄出的信。新住处离电车线更远，邻居说德语很快；他只能点头，等对方走后再把门关上，把今天听错的词一个一个记在旧票据背面。
  _effects: state_parameter mb17-newcomer.language 24 -> 25; state_parameter mb17-newcomer.localNetwork 17 -> 15; state_parameter mb17-newcomer.housingStrain 63 -> 67; cash -1 (the move and a new rent deposit（其中1克朗记入债务）); mood -0.030000000000000027_

  楼上住户投诉夜里有人拖椅子，管事的把巡查簿翻到Hedwig的那一页，说签字太潦草，看不出巡过几趟。Hedwig想辩解，却想起那晚确实在楼梯间多站了一刻钟；扣掉的工钱刚好够买一双不漏水的鞋。

- **1943.01** `state_tick:bc18-ridiculous-fame/ridiculous-fame-year/the-story-holds-another-year` tags: bc18-ridiculous-fame, fame, reputation, mistaken_identity, promotion, media, absurd, public
  又一年过去，Hedwig仍在被介绍成那个人物。邀请函从市郊的园艺会、女子学校和退伍军人酒馆寄来；Hedwig学会在签名后加一句模糊的谦辞，听起来像有许多往事不便细说。每一次应付都带来一点小钱，也让真正的解释更不合时宜。
  _effects: state_parameter bc18-ridiculous-fame.notoriety 25 -> 32; state_parameter bc18-ridiculous-fame.invitations 1 -> 2; cash 1 (一场小型演讲的车马费); reputation 0.01; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1943.01** `agency:support_process` tags: agency, agency:support_process
  Hedwig想帮忙，但组织者不信任新来的人，只让其在外围等消息。
  _effects: mood -0.020000000000000018_

- **1943.03** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.laundry_line_dispute/compromise_split_line` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Hedwig把洗好的床单搭上院子里的公用晾衣绳，占了整条。Maria Berg抱着一盆湿衣服下来，说自家孩子明天要穿干净衬衫上学。两人在绳子两头扯了一阵，最后Hedwig把床单对折，腾出一半，Maria Berg用木夹子把衬衫一件件排上去。谁也没道歉，但第二天见面都点了头。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000151:child; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1943.09** `r3-mig.political-exile::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/checked-in-under-false-name` tags: migration, exile, politics, safe_house, risk, vienna, urban, crisis
  Hedwig在旅馆登记簿上写了一个假名，Ferdinand Huber 2在柜台后面看了一眼，说“住三楼，别开窗”。半夜有人敲门，Hedwig从猫眼看见Mitzi站在走廊里。Mitzi说门口停着一辆没牌照的车，已经停了两个钟头。
  _effects: cash -3 (边境旅馆的假名（其中2克朗记入债务）); condition; flag +checked_in_under_false_name; relation friend with person-phase15-Mitzi-world-vienna-1912; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-124; knowledge “a car without plates is parked outside the hotel”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-124_

- **1943.11** `r1-fam.landlord-harassment::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/landlord.caught` tags: social, housing, landlord, harassment, tenants, vienna, urban, crisis
  Hermann Lechner有把万能钥匙，常在Hedwig上工时进来，把家具挪个位置，说是检查水管。有一次，Hedwig提前回来，看见他坐在床边翻抽屉。Hedwig没有喊，只把门从外面锁上，去叫了巡警；Hermann Lechner在屋里拍了半个钟头的门。
  _effects: reputation 0.05; flag +landlord_caught_searching_the_room; role; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-25; knowledge “房东有一把能开每间屋的万能钥匙。”_

- **1944.01** `office16-admin.quiet-ledger::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/nothing-moved` tags: office, administration, budget, competence, quiet, power, vienna, urban
  账本看上去是平的：修屋顶的钱被挪去补煤款，煤款又被挪去付印刷费。Hedwig在每一栏都签了字，知道明年还会这样。散会后，Franz Berg把一张催修屋顶的纸条压在台灯下面。
  _effects: reputation -0.01; flag +deferred_maintenance; knowledge “屋顶的修理费被挪去补煤款，煤款又被挪去付印刷费。”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-89_

- **1944.02** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.shared_stove_fire/fire_no_damage` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Hedwig刚把锅端下灶，火苗就蹿了起来，Hermann Lechner 2一脚踩灭地上的油布，顺手抄起沙箱里的沙子盖住灶口。两人都没受伤，但Hermann Lechner 2的鞋底烧穿一个洞，那是唯一一双出门的鞋。
  _effects: relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-95; mood -0.04999999999999999; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-95_

- **1944.06** `bc18-enemy.accidental-help::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/warehouse-sinecure` tags: enemy, mistake, work, reputation, absurd, luck, social, vienna
  Rosa把Hedwig的名字推荐给码头仓库，说那里正缺一个“能扛两百斤的人”。Hedwig去了才知道，岗位是看管一只装满旧账本的暖房，工钱三克朗，主要工作是别让老鼠咬纸。Rosa听说后，在工会门口堵了三天，问这算哪门子惩罚。
  _effects: cash 3 (仓库暖房的看管工钱); role; relation rival with person-phase15-Rosa-world-vienna-1912; flag +enemy_recommended_a_sinecure; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1944.09** `vienna-courtship-courtyard.sunday-walk/separate-coffee` tags: adulthood, courtship, partner, social, family
  两人拐进街角的咖啡馆，站到锌皮柜台前。Josef Berg 3点了牛奶咖啡，Hedwig要了黑咖啡，十二赫勒一杯。侍者把两杯推过来时，两人几乎同时把钱币按在柜台上，各付各的，谁也没说什么；那一声铜板响，把客气摆得比咖啡还清楚。
  _effects: cash -0.12 (自己那杯黑咖啡（其中0.12克朗记入债务）); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-84_

- **1945.01** `vienna-life-1945-liberation/survives` tags: war, liberation, 1945
  苏联士兵进城时，Hedwig躲在门后听见坦克履带压过石板路。几天后街口贴出告示，Hedwig去领了一份汤和一块面包。
  _effects: role; food; cash -2 (replacement documents（其中2克朗记入债务）)_

- **1945.01** `office16-admin.quiet-ledger::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/the-ledger-was-cooked` tags: office, administration, budget, competence, quiet, power, vienna, urban

  Hedwig把最后一箱书搬下楼时，Mitzi Kral 2堵在门口，说墙皮被蹭掉一块，箱子先留下。Hedwig说要赶车，Mitzi Kral 2说那就把修墙的钱付了。板车在巷口按铃，Hedwig只抱走了装证件的木盒。
  _effects: cash -2 (板车与押金收据（其中2克朗记入债务）); flag +landlord_withheld_trunk; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-71; knowledge “the landlord keeps the trunk against a wall repair claim”_

  Helene Bauer把Hedwig在夜里说过的话整理成三页纸，交到了办事处。纸上没有一句是假的，但每一句都离开了原来的房间。第二天，Hedwig被叫去问话，问话的人已经知道该问什么。Helene Bauer在走廊里遇见Hedwig，点了头，像只是借过一支笔。
  _effects: state_add suspected-witchcraft state:world-vienna-1912:658; reputation -0.06; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-19; flag +denounced_by_known_person; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-19_

  背叛发生后的夜里，Hedwig听见墙里有一声很轻的响。第二天，Helene Bauer病倒了；没有人把这两件事放在一起。
  _effects: health -0.17999999999999994 (the curse answers a real betrayal); condition; state_parameter cursed-by-wish.triggers 1 -> 2; mood 0.020000000000000018_

  第二轮投票是十七比十七。按章程，主席可以投第二票；Hedwig把自己的名字念了两遍，会议室里没有人笑。散会后，Emil在门口说下一次会带更多的人来，Hedwig说椅子够用。
  _effects: reputation -0.020000000000000004; mood -0.020000000000000018; relation rival with person-phase15-Emil-world-vienna-1912; flag +tie_broken_by_chair; knowledge “章程允许主席在平票时投第二票，但没有人记得上一次使用是什么时候。”_

- **1946.06** `vienna-medical-treatment.night-fever/hospital-night` tags: health, medical, family, money
  电车在环城大道上哐当响，Luise Kral 2攥着Hedwig的胳膊，怕人从座位上滑下去。急诊厅的煤气灯发黄，长椅上全是咳嗽声，押金窗口的铜盘递出两克朗，换来一张硬纸号牌。
  _effects: cash -2 (医院急诊押金（其中2克朗记入债务）); health 0.08 (急诊观察和冰敷退烧); relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-21_

- **1947.01** `vienna-life-injury-recovery/home-care` tags: health, work, family
  Hedwig没有去诊所，只用热水和旧布洗了伤口。第二天伤口周围发红，家里人把仅剩的干净床单撕成条。
  _effects: cash -2 (clean cloth（其中2克朗记入债务）)_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:012708_

- **1947.01** `df18-curse.name-in-the-door::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/name-and-date` tags: curse, name, door, omen, neighbor, dark, supernatural, vienna
  Hedwig在自家门框上看见一行新刻的字，是自己的名字，下面还有当天的日期。刀痕很浅，像是用钥匙划的。Josef Bauer说不是他，Hedwig也没有再问。那天夜里，Hedwig把门闩插了两遍，听见楼梯上有脚步声停了一下。
  _effects: knowledge “门框上出现了我的名字和当天的日期。”_

- **1947.01** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.shared_stove_fire/fire_no_damage` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Hedwig刚把锅端下灶，火苗就蹿了起来，Rosa一脚踩灭地上的油布，顺手抄起沙箱里的沙子盖住灶口。两人都没受伤，但Rosa的鞋底烧穿一个洞，那是唯一一双出门的鞋。
  _effects: mood -0.030000000000000027_

- **1947.01** `state_tick:suspected-witchcraft/rumor-season/whispers-fade` tags: suspected-witchcraft, witchcraft, rumor, religion, legal, reputation, fear, supernatural
  一年过去，Hedwig没有再点蜡烛，也没有再去那家后间。教堂的登记册翻到了新的一页，旧页边缘留着指甲划过的痕迹；街上的人开始讨论别的事。
  _effects: state_parameter suspected-witchcraft.evidence 20 -> 12; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1947.01** `p10-migration-tasks.move-district/move-three-loads-settled` tags: adulthood, migration, housing, family, travel, task, moving, district
  第二趟推到桥心，邻居扶了一把歪掉的柜子。孩子抱着书包跟在车后，问学校远不远。他把钥匙塞进衣兜，听见新房东在楼梯口说：季度租，少一个赫勒也不行。
  _effects: cash -9 (结清旧屋煤账、给新房东交部分季度租金与手推车租用费（其中9克朗记入债务）); residence; mood -0.020000000000000018; knowledge “新房子的房东只按季度收租，一次要交十克朗以上”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-116; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-8_

- **1947.06** `vienna-women-housework.wash-day/wash-day.quarrel` tags: gender, housework, home, labour, family
  铁桶磕在石沿上，水晃出来打湿了Hedwig的裙角。Elisabeth Bauer的声音拔高，说轮到她就是轮到她。Hedwig咬着嘴没再喊，把盆往自己门前拖，湿石板上一道水痕。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-elisabeth-bauer; reputation -0.020000000000000004_

- **1947.07** `p9-social-tenement-neighbors.gramophone-wall/gramophone-wall.cotton-in-ears` tags: housing, neighbors, tenement, daily, money, reputation
  Hedwig忍到天亮，第二天去药房买了五分钱的棉花，撕成两小团塞在耳里。夜里的进行曲还剩一点闷响，翻个身也就过去了。此后在楼梯间遇见Alois Lechner只是点头，再没有进过那家的门。
  _effects: cash -0.05 (在药房买棉花塞耳朵（其中0.05克朗记入债务）); relation acquaintance with person-alois-lechner_

  Hedwig把洗好的床单搭上院子里的公用晾衣绳，占了整条。Rosa抱着一盆湿衣服下来，说自家孩子明天要穿干净衬衫上学。两人在绳子两头扯了一阵，最后Hedwig把床单对折，腾出一半，Rosa用木夹子把衬衫一件件排上去。谁也没道歉，但第二天见面都点了头。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-phase15-Rosa-world-vienna-1912; mood 0.020000000000000018_

  Hedwig用三个月把账本上的零碎支出并成四类，又停掉两份没有人看的简报。年底结账时，Ferdinand Huber 2发现不仅没有赤字，还剩下够换一批档案柜的钱；没有剪彩，只有仓库里新柜子的木头味。
  _effects: organization_funds; reputation 0.04000000000000001; skill; mood 0.020000000000000018; flag +balanced_the_books; knowledge “停掉两份简报、合并零碎支出后，年底还剩一笔够换档案柜的钱。”_

  _effects: cash 6 (互助会补上本月房租差额); food; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-33; flag +parish_registered_

  Hermann Lechner 2每周三晚上来接Hedwig，两个人走四十分钟去教会的地下室。第十周，Hedwig第一次在会上开口，说自己昨晚路过酒馆没有停。散会时，家里人在门口等着，手里提着一壶热茶。Hedwig接过茶，手没有抖。
  _effects: cash -2 (戒酒会上的空椅子); health 0.1 (戒酒会上的空椅子); flag +sober_after_treatment; role; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-95_

  _effects: role; reputation 0.020000000000000004_

  军队征收站设在市场边上，Emil把一张征收单拍在Hedwig的车上，说要征用马和车，按市价给收据。Hedwig问收据什么时候能兑，Emil说“战后”。Rosa Huber在旁边牵着自己的驴，缰绳攥得发白。

- **1949.04** `vienna-market-debt.pawn-watch/pawn-chain-only` tags: market, debt, money, home, shop
  链子从你指缝里滑下去，落在绒布上几乎没有声音。掌柜说两克朗四十赫勒，你点了点头。出门时你把表掏出来上了几圈弦，它还在走，只是再没法挂在马甲上了。
  _effects: cash 2.4 (典当银表链所得); mood -0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person-alois-lechner_

  Hedwig在受伤后的第三天去了诊所。医生清洗伤口、换药，收下诊费，嘱咐不要急着回机器旁。Hedwig把药方折好，回家路上买了纱布。
  _effects: health 0.05 (wound treatment); cash -8 (clinic and bandages（其中8克朗记入债务）)_

  报纸在头版登了Hedwig和承包商在一张桌子上的照片，配文只有一句“谁签的字”。质询会开了三个小时，Hedwig把合同、比价单和验收记录一页页摊开；记者第二天没有再写，但把照片留在了档案里。

- **1950.01** `r13-work.unemployment-benefit::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement::postwar-benefit-office/sent-to-relief-work` tags: work, unemployment, benefit, institution, poverty, vienna, urban, postwar
  Maria Berg 2说补助金只发给有家属的人，Hedwig单身，只能去修路队领工钱。第二天Hedwig扛着铁锹站在城郊，和几十个男人一起把碎砖填进车辙。工钱按日结，比补助多两克朗，但一天下来，手掌磨出了三个水泡。
  _effects: cash 2 (补助金窗口的第二张表); health -0.04 (补助金窗口的第二张表); condition; flag +work13_sent_to_relief_work_

  婚礼在借来的房间里举行，证人是洗衣房的Anton Moser。戒指是从当铺买的，誓词写在洗衣单背面；Anton Moser念到“无论贫穷还是疾病”时，楼下有人敲门，三个人同时屏住呼吸。门开了，是来收煤气费的。
  _effects: state_add ld18-impossible-attachment state:world-vienna-1912:796; cash -1 (the pawnshop rings and the gas bill（其中1克朗记入债务）); mood 0.06000000000000005; flag +private_wedding_rite; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-108; flag +impossible_attachment_active_

- **1950.12** `vienna-legacy-worklife.union-office/union-office.write-off` tags: legacy, work, family, reform, ordinary_life
  账本的纸边已经发毛，Hedwig翻到那一页时，手指沾上一层灰。有人说“三十四克朗够买两袋面粉”，没人接话。最后举手表决，赞成销账的手比反对的多两只，老成员把手放下去时，椅子又晃了一下。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:004382:child; flag +union_debt_written_off_

  银幕上是城西的街口，镜头扫过一排晾衣绳。Hedwig认出绳下站着Rosa Hofer，也认出旁边那个被两名便衣夹住的人。散场灯亮时，Rosa Hofer还在笑，Hedwig却把围巾往上拉了拉。
  _effects: knowledge “新闻片里那个被便衣夹住的人，我认识。”; relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:005396:child; flag +recognized_neighbor_on_newsreel; mood -0.030000000000000027_

- **1951.11** `df18-dream.water-in-the-cellar::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/tell-the-landlord` tags: dream, omen, home, water, coincidence, eerie, everyday, vienna
  Hedwig把梦告诉房东。房东笑了一声，说老房子下水慢，做梦都带水汽。第二天，房东带人来查，真在墙里找到一条裂缝，顺手把地窖的租金减了一克朗。Hedwig把省下的钱买了一盏新灯，放在楼梯口。
  _effects: cash 1 (房东减掉的一克朗地窖租金); reputation 0.01999999999999999; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-96; knowledge “房东因为我的梦查了墙，减了地窖租金。”; flag +df18_landlord_fixed_crack; mood 0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-96_

  Anton Moser的母亲病重，家里要她回去嫁给一个开杂货铺的人。Anton Moser来告别时，Hedwig替她把箱子提到车站；两个人在月台上站到铃响，谁也没有说“等我”。火车开走后，Hedwig回到那两间房，发现隔壁的钥匙还插在门上。
  _effects: state_remove ld18-impossible-attachment state:world-vienna-1912:796; flag +left_for_a_marriage; flag -impossible_attachment_active_

  Hedwig把床上的旧褥子重新铺平，Franz Lechner放下自己的铺盖卷，从怀里掏出一把钥匙看了看。厨房里多了一口人的碗，灶边的煤省着烧也够。Grete Huber在门口看了一眼，没多话。
  _effects: cash 4 (出租空床收进的月租); household; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-126; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-126_

  天没亮，Hedwig就去天井的水龙头前排着，替Ferdinand Hofer 2占了一个位置。水来时两人把桶递来递去，Ferdinand Hofer 2把最后半桶倒进Hedwig的锅里，说自家还有雨水。桶底结着薄冰，两人用围裙垫着手提。
  _effects: relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-119; role; flag +water_rota; knowledge “天井的水龙头每天只开一小时，要提桶去排。”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-119_

- **1952.08** `vienna-women-housework.coal-stove/coal-stove.borrow-fire` tags: gender, housework, home, labour, family
  Hedwig端着一只旧锅去敲Emil Lechner 3的门，想借几块烧红的炭。Emil Lechner 3让她从自家炉膛里夹了一块，又抓了一小把引火的木片。Hedwig连声道谢，回来把炭放进炉栅，火是有了，屋里却还是冷得能呵出白气。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-150; food; mood 0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-150_

  _effects: state_add under-investigation state:world-vienna-1912:909_

- **1953.08** `vienna-market-debt.pawn-watch/keep-watch-borrow-elsewhere` tags: market, debt, money, home, shop
  你们站在当铺招牌下面，风从巷口灌进来。邻居说他婆娘这个月也要买煤，但两克朗还能匀出来。你把表给他看了一眼，说这表不能当，是爹留下的。他点点头，没再问。
  _effects: cash 2.3 (向邻居借来的现钱); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-126; mood -0.020000000000000018_

  _effects: food; skill; relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:001365:child; mood 0.020000000000000018_

  Helene Weiss 2带了两只空箱子来，走的时候还是空的。Hedwig把三年的合同、收据和会议记录按月份排在地上，Helene Weiss 2抽查了十二份，每一份都能对上签字人；最后一句是“下次可以少准备一点”。

  楼上住户投诉夜里有人拖椅子，管事的把巡查簿翻到Hedwig的那一页，说签字太潦草，看不出巡过几趟。Hedwig想辩解，却想起那晚确实在楼梯间多站了一刻钟；扣掉的工钱刚好够买一双不漏水的鞋。

### 359 subject events

- **1915.08** `birth` tags: birth
  Mitzi Weiss在Kral-Wohnung, Leopoldstadt出生。Luise Kral、Josef Weiss在场；家里现在有6口人。
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:000292_

- **1916.07** `vienna-infant-household.baptism-gift/spoon-kept` tags: infancy, health, family, home
  Luise Kral把银勺擦亮，收进床头木匣，和结婚戒指放在一起。邻居隔周来访，看见勺子在匣里，点了点头。Mitzi Weiss的粥还是稀的，但Luise Kral说这勺留给Mitzi Weiss长大用。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-hermann-kral; mood 0.04999999999999999; food_

- **1917.09** `vienna-infant-household.croup/steam-kettle` tags: infancy, health, family, home
  Josef Weiss把铜壶烧开，提起壶嘴对着床头旧毛巾罩出的布篷，让蒸汽灌进去。Mitzi Weiss的犬吠样咳嗽慢慢松下来，天亮时睡着。Josef Weiss用掉一整天份的煤，手背被蒸汽烫红一片。
  _effects: health 0.050000000000000044 (蒸汽缓解了喉部痉挛); cash -1 (多烧了一天的煤)_

- **1921.02** `statistical_infection`
  Mitzi Weiss在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang病倒了，肺炎让身体发热、虚弱，接下来的日子只能卧床。

- **1921.03** `statistical_illness_worsened`
  Mitzi Weiss的肺炎没有完全好转，咳嗽和发热又拖了一个月，家里人轮流守着。

- **1921.06** `vienna-early-childhood.stairwell-fall/landlord-complaint` tags: childhood, family, home, street
  摔伤之后两天，房东女人在门厅拦住Luise Kral，楼里的Alois Berg正提着菜篮站在门边。房东女人说楼梯间不许孩子嬉闹，铁扶手磕坏要赔。Luise Kral把裹着布条的Mitzi Weiss往身后一拉，回了句“灯都没有，谁看得见台阶”。房东女人把话记在房租本边上，说下回修灯的钱从房租里扣。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-1; mood -0.07000000000000006; flag +stairs_accident_noted; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-1_

- **1921.06** `statistical_illness_worsened`
  Mitzi Weiss的肺炎没有完全好转，咳嗽和发热又拖了一个月，家里人轮流守着。

- **1921.08** `statistical_recovery`
  Mitzi Weiss熬过了这场肺炎，能坐起来喝一点汤，但身体还很虚。

- **1922.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.laundry-help/laundry-three-heller` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, laundry, work
  “拽平，别留褶。”Alois Berg在绳子那头喊。Mitzi Weiss踮着脚把床单拉直，碱水把指缝泡得发白。收工时天已经黑了，Alois Berg数了三赫勒，又在围裙上擦干手才递过来。Mitzi Weiss把钱塞进袜筒，回家路上一直攥着，虎口的裂口沾了汗，火辣辣地疼。
  _effects: cash 0.03 (帮邻居晾床单挣的三赫勒); health -0.030000000000000027 (碱水泡手，虎口裂开); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-1; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-23_

- **1923.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.mind-sibling/sibling-shelter-missed-river` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, sibling, care
  巷口的喊声第二次传来时，Mitzi Weiss正用膝盖压住木盆边沿，给Anna Weiss挡穿堂风。Anna Weiss在旧布底下翻了个身，Mitzi Weiss立刻停住，等呼吸又匀了才敢动。Josef Weiss推门进来，先看见盆里睡着的Anna Weiss，再看见Mitzi Weiss冻红的脚背，把灶上留的汤热了热。
  _effects: mood -0.020000000000000004; relation kin with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000680:child; relation kin with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-8_

- **1923.05** `p9-childhood-household.water-and-coal/late-for-school` tags: childhood, family, household, home, siblings, illness, neighbors, injury
  水桶从一楼提到四楼，Mitzi Weiss在每层平台歇一次，桶底磕在台阶上，水溅出来，顺着板缝往下滴。煤球筐压在另一只手上，膝盖弯下去就发软。到Leopoldstädter Volksschule时校门已经关了半扇，老师把Mitzi Weiss的名字写在迟到簿上，让站在走廊尽头。
  _effects: health -0.040000000000000036 (提水抬煤累得手臂发酸); reputation -0.03; mood -0.04; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-19_

- **1923.12** `vienna-childhood-friends.hidden-book/book-confiscated` tags: childhood, social, school, street
  书页在桌肚里翻动时，Franz Lechner已经站在旁边。Mitzi Weiss的手还按在算术本上，书被抽走，书脊在讲台边磕了一声。Franz Lechner看了一眼折角，说“放学留下”，又拉开抽屉把书放进去。Mitzi Weiss盯着抽屉缝，整节课没听进去。
  _effects: mood -0.049999999999999996; relation acquaintance with person-franz-lechner_

- **1924.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.laundry-help/laundry-dragged-docked-pay` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, laundry, work
  “你看看这角。”Franz Lechner把床单拎起来，煤渣簌簌往下掉。Mitzi Weiss伸手去拍，反而把灰按进布纹里。Franz Lechner从工钱里数回两枚，只留一赫勒在Mitzi Weiss手心，让重洗那角。晚饭前Mitzi Weiss一直蹲在井台边，换了两盆水，指甲缝里全是黑泥。
  _effects: cash 0.01 (被扣后只剩一赫勒); mood -0.05; relation acquaintance with person-franz-lechner; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-30_

- **1924.04** `vienna-childhood-friends.courtyard-race/fell-on-wet-stones` tags: childhood, social, school, street, injury
  Mitzi Weiss想从床单底下钻过去，脚下一滑，膝盖磕在石板棱上。Berta Moser听见哭声出来，先看了看伤口，又回去端水。手帕扎得很紧，Mitzi Weiss试着站起来，被Berta Moser按回台阶上。
  _effects: health -0.040000000000000036 (膝盖在湿石板上磕破); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-10; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-10_

- **1925.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.mind-sibling/sibling-falls-at-well` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, sibling, care
  土豆滚进墙角的煤灰里，Mitzi Weiss弯腰去追，再抬头时Katharina Weiss已经坐在井台边，裤腿湿了一片，哭声一抽一抽。Alois Berg把孩子抱进灶间，Mitzi Weiss跟在后面，手指抠着门框。Josef Weiss回来没问经过，只把Mitzi Weiss的碗收走一半，说“看个人都看不住”。
  _effects: health -0.030000000000000027 (在井台边摔了一跤); mood -0.06000000000000001; relation kin with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-8_

- **1925.02** `r1-child.pencil-betrayal::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement::reform-police-school/denounced-to-police` tags: friendship, betrayal, theft, school, shame, reform, medical, schooling
  新校规说偷窃要记入品行册，还要通知警察。Josef Berg的父亲是巡警，当天就来了学校。Josef Berg在办公室里改口，说Mitzi Weiss逼Josef Berg偷的。Mitzi Weiss看着窗外的警帽，一句话也没说。
  _effects: reputation -0.14; flag +denounced_to_police_by_friend; relation rival with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000269:child_

- **1925.03** `r14-soc.childhood-oath::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/shared-last-bread` tags: childhood_friendship, childhood_oath, poverty, loyalty, neighbor, reform, medical, schooling
  面包房后门的泔水桶边，Anna Weiss捡起半块没沾灰的黑面包，掰成两半。Mitzi Weiss把大的那半推回去，说自己早上吃过；其实Mitzi Weiss的胃从昨夜就在叫。两人蹲在台阶上吃完，约好谁也不许把这件事说出去。多年后Mitzi Weiss仍记得，那半块面包边上有股煤灰味。
  _effects: cash -1 (砖墙根下的铜纽扣); mood 0.05; flag +shared_last_bread; relation friend with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000680:child_

- **1925.03** `lc17-prodigy.noticed::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/signs-the-card` tags: childhood, prodigy, talent, school, music, family, promise, reform
  老师在Mitzi Weiss的练习本背面画了五条线，说手指还要再长一年。第二天，Franz Lechner把一张写着“每周三次”的纸条交给Mitzi Weiss家里的大人，学费按季度付，先交一半。Mitzi Weiss把纸条折成小船，放在窗台上，直到墨迹被煤气灯熏黄。
  _effects: state_add lc17-child-prodigy state:world-vienna-1912:56; cash -1 (the first quarter of music lessons); relation acquaintance with person-franz-lechner; skill; flag +prodigy_lessons; mood 0.03; flag +child_prodigy_

- **1925.11** `kernel-social.first-meeting::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/first-meeting.coal-basket` tags: social, friendship, first_meeting, childhood, neighbor, everyday, reform, medical
  在合住的出租公寓的楼梯上，Mitzi Weiss抱着一筐煤，Anna Weiss从楼上下来，伸手托住筐底。两人把煤抬到四楼，在窗台上分了一块黑面包；此后上学、提水都结伴，谁家晚归就替谁留门。煤灰落在鞋面上，他们用同一块破布擦手。
  _effects: relation friend with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000680:child; role; flag +childhood_friend; knowledge “那个总在楼梯上帮忙的孩子住在同一栋楼的顶层。”_

- **1926.01** `lc17-prodigy.recital::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/clean-hands` tags: childhood, prodigy, performance, practice, family, shame, music, reform
  客厅里坐了十二个人，壁炉上的钟慢了一刻钟。Mitzi Weiss把曲子从头拉到尾，中间只错了一个音；Franz Lechner没有笑，只在客人走后把谱子翻到下一页。厨房里端来剩下的杏仁饼，Mitzi Weiss拿了一块，发现手心全是汗。
  _effects: state_parameter lc17-child-prodigy.promise 55 -> 61; state_parameter lc17-child-prodigy.pressure 35 -> 39; state_parameter lc17-child-prodigy.loveOfIt 70 -> 73; mood 0.05; reputation 0.03; skill; cash 1 (an envelope from the host); relation acquaintance with person-franz-lechner_

- **1926.01** `state_tick:lc17-child-prodigy/practice-year/keeps-practicing` tags: lc17-child-prodigy, childhood, promise, talent, pressure, family, education, prodigy
  Mitzi Weiss今年自己把练习时间从半小时加到三刻钟。琴谱的边角被翻得起毛，厨房里有人听见同一段反复了七遍，最后一遍终于没有错音；Mitzi Weiss停下来，才发现窗外天已经黑了，煤气灯在玻璃上照出一小块黄色的自己。
  _effects: state_parameter lc17-child-prodigy.promise 61 -> 64; state_parameter lc17-child-prodigy.loveOfIt 73 -> 77; state_parameter lc17-child-prodigy.pressure 39 -> 37; skill; mood 0.020000000000000004_
