  锁门的人在走廊里咳嗽了一声。Franz Lechner一边道歉一边收拾，最后从粉笔盒底下抽出一张旧练习纸，圈了四道题。Mitzi Weiss接过来，想问第二题怎么开头，Franz Lechner已经提着包下楼了。校门口还有太阳，Mitzi Weiss把纸摊在台阶上看了一会儿。
  _effects: skill; mood -0.03; relation acquaintance with person-franz-lechner_

- **1926.03** `r1-fam.childcare-burden::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/childcare.missed-school` tags: family, childcare, childhood, school, exhaustion, reform, medical, schooling
  Luise Kral天不亮就去洗衣房，Mitzi Weiss把最小的弟弟绑在背上，给另外三个煮面糊。锅开了，弟弟在背上哭，Mitzi Weiss一边晃一边数着还剩几根柴。学校点名册上，Mitzi Weiss的名字后面画了一排小圈；Berta Moser说再这样下去，老师要上门了。
  _effects: condition; flag +kept_siblings_out_of_school; role; knowledge “学校在点名册上用一个个小圈记旷课。”; education primary_

- **1926.04** `iw18-play.kite-on-roof::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/the-kite-is-lost` tags: childhood, play, kite, roof, neighbor, comic, reform, medical
  风把风筝往屋脊另一边推，竹骨卡在两片瓦之间。Mitzi Weiss和Otto Lechner仰头看到脖子发酸，最后只看见一截纸尾巴在烟里抖。Otto Lechner说再糊一只，Mitzi Weiss说纸不够了；两个人蹲在墙根，把剩下的线绕回木轴上。
  _effects: mood -0.03; relation friend with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000828:child; flag +kite_lost_on_roof_

- **1926.12** `r1-fam.naming-child::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement::naming-reform/naming.new-spelling` tags: family, childhood, naming, school, memory, reform, medical, schooling
  新来的登记员说，按市政的新规定，姓氏要按发音统一拼写。Josef Weiss把自己的姓念了三遍，登记员写成另一个样子；Luise Kral说这样孩子以后连祖坟都找不着。Josef Bauer把两张表格并排放着，说旧拼写还能用一年。
  _effects: flag +surname_spelling_changed; role; knowledge “旧拼写还能再用一年。”_

- **1927.01** `vienna-life-school-day/recites` tags: childhood, school, hunger, injury
  Franz Lechner让Mitzi Weiss站到黑板前背乘法表。孩子背到七乘八时停了一下，还是背完了；放学时手指冻得发红，石板上的字被袖子擦掉一半，Franz Lechner在名册上给了一个小小的勾，Mitzi Weiss把石板抱在胸前，一路没舍得放进书包。
  _effects: education primary; health -0.010000000000000009 (cold classroom)_

- **1927.01** `r13-edu.school-roll-call::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-school/attends-through-frost` tags: education, school, attendance, childhood, poverty, reform, medical, schooling
  Mitzi Weiss踩着结冰的台阶进教室，第三个空位还空着。Franz Lechner让Mitzi Weiss把昨天的课文背完，又用戒尺敲了敲黑板，叫全班跟着念。放学时名字被写在红榜末尾，Mitzi Weiss把冻僵的手指揣进袖口，觉得明天还能来。放学路上，Mitzi Weiss把冻裂的手贴在热煤渣上。
  _effects: mood 0.03; flag +school_attendance_streak; skill_

- **1927.05** `vienna-childhood-street.market-errand/errand-bullied` tags: childhood, street, social, home
  Mitzi Weiss被推到拱廊柱子边，围裙带子绕了两圈，系在铁环上。Mitzi Weiss一挣，肩膀撞上石柱，整条胳膊麻了一下。Katharina Berg提着菜篮过来，把篮子往地上一放，从怀里摸出削土豆的小刀，挑开绳结，没跟那两个孩子说一句话。
  _effects: relation friend with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000823:child; mood -0.05000000000000002; condition_

- **1927.12** `r14-soc.patron-child::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/borrowed-lessons` tags: cross_class_childhood, childhood_friendship, charity, shame, class, reform, medical, schooling
  Alois Weiss在厨房教Mitzi Weiss认了半页字母，条件是别让女主人看见。Maria Weiss把用剩的练习本塞过来，封面上还印着校徽。Mitzi Weiss拿回家，用橡皮擦了三遍，才敢在第一页写下自己的名字。那本练习本后来被翻得掉了封皮，第一页的名字还在，封皮上的校徽被擦得只剩一圈金边。
  _effects: mood 0.08; flag +borrowed_lessons; skill; relation friend with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:001194:child; knowledge “the alphabet came from a rich household cook”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-14_

- **1927.12** `vienna-school-childhood.fountain-pen/fountain-pen.broken` tags: school, social, childhood
  Josef Weiss把账单翻了个面，上面全是数字。Mitzi Weiss没再问，回家用线把旧笔尖绑在木杆上。第二天墨水从裂缝渗到指缝，Franz Lechner把本子推回来让重写。
  _effects: mood -0.05; relation kin with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-8; skill_

  _effects: state_parameter lc17-child-prodigy.pressure 37 -> 43; state_parameter lc17-child-prodigy.loveOfIt 77 -> 71; state_parameter lc17-child-prodigy.promise 64 -> 62; mood -0.04000000000000001_

- **1928.01** `r13-fam.help-homework::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-school/sums-finished` tags: task, family, school, homework, sibling, literacy, reform, medical
  Mitzi Weiss把煤油灯往桌子中间挪，让Anton Moser看清算术本上的格子。孩子把七加八数了三遍，Mitzi Weiss没有直接说答案，只把豆子一颗颗排在桌上。最后一页写完时，灯芯已经烧黑，Mitzi Weiss才发现自己的作业还空着半页。
  _effects: flag +helped_sibling_with_homework; role; skill; relation kin with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:001330:child; skill_

- **1928.03** `recipe-education.scholarship-exam::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-school/passed` tags: education, exam, class, poverty, ambition, reform, medical, schooling
  Mitzi Weiss用Rosa借的钢笔写完最后一题，笔尖在纸上划出一道细缝。放榜那天，Franz Lechner把Mitzi Weiss的名字念了两遍，Rosa站在人群后面，先鼓掌，再低头看自己的鞋。
  _effects: reputation 0.08; role; relation colleague with person-franz-lechner; education secondary_

- **1928.03** `r3-crime.juvenile-court::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-school::court-without-bars/admonished-and-sent-home` tags: crime, youth, court, family, school, reform, medical, schooling
  Maria Kral把案卷合上，问Mitzi Weiss知不知道错了。Mitzi Weiss的腿在长椅上晃着，够不着地，说“知道”。Luise Kral在旁边一直点头，Berta Weiss把一张写着“训诫”的纸递给她。出门时，Luise Kral把纸折好放进内衣口袋，怕人看见。
  _effects: flag +admonished_by_juvenile_court; role; relation guardian with person-luise-kral; knowledge “the juvenile court keeps a record even without a sentence”_

- **1928.09** `r13-turn.school-leaving::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement::reform-leaving-age/straight-to-factory` tags: education, school, work, family, transition, reform, medical, schooling
  Franz Lechner把Mitzi Weiss的名字从升学名单上划掉，说厂里正缺人手。家里没有人争，只把午饭里的半块面包塞给Mitzi Weiss。Mitzi Weiss进了纺织厂，工牌上写着“临时”，每天站十二个钟头，月底工钱比学徒多两克朗。
  _effects: cash 2 (毕业钟响过以后); health -0.06000000000000005 (毕业钟响过以后); condition; flag +left_school_for_factory; role_

- **1928.10** `vienna-adolescence.workshop-apprentice/apprenticeship-accepted` tags: adolescence, school, work, family, social
  作坊里全是松木味。Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne把一块方木夹进台钳，让Mitzi Weiss推刨子。Mitzi Weiss推了十分钟，木花卷到脚背上，刨面总算平了。Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne用拇指摸了摸，收下Mitzi Weiss，从钱盒里数出三克朗，说是头一周的饭钱。Josef Weiss在门口把围裙攥得很紧。
  _effects: cash 3 ({subject}学徒首周的膳食预付款); education apprenticeship; reputation 0.1; relation kin with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-8_

- **1929.01** `r13-edu.ask-teacher::ctx-vienna-university-1912::milieu-university/note-under-inkwell` tags: education, teacher, help, school, confidence, university, culture, schooling
  Mitzi Weiss等到天黑，Franz Lechner一直在改别的班的卷子，头也没抬。Mitzi Weiss把问题写在纸条上压在墨水瓶下，第二天发现纸条还在原处，只是背面多了一行字：“先看第三章。”Mitzi Weiss按纸条翻到第三章，发现那页夹着一张旧的辅助线图，图上的线画得很轻。
  _effects: skill_

- **1929.02** `r3-teen.vocational-school::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-school::reform-vocational-merge/machine-track` tags: education, vocational, class, craft, ambition, reform, medical, schooling
  分班牌上写着“金属加工”。Franz Lechner把Mitzi Weiss带到车床边，说先学量具，再碰刀具。Rosa在隔壁木工班敲钉子，隔着玻璃朝她比了个手势。Mitzi Weiss把校徽别在沾油的袖子上。
  _effects: flag +assigned_to_machine_track; role; skill; education secondary_

- **1929.04** `p9-childhood-adolescence.courtyard-gang/gang-corner-turn-back` tags: adolescence, friendship, identity, work, school, secret, church
  Mitzi Weiss只把Elisabeth Bauer送到巷口的煤油灯下就折回去。第二天在水泵边，Elisabeth Bauer学Mitzi Weiss走路的样子，几个人跟着笑，Mitzi Weiss拎起水桶没有接话。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-elisabeth-bauer; mood -0.020000000000000018_

- **1929.04** `r13-turn.school-leaving::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-school::reform-leaving-age/signed-apprenticeship` tags: education, school, work, family, transition, reform, medical, schooling
  毕业钟敲过三下，Franz Lechner把成绩单按在讲台上，说Mitzi Weiss还能再读一年，只要家里肯出四克朗。家里人在走廊里把学徒契约展开，纸角还带着面粉。Mitzi Weiss签了名，第二天就站到刨床前，手指上多了第一道木刺。
  _effects: cash -2 (毕业钟响过以后); flag +left_school_for_apprenticeship; role; education apprenticeship_

- **1929.09** `r3-teen.first-friend-group::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-school/split` tags: friendship, roof, belonging, betrayal, class, reform, medical, schooling
  团里有人偷了市场的香肠，被抓住后供出了所有人。Friedrich Winter把Mitzi Weiss拉到楼梯拐角，让她把责任推给最小的那个，说这样大家都能脱身。Mitzi Weiss没答应，只把口袋里的香肠纸揉成一团。第二天，屋顶上的折叠椅少了一把，Friedrich Winter见了她也不再吹那两声口哨。
  _effects: mood -0.09999999999999998; flag +friend_group_split; relation rival with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000135:child; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-17_

  Mitzi Weiss今年自己把练习时间从半小时加到三刻钟。琴谱的边角被翻得起毛，厨房里有人听见同一段反复了七遍，最后一遍终于没有错音；Mitzi Weiss停下来，才发现窗外天已经黑了，煤气灯在玻璃上照出一小块黄色的自己。
  _effects: state_parameter lc17-child-prodigy.promise 62 -> 65; state_parameter lc17-child-prodigy.loveOfIt 71 -> 75; state_parameter lc17-child-prodigy.pressure 43 -> 41; skill; mood 0.020000000000000018_

  补考那天，Mitzi Weiss在考场门口等到钟响，监考说名单上没有名字。Franz Lechner跑去教务处，回来时手里只有一张转学建议。Mitzi Weiss去了印刷厂当学徒，师傅说铅字比字母好认，至少不会骗人。
  _effects: condition; flag +left_school_after_exam_failure; relation colleague with person-franz-lechner; education apprenticeship_

- **1930.05** `r1-cul.fan-letter::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/quoted-without-permission` tags: culture, letters, fandom, literacy, ambition, reform, medical, schooling
  Mitzi Weiss的信寄出半年，没有回音。后来Grete Kral在一篇文章里引用了这封信，没有署名，只写一个读者说。Mitzi Weiss在报上读到自己的句子，先是高兴，随后发现文章里的意思和自己写的相反。
  _effects: mood -0.08000000000000002; reputation 0.03; flag +letter_quoted_without_permission; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-72; knowledge “the quoted sentence means the opposite of what was written”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-72; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-22_

- **1930.07** `r15-work.master-takes-on::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/unpaid-trial` tags: work, craft, apprentice, trade, poverty, reform, medical, schooling
  Friedrich Hofer先说试三个月，不给工钱，只管一顿午饭。家里同意了，因为多一张嘴比多一双手更难。Mitzi Weiss每天最早到，最晚走，三个月后师傅才把契约拿出来。
  _effects: food; mood -0.03999999999999998; state_add apprenticeship-contract state:world-vienna-1912:165; education apprenticeship_

- **1930.10** `yf18-firstlook.tram-glove::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/return-it-at-the-gate` tags: first_love, love, tram, glove, youth, courtship, reform, medical
  下一站，Mitzi Weiss追下车，把那只手套递还给Grete Weiss。Grete Weiss说了一声谢谢，手指在毛线手套里缩了一下；Mitzi Weiss回到车上才发现自己坐过了两站，只好沿着环城路走回家。
  _effects: state_add yf18-first-awakening state:world-vienna-1912:166; mood 0.02999999999999997; flag +yf18_first_awakening_active_

- **1931.01** `yf18-apprentice.press-guard::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-school/refuse-the-machine` tags: apprenticeship, work, danger, machine, injury, refusal, reform, medical
  教堂年历的订单催得紧，Magistrat der k. k. Reichshaupt- und Residenzstadt Wien把压印机的护板卸了，说这样进纸快一半。Mitzi Weiss看着那块铁板，说手会进去；Magistrat der k. k. Reichshaupt- und Residenzstadt Wien把工钱袋往桌上一扔，问Mitzi Weiss是不是不想出师了。Mitzi Weiss没有碰机器，那天下午的活由Josef Weiss接手。
  _effects: state_parameter apprenticeship-contract.progress 8 -> 3; state_parameter apprenticeship-contract.failures 0 -> 1; flag +yf18_refused_unguarded_press; mood 0.020000000000000018_

  Mitzi Weiss在教堂门口或学校走廊里又遇见Grete Weiss。这一次对方先点了头，Mitzi Weiss准备好的整段话全忘了，只把手里的书换到另一只手上；回家后Mitzi Weiss才发现，自己把书拿倒了。
  _effects: state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.intensity 55 -> 60; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.nerve 40 -> 48; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.reciprocated false -> true; mood 0.02999999999999997_

  _effects: cash -6 (职业夜校第一期学费); education apprenticeship; skill; relation acquaintance with person-franz-lechner_

- **1931.03** `vienna-adolescence.workshop-apprentice/apprenticeship-accepted` tags: adolescence, school, work, family, social
  Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne先看Mitzi Weiss的手，又让Mitzi Weiss推两下刨子试试。Mitzi Weiss用力过猛，刨刀啃进木头，Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne皱眉，让再来一遍。第二遍木花薄而匀。Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne说“留下”，当面点了三克朗给Josef Weiss，算头一周饭钱，又指了指墙上的钟，说迟到一刻钟扣半天。
  _effects: cash 3 ({subject}学徒首周的膳食预付款); education apprenticeship; reputation 0.09999999999999999; relation kin with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-8_

- **1931.04** `r2-leisure.ferris-wheel::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement::ferris-wheel-reform/tested-the-new-brake` tags: leisure, fairground, ferris_wheel, fear, youth, reform, medical, schooling
  游乐场新装了紧急制动，铁牌上写着“由市议会验讫”。摩天轮升到一半，Josef Lechner拉了一下闸，座舱猛地停住，又慢慢往下走。Mitzi Weiss问这是不是坏了，Josef Lechner说这是新规矩，每趟都要试一次。Anna Weiss在座位上把栗子撒了一地。
  _effects: mood -0.02999999999999997; condition; flag +tested_new_ferris_brake; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-2; knowledge “the municipal inspector requires a brake test on every ride”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-2_

  多瑙河上的铁桥在夜里只剩两排灯。Mitzi Weiss翻过栏杆，鞋底踩上结霜的钢梁，走了七步；Anna Weiss在桥头喊了一声，风把声音吹散。Mitzi Weiss翻回来时，Grete Weiss把一支烟塞进Mitzi Weiss嘴里，手抖得点不着火。

  Mitzi Weiss在考场上拉断了一根弦，换弦时又把琴马碰歪了。Franz Lechner在表格上写了“明年再试”，师傅那边则说这个月的工钱要扣，因为少干了三天。回家的路上，Mitzi Weiss把断弦绕在手指上，绕得很紧，直到指尖发白。
  _effects: state_parameter lc17-child-prodigy.promise 65 -> 55; state_parameter lc17-child-prodigy.pressure 41 -> 51; state_parameter lc17-child-prodigy.loveOfIt 75 -> 69; state_parameter apprenticeship-contract.progress 23 -> 18; cash -1 (exam fee and a new string); mood -0.07; flag +failed_conservatory_

  多瑙河的浴棚按男女分开，木板缝里能看见对面的人影。Mitzi Weiss穿着借来的毛线泳衣，站在第三间门口不敢下水；Anna Weiss从水里冒出来，头发贴在额头上，说水不冷。Mitzi Weiss走进河里时，满脑子都是自己肋骨上那道疤。
  _effects: state_add yf18-first-awakening state:world-vienna-1912:212; relation friend with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000680:child; mood 0.02999999999999997_

  _effects: state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.intensity 42 -> 47; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.nerve 15 -> 23; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.reciprocated false -> true; relation friend with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000680:child; mood 0.03_

- **1933.01** `r13-fam.help-homework::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/sums-finished` tags: task, family, school, homework, sibling, literacy, reform, medical
  Mitzi Weiss把煤油灯往桌子中间挪，让Maria Weiss看清算术本上的格子。孩子把七加八数了三遍，Mitzi Weiss没有直接说答案，只把豆子一颗颗排在桌上。最后一页写完时，灯芯已经烧黑，Mitzi Weiss才发现自己的作业还空着半页。
  _effects: skill; relation kin with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:001194:child; skill_

- **1933.05** `yf18-friend.inkwell::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/name-the-friend` tags: exam, cheating, friendship, betrayal, school, reform, medical, schooling
  Mitzi Weiss说Grete Weiss把答案抄在墨水瓶底，还答应分给自己一半。Franz Lechner把Grete Weiss的座位调到第一排；第二天，Mitzi Weiss的课桌里多了一张纸条，上面只有一行字：“你以后别走夜路。”Mitzi Weiss把纸条交给Franz Lechner，事情从此不再是两个人的事。
  _effects: relation_end friend with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000288:child; reputation 0.01999999999999999; flag +yf18_named_friend; mood -0.020000000000000018_

- **1933.09** `yf18-politics.latin-pamphlet::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/pass-it-on` tags: politics, school, pamphlet, censorship, awakening, reform, medical, schooling
  Franz Lechner把一本拉丁文课本推到Mitzi Weiss面前，说第三十四页的译文错了。Mitzi Weiss翻到那一页，夹在里面的是一张油印纸，写着面粉配给和工厂检查的数字。放学后，Mitzi Weiss把纸抄了一遍，塞进Anna Weiss的历史作业本；那天晚上，Mitzi Weiss第一次觉得自己住的街区有一张看不见的地图。
  _effects: knowledge “配给数字与官方公告并不一致”; flag +yf18_read_pamphlet; relation acquaintance with person-franz-lechner; mood 0.03_

- **1934.01** `major-family.proposal-refused/proposal-refused.bad-blood` tags: family, courtship, refusal, major, lifecycle
  Josef Berg 3的母亲把Mitzi Weiss带来的点心原封退回，说家里不娶有肺病亲戚的人。点心是早上排队买的，油纸还热着，Mitzi Weiss把它抱在胸前。Josef Berg 3站在门后没有出来，只让妹妹传话。Mitzi Weiss把戒指留在门房，走回Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang时天还没黑，楼下的狗一直叫。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-84; role; flag +marriage_refused; cash -2 (pastries and the returned ring); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-84_

- **1934.01** `vienna-family-courtship/ask-to-share-a-home` tags: family, courtship, household, social, lifecycle
  Mitzi Weiss在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang靠窗的桌边，把一只空咖啡杯转了半圈，说房租两人合付、桌子两人共用。Gustav Kral的手停在糖罐盖上，问了一句“要是过不下去呢”，没有当场答应，只说要想一想；杯里的咖啡凉了，话还悬在桌面上。
  _effects: interaction; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-74_

  北站的钟指到十一点四十，Mitzi Weiss把包袱塞进头顶的行李架，票根攥在手心。Josef Lechner在检票口翻看Mitzi Weiss的工票，问去格拉茨做什么；Mitzi Weiss说去看表姐，声音没有抖。汽笛响时，Mitzi Weiss看见站台上没有一张熟悉的脸，忽然笑了一下。
  _effects: state_remove yf18-runaway-plan state:world-vienna-1912:239; move place-wien; cash -4 (the fare and a platform coffee); flag +yf18_left_vienna; mood 0.04999999999999999; flag -yf18_runaway_plan_active_

  _effects: food; cash -0.08 (两个剩面包四赫勒一个); health -0.010000000000000009 (在风口里走了一段，脸被吹得发红)_

  _effects: state_parameter mb17-remittance-web.arrears 3 -> 5; mood -0.04000000000000001_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:007001_

- **1937.01** `vienna-life-injury-recovery/home-care` tags: health, work, family, injury
  Mitzi Weiss没有去诊所，只用热水和旧布洗了伤口。第二天伤口周围发红，家里人把仅剩的干净床单撕成条。
  _effects: health -0.030000000000000027 (untreated wound); cash -2 (clean cloth); condition_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:006683_

- **1937.01** `vienna-family-childbirth/son-born` tags: family, childbirth, death, lifecycle, birth, injury, state, state:raising-child
  Mitzi Weiss在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang生下一个男婴，取名Hermann Bauer。工友凑的钱抵了一部分，家里还是从糖罐里数出六克朗给接生的人；Josef把消息带到工厂，回来时把唯一的摇篮重新刷了一遍，Mitzi Weiss躺在床上出了一身虚汗。
  _effects: birth male; health -0.07999999999999996 (childbirth); cash -6 (midwife fee); state_add raising-child state:world-vienna-1912:365_

- **1937.01** `state_reaction:mb17-remittance-web/family-emergency` tags: mb17-remittance-web, migration, remittance, household, obligation, money, diaspora, state
  故乡来了一封电报，说屋顶塌了一角，老人暂时住到邻居家。Mitzi Weiss当晚就去邮局，把本来留着交学费的钱先汇了出去；回家后，他把墙上的缴费单取下来，折好放进抽屉。
  _effects: state_parameter mb17-remittance-web.obligation 50 -> 60; state_parameter mb17-remittance-web.arrears 5 -> 6; cash -3 (an emergency money order sent home); mood -0.04000000000000001_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:007234_

- **1937.01** `oj18-radio.repair::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-school/cold-joint` tags: radio, repair, craft, customer, work, odd_job, suspense, vienna
  Luise Kral的收音机只发出炒豆子一样的杂音。Mitzi Weiss把后板拆开，发现一根接地线被老鼠咬断，焊点冷得像一颗坏牙。修好以后，Luise Kral留Mitzi Weiss听完了整点新闻；出门时，Mitzi Weiss在巷口还听见那台机器在响。
  _effects: organization_found 屋顶无线电修理铺 (household_firm); position 无线电修理匠 @radio:person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000292:child-evt:world-vienna-1912:007237; state_add oj18-odd-apprenticeship state:world-vienna-1912:366; cash 2 (radio repair fee); skill; reputation 0.03; relation colleague with person-luise-kral; flag +radio_repairer; knowledge “收音机的接地线被老鼠咬断，焊点冷得像一颗坏牙。”; role; flag +odd_job_apprentice_

  _effects: birth female; health -0.07999999999999996 (childbirth); relation partner with person-phase15-Josef-world-vienna-1912; role; role; cash -6 (midwife fee); state_add raising-child state:world-vienna-1912:367_

  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 82 -> 90; state_parameter raising-child.caregiverBurden 37 -> 34; mood 0.01999999999999999_

  _effects: state_parameter mb17-newcomer.language 20 -> 21; state_parameter mb17-newcomer.localNetwork 13 -> 11; state_parameter mb17-newcomer.housingStrain 69 -> 73; cash -1 (the move and a new rent deposit); mood -0.03_

  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:009633_

- **1942.01** `state_reaction:ml18-sudden-fortune/a-relative-remembers` tags: ml18-sudden-fortune, money, windfall, sudden_fortune, household, luck, inheritance, lottery
  一个多年没走动的亲戚提着一盒点心上门，先问Mitzi Weiss的身体，再问那笔钱是怎么来的。临走时他说起小时候替Mitzi Weiss家垫过一冬天的煤钱，数字记不清了，但意思很清楚。
  _effects: state_parameter ml18-sudden-fortune.claims 2 -> 3; flag +windfall_claim_by_relative; mood -0.020000000000000018_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:009949_

- **1942.01** `ld18-absurd.moral-prize::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/the-medal-is-withdrawn` tags: absurd, irony, scandal, public, marriage, queer, secret_intimacy, vienna
  铜牌挂了一个月，有人写信告诉协会，说她们不是姐妹。Maria Berg 2把铜牌取回去，还在门口站了一会儿，问Mitzi Weiss为什么要骗人。Mitzi Weiss说没有人问过；Maria Berg 2张了张嘴，最后只说了一句“可惜”。
  _effects: state_add ld18-public-desire state:world-vienna-1912:541; reputation -0.08000000000000002; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-88; state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.pressure 15 -> 27; mood -0.06000000000000005; flag +public_desire_active_

  _effects: state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.pressure 27 -> 33; state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.discretion 55 -> 52; mood -0.020000000000000018_

- **1942.05** `p9-social-tenement-neighbors.stove-and-line/stove-and-line.sheet-quarrel` tags: housing, neighbors, tenement, daily, money, reputation
  Mitzi Weiss发现自己搭在栏杆上的床单被人挪到了绳尾，角上沾了煤灰，一时火起，在楼梯间拦住Hermann Weiss问是谁动的。Hermann Weiss说是Anna Weiss嫌滴水，两人越说越响，最后各自回屋摔上门，炉子空着，谁也没再去烧水。
  _effects: mood -0.050000000000000044; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-107_

- **1942.12** `state_reaction:mb17-remittance-web/border-closes` tags: mb17-remittance-web, migration, remittance, household, obligation, money, diaspora, state
  边境又收紧了，往故乡的列车要换乘三次。邮局说汇款还能走，但信可能压在路上；Mitzi Weiss还是把两张单子一起递进窗口，像把一句话交给一个不保证送达的人。
  _effects: state_parameter mb17-remittance-web.arrears 12 -> 12; mood -0.040000000000000036_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:010079_

- **1942.12** `r2-law.citizenship-papers::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/oath-administered` tags: law, citizenship, papers, exam, belonging, vienna, urban, crisis
  Gustav Winter问Mitzi Weiss国歌第二句是什么，又让她说出本区议员的名字。Mitzi Weiss答对了一半，Gustav Winter在表格上写“基本合格”，让下周来宣誓。Alois Lechner在门外等着，手里拿着一面小旗；Mitzi Weiss出来时，Gustav Winter又在后面补了一句：别迟到。
  _effects: cash -8 (入籍宣誓前的口试); flag +citizenship_oath_scheduled; role; relation friend with person-alois-lechner; knowledge “the oath ceremony is held every second Tuesday”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-12_

- **1943.01** `office16-favor.private-sinecure::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-university/audit-noticed` tags: office, favor, corruption, patronage, family, power, vienna, urban
  审计员在顾问的考勤卡上看见连续四十个“外勤”，而外勤地点写的是同一家咖啡馆。Mitzi Weiss把考勤卡收进抽屉，补了一张“社区联络”的说明；Otto Huber的工资没有停，但办公室门口多了一张审计通知。
  _effects: reputation -0.06; state_add office-under-siege state:world-vienna-1912:583; flag +sinecure_audited; knowledge “考勤卡上的四十次外勤都指向同一家咖啡馆。”; mood -0.050000000000000044; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-77; flag +office_under_siege_

- **1943.01** `vienna-legacy-worklife.sick-fund/sick-fund.advance-from-fund` tags: legacy, work, family, reform, ordinary_life
  药房在街口，柜台玻璃下面压着价目纸。Mitzi Weiss把两克朗推过去，伙计找回来十二赫勒，药片用报纸包成一个小包。工友接过去时手还在抖，报纸边角很快被汗洇软。
  _effects: cash -2 (从互助金垫付药费); health 0.05999999999999999 (及时买到药); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-17_

- **1943.01** `vienna-parish-aid.soup-kitchen/soup-kitchen.leave` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  Mitzi Weiss看见熟人排在前面，帽檐压得低低的，便转身拐进旁边的小巷。Friedrich Winter 2追出来问怎么不领了，Mitzi Weiss说人太多，改天再来。回到屋里，灶是冷的，早上剩的半个土豆还在盘子里。Mitzi Weiss把它切成片，就着水咽下去。
  _effects: mood -0.050000000000000044; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-48; food; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-48_

  一个陌生女人在电车上叫住Mitzi Weiss，说自己认识她的“那位”，还劝她趁年轻找个正经人家。Mitzi Weiss没有回答，提前一站下了车；走在回家的路上，她发现自己的步子越来越快，像在躲一个并不存在的人。
  _effects: state_parameter ld18-public-desire.rumorHeat 68 -> 76; state_parameter ld18-public-desire.shame 39 -> 43; reputation -0.03; mood -0.020000000000000018_

- **1944.01** `r13-edu.literacy-class::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/signs-own-name` tags: education, literacy, adult_education, class, belonging, vienna, urban, crisis
  Mitzi Weiss在石板上写自己的名字，写到第三遍才把最后一笔收住。Franz Lechner把Mitzi Weiss的手按在粉笔上，说慢慢来。放学时，Mitzi Weiss把石板抱在怀里，经过肉铺时第一次看懂招牌上写的是“今日有肝”。那天晚上，Mitzi Weiss在账本封皮上又写了一遍，写到最后一笔时手没有抖。
  _effects: mood 0.07999999999999996; flag +learned_to_sign_name; skill_

  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 16 -> 19; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1944.02** `r3-crime.vigilante-justice::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/untied-the-rope` tags: crime, vigilante, violence, village, law, vienna, urban, crisis
  Friedrich Hofer把绳子往木桩上绕第三圈时，Mitzi Weiss走过去解开了结。Josef Huber的脸已经肿了，嘴里塞着一只袜子。Franz Berg赶来时，人群还没有散。Friedrich Hofer说“你护着Josef Huber，明天丢的就是你家”，Mitzi Weiss把绳子扔在地上，说“那就丢”。
  _effects: condition; flag +opposed_vigilante_justice; relation rival with person-friedrich-hofer; knowledge “the suspect was tied up behind the mill without evidence”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-111_

- **1944.06** `oj18-mortuary.first-body::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/coffin-coop` tags: death, mortuary, funeral, work, odd_job, night, everyday, vienna
  师傅Otto Winter把一块冷毛巾塞进Mitzi Weiss手里，让Mitzi Weiss先擦死者的脸，再擦自己的。殡葬合作社的规矩写在门背后：先入殓，后结账；无名者也要有一块写着日期的木牌。Mitzi Weiss做完第一具，手抖得点不着烟。
  _effects: organization_found 圣马克斯殡葬合作社 (other); position 入殓工 @mortuary:person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000292:child-evt:world-vienna-1912:011484; state_add oj18-odd-apprenticeship state:world-vienna-1912:615; cash 1 (first mortuary wages); skill; flag +mortuary_worker; knowledge “殡葬合作社的规矩：先入殓，后结账；无名者也要有一块日期木牌。”; role; flag +odd_job_apprentice_

  故乡来了一封电报，说屋顶塌了一角，老人暂时住到邻居家。Mitzi Weiss当晚就去邮局，把本来留着交学费的钱先汇了出去；回家后，他把墙上的缴费单取下来，折好放进抽屉。
  _effects: state_parameter mb17-remittance-web.obligation 71 -> 81; state_parameter mb17-remittance-web.arrears 12 -> 12; cash -3 (an emergency money order sent home); mood -0.040000000000000036_

- **1944.10** `vienna-housing-move.rent-notice/rent-notice-demand-repair` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money
  Mitzi Weiss没有立刻答话，先去敲Karl Kraus的门。两人一起数落墙上的水渍和门框里灌进来的风，楼梯间的窗户修好了，自家墙皮还在剥落。Karl Kraus提议一起把话带给看门人：先修好漏水的墙角，涨租的事再谈。Mitzi Weiss把纸重新折好，请他把话带到门外。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-karl-kraus; mood 0.030000000000000027; flag +rent_dispute_

- **1945.01** `vienna-life-1945-liberation/survives` tags: war, liberation, 1945
  苏联士兵进城时，Mitzi Weiss躲在门后听见坦克履带压过石板路。几天后街口贴出告示，Mitzi Weiss去领了一份汤和一块面包。
  _effects: role; food; cash -2 (replacement documents)_

- **1945.01** `vienna-family-courtship/ask-to-share-a-home` tags: family, courtship, household, social, lifecycle
  Mitzi Weiss在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang靠窗的桌边，把一只空咖啡杯转了半圈，说房租两人合付、桌子两人共用。Hermann Moser的手停在糖罐盖上，问了一句“要是过不下去呢”，没有当场答应，只说要想一想；杯里的咖啡凉了，话还悬在桌面上。
  _effects: interaction_

- **1945.01** `office16-office.lose-the-chair::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-university/carried-out` tags: office, removal, loss, politics, power, transition, vienna, urban
  最后一次会议没有让Mitzi Weiss发言。保安把纸箱搬到门口，箱子里有半瓶墨水、一只坏掉的台灯和没写完的年度报告。Maria Berg 2跟在后面，手里拿着新的门锁；走廊尽头，秘书已经把名牌翻了过去。
  _effects: position_end 无线电修理匠 @radio:person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000292:child-evt:world-vienna-1912:007237; reputation -0.06; mood -0.07999999999999996; condition; flag +carried_out_of_office; knowledge “名牌在会议结束前就被翻了过去。”_

  印泥是赭红色的，像干了的面包瓤。Mitzi Weiss按完手印，工头把表举到窗边吹干，阳光里那张纸透出指印的轮廓。楼下第十区的街面传来运煤车的铁皮声，星期六下午突然显得很宽。
  _effects: cash -1 (星期六下午减少的计件工钱); mood 0.050000000000000044; flag +accepted_hours_reform_

- **1946.01** `interaction_response` tags: interaction, partnership, household
  Hermann Moser在几天后找到Mitzi Weiss，说可以试试。两人开始合付房租，也把各自欠的账摊在桌上。
  _effects: relation partner with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:002255:child; household; role; interaction_

- **1946.01** `vienna-family-childbirth/daughter-born` tags: family, childbirth, death, lifecycle, birth, injury, state, state:raising-child
  Mitzi Weiss在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang生下一个女婴，取名Elisabeth Moser。接生的人收六克朗，Hermann Moser在走廊烧水，邻居借来一只摇篮；Mitzi Weiss起身时眼前发黑，三天后腰还像断了一样，孩子哭声响亮，把屋里的冷气都顶了回去。
  _effects: birth female; health -0.07999999999999996 (childbirth); cash -6 (midwife fee); state_add raising-child state:world-vienna-1912:662_

- **1946.01** `state_reaction:oj18-odd-apprenticeship/the-thing-in-the-basement` tags: oj18-odd-apprenticeship, work, odd_job, apprenticeship, night, trust, secrecy, state
  夜班结束前，Mitzi Weiss听见地下室传来拖拽声。下去时，门开着，地上只有一道湿拖把的痕迹；第二天，所有人都说那间屋子锁了十年。
  _effects: state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.strangeKnowledge 30 -> 38; state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.trust 30 -> 27; mood -0.030000000000000027; knowledge “地下室那扇门后面有东西被搬动过，但巡查簿上没有记录。”_

- **1946.01** `ld18-journalist.interview::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-university/traded-the-name` tags: press, interview, public_desire, scandal, money, queer, secret_intimacy, vienna
  Maria Berg说，只要Mitzi Weiss肯把Grete Weiss的名字说出来，就可以把另一桩旧案从版面上撤掉。Mitzi Weiss看着窗外的电车，问旧案里有没有孩子；Maria Berg说有。Mitzi Weiss把茶喝完，说那你们登吧。
  _effects: state_parameter ld18-public-desire.protection 0 -> 10; state_parameter ld18-public-desire.shame 47 -> 57; relation friend with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000288:child; cash 1 (the journalist's retainer); mood -0.030000000000000027; flag +traded_the_name_

- **1946.01** `state_reaction:ld18-impossible-attachment/a-question-at-the-office` tags: ld18-impossible-attachment, love, intimacy, queer, unregistered, commitment, secret, desire
  办公室有人问Mitzi Weiss，为什么总有一个女人在楼下等她。Mitzi Weiss说那是表妹；对方笑了一下，说现在的表妹真体贴。下午，Mitzi Weiss把桌上的相框扣倒，又在没人时翻回来，用袖子擦了擦玻璃上的灰。
  _effects: state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.pressure 42 -> 50; state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.discretion 46 -> 42; reputation -0.019999999999999997_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:012736_

- **1946.01** `state_reaction:ld18-public-desire/a-moral-society-letter` tags: ld18-public-desire, desire, public, scandal, reputation, body, rumor, notoriety
  道德协会寄来一封措辞客气的信，请Mitzi Weiss去“谈谈自己的生活方式”。她把信读完，折好，垫在桌脚下；桌子还是晃。第二天，她照常去上班，只是把头发剪短了一截，短到所有人都能看出她不在乎。
  _effects: state_parameter ld18-public-desire.protection 10 -> 5; state_parameter ld18-public-desire.defiance 30 -> 38; mood -0.010000000000000009_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:012736_

- **1946.01** `state_reaction:ld18-impossible-attachment/a-family-wedding` tags: ld18-impossible-attachment, love, intimacy, queer, unregistered, commitment, secret, desire
  亲戚的婚礼上，Mitzi Weiss被安排和{link:beloved}分开坐。司仪问有没有人反对，Mitzi Weiss低头喝汤；{link:beloved}在另一桌替别人倒酒，手很稳，酒没有洒出来。散席时，两个人在衣帽间碰见，只说了一句“外面下雨”。
  _effects: state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.pressure 50 -> 60; state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.grief 0 -> 4; mood -0.020000000000000018_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:012742_

- **1946.01** `state_reaction:ld18-public-desire/a-wedding-invitation` tags: ld18-public-desire, desire, public, scandal, reputation, body, rumor, notoriety
  请柬上写着Mitzi Weiss的名字，旁边用铅笔注了一句“一个人来也可以”。婚礼上，新娘把手捧花直接递给她，笑着说“下一个该你了”；同桌的人都在等Mitzi Weiss怎么回答。她把花放在膝上，说这花很香。
  _effects: state_parameter ld18-public-desire.spectacle 50 -> 58; state_parameter ld18-public-desire.shame 57 -> 62; mood -0.020000000000000018_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:012783_

- **1950.01** `r14-soc.borrow-from-usurer::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/eighteen-kronen-at-twenty-percent` tags: task, criminal_debt, debt, money, fear, vienna, urban, postwar
  Maria Moser在钟表铺后间放债，桌上摆着一把算盘和一杯冷茶。Mitzi Weiss借十八克朗，周息两成，押的是下个月的工钱。Maria Moser让Mitzi Weiss把名字写在借据上，又让在名字旁边按一个指印。“按重一点，”Maria Moser说，“省得以后不认”。借据被Mitzi Weiss折成小方块，塞进内袋，走路时一直硌着胸口。
  _effects: cash 18 (钟表铺后间的算盘); mood -0.050000000000000044; flag +criminal_debt; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-38; knowledge “the usurer charges twenty percent a week”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-38_

  上诉期的最后一天，Mitzi Weiss把申诉书交到区公所。Helene Bauer 2看了一眼钟，说邮戳是昨天的，算数。驱逐令暂缓，等上面复核。Gustav Winter在门口等着，听见“暂缓”两个字，把已经掏出来的手铐又放回口袋。
  _effects: mood 0.040000000000000036; condition; flag +appeal_filed_on_last_day; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-12; knowledge “a postmark from the day before keeps the appeal alive”_

- **1953.01** `df18-luck.repayment-comes-due::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-university/give-a-name` tags: luck, debt, obligation, work, betrayal, dark, vienna, urban
  Rosa Berg要Mitzi Weiss给一个名字，说只要有人愿意在夜里替Mitzi Weiss送一只箱子，账就减一半。Mitzi Weiss想到了常来送煤的Maria Berg 2。箱子送出去以后，Maria Berg 2在码头上被巡警拦下，Mitzi Weiss再见到Maria Berg 2时，两个人都没有提那只箱子。
  _effects: state_parameter df18-borrowed-luck.debt 35 -> 25; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-88; reputation -0.01999999999999999; knowledge “我把一个名字交给了债主，那个人后来被巡警拦下。”; flag +df18_name_given_to_lender; mood -0.020000000000000018_

- **1953.01** `state_tick:ld18-impossible-attachment/invented-anniversary/the-date-that-is-not-on-paper` tags: ld18-impossible-attachment, love, intimacy, queer, unregistered, commitment, secret, desire
  Mitzi Weiss和{link:beloved}把认识的那一天当作纪念日。没有登记，没有证人，只有一块从旧货摊买来的蛋糕和两杯兑了水的酒；Mitzi Weiss在日历上画了一个别人看不懂的记号，第二年又画在同一个格子里。
  _effects: state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.devotion 86 -> 90; state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.pressure 85 -> 82; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1953.01** `state_tick:df18-borrowed-luck/luck-account/price-arrives` tags: df18-borrowed-luck, luck, debt, obligation, superstition, risk, bargain, transfer
  第六个月，债主在楼梯口拦住Mitzi Weiss，说利息不能总按原来的算法。Mitzi Weiss看着对方手里的账本，忽然发现那上面的墨迹比上次新，数字也比上次多。
  _effects: cash -2 (利息被重新计算); state_parameter df18-borrowed-luck.debt 25 -> 31; state_parameter df18-borrowed-luck.luck 25 -> 20; mood -0.030000000000000027_

- **1953.03** `office16-office.lose-the-chair::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-university/reappointed` tags: office, removal, loss, politics, power, transition, vienna, urban
  计票拖到凌晨，Mitzi Weiss以两票之差留任。Ferdinand Huber 2把已经写好的交接清单折起来，笑着说下次再来。Mitzi Weiss回到办公室，发现桌上的文件被人重新排过；最上面那份，是新一年的预算草案。
  _effects: reputation 0.06; mood 0.06000000000000005; organization_funds; state_add office-patronage state:world-vienna-1912:911; flag +reappointed; knowledge “桌上的文件被人重新排过，最上面是新一年的预算草案。”; flag +office_patronage_network_

- **1953.05** `r3-crime.false-accusation::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement::postwar-housing/maid-confesses` tags: crime, court, servant, class, injustice, vienna, urban, postwar
  就在法官要敲槌时，Helene Huber忽然站起来，说糖罐是她打碎的，碎片埋在院子里的李树下。市立太平间的脸涨得通红，Maria Berg 2被叫去后院挖了半个钟头。Mitzi Weiss被当庭释放，门外下着雨，Helene Huber追出来，把一个纸包塞进Mitzi Weiss手里，里面是三个月的工钱。
  _effects: reputation 0.05000000000000002; flag +cleared_by_maid_confession; relation friend with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:004997:child; knowledge “the sugar bowl was broken and buried in the yard”_

- **1953.12** `p9-social-tenement-neighbors.stove-and-line/stove-and-line.first-come-order` tags: housing, neighbors, tenement, daily, money, reputation
  Mitzi Weiss把四只洗衣盆按到厨房的先后排成一列，用粉笔在门后写下各家的时辰：六点到七点半、七点半到九点。Elisabeth Bauer起初嘟囔，看到自家名字排在第二格，也就把水壶提了回去。晾衣绳按同样次序分两段，谁家的床单先上谁先占。
  _effects: mood 0.040000000000000036; relation acquaintance with person-elisabeth-bauer_

- **1954.01** `bc18-office.promotion-by-typo::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-university/accept-the-post` tags: office, promotion, bureaucracy, mistake, status, absurd, politics, vienna
  晋升名单上，Hermann Lechner 2把“助理文书”误排成了“主任”。文件一路盖到市长办公室，竟没有人退回来；Mitzi Weiss在星期一搬进了有地毯的办公室，领到每月六克朗的主任薪金。Josef在走廊里看了Mitzi Weiss一眼，没有问Mitzi Weiss会不会看预算，只问要不要配一名速记员。
  _effects: cash 6 (主任薪金); state_add bc18-ridiculous-fame state:world-vienna-1912:958; role; relation acquaintance with person-phase15-Josef-world-vienna-1912; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-95; flag +promoted_by_a_typo; mood 0.030000000000000027; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-95; flag +ridiculously_famous; reputation 0.020000000000000018; mood 0.020000000000000018_

  户籍窗口换了一张新表格，配偶一栏不能空着。Mitzi Weiss最后写了“同住人”，办事员抬头看了一眼，说这不作数。回家后，Mitzi Weiss把表格折成很小的一块，塞进针线盒；{link:beloved}问今天顺不顺利，Mitzi Weiss说和平时一样。
  _effects: state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.pressure 82 -> 88; state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.discretion 30 -> 27; mood -0.020000000000000018_

  Mitzi Weiss把今年的任命名单又看了一遍。三个名字是自己写的，两个是别人硬塞的；他们都记得是谁让他们进来的。办公室没有多一把椅子，但走廊里打招呼的人多了。

- **1954.05** `vienna-social-movement.meeting/speak-and-organize` tags: development, social_movement, organization, meeting
  Mitzi Weiss在后屋的会议上报出自己街区的名字：谁家缺煤，谁被房东催租，谁知道哪间印刷厂还在招人。环城大道写字楼管理处把这些事记进一本旧账本，散会时每个人带走一叠更小的纸条。
  _effects: skill; reputation 0.020000000000000018_

- **1954.10** `vienna-housing-move.rent-notice/rent-notice-neighbors-together` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money
  Mitzi Weiss把Anna Weiss 4和楼里另两户人家叫到楼道，把那张纸摆在窗台上传看。看门人在楼梯拐角听着，说房东礼拜三来收钱。几家人合计好，到那天一起等在门厅，先把克朗递过去，再把修补的事一件件摆出来。Mitzi Weiss把纸折进围裙口袋，觉得人凑齐了话也硬气些。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-108; mood 0.040000000000000036; flag +tenant_collective_

- **1955.01** `statistical_death/violence` tags: death
  Mitzi Weiss在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang卷入一场冲突，头部和胸口受了重伤。旁人把人抬回家，夜里伤势恶化。Luise Weiss在场。
  _effects: death (暴力); flag -impossible_attachment_active; flag +df18_luck_settled; flag -office_patronage_network; flag -ridiculously_famous_
